### Task 4: Договор исполнителя и адаптер ComfyUI (§8.1, §8.2, §12.2)

**Files:**
- Create: `h3_48gb/studio/executors/base.py`, `h3_48gb/studio/executors/comfy.py`
- Modify: `requirements-studio.txt` (`websockets==15.0.1`)
- Test: `tests/test_studio_executor_contract.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ComfyApi` (задача 2), `templates` (задача 3), `DispatcherClient` (`engines/dispatcher_client.py`).
- Produces: `base.Capabilities`, `Probe`, `EngineStatus` (TypedDict §8.1), `TaskSpec(task_id, template, template_sha256: str | None = None, values: dict, images: dict[str, list[Path]], dest_dir)` (Pydantic, frozen; ред. 2: версия шаблона, с которой задача спланирована, — иная версия → `ExecutorError` «шаблон … изменился после постановки», ничего не исполняется), `ExecutorError`, `SubmitUncertain(ExecutorError)`, `Executor` (Protocol §8.1). `ComfyExecutor(*, url, output_dir, scope, dispatcher=None, engine="comfy-studio", api=None, ws_connect=None)`: `name="comfy"`, `capabilities={"find": "exact", "restart_evidence": True, "progress": True, "cancel": "hard", "async_submit": True}`, методы договора; `submit` загружает картинки как `<task_id>-<n>.<ext>`, шлёт `client_id = extra_data.studio_task_id = task_id`, ловит прогресс по WS (`ws://…/ws?clientId=<task_id>`, `proxy=None`); `find` → `prompt_id` из `/queue`/`/history` или `file:<task_id>` по файлам, `None` — только когда задачи доказуемо нигде нет; движок не ответил и файлов нет → `ExecutorError` («не удалось проверить» ≠ «не найдено», ред. 2); `status` — из `/history`, затем `/queue`, затем повторно `/history`, иначе `lost`; `result(ref, dest)` копирует файлы в `dest`; `engine_version() -> str | None` — `system.comfyui_version` из `/system_stats` (ред. 3).
- Live: `STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY=<url> STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY_OUTPUT=<dir> [STUDIO_LIVE_DISPATCHER=<url>] STUDIO_LIVE_TEMPLATES=krea2-t2i[,h3-fl2va,h3-ref2va]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты**

Create `tests/test_studio_executor_contract.py`:

```python
"""The executor contract (studio spec §8.1, §12.2) for the ComfyUI adapter: every declared
capability, on the fake always and on a live ComfyUI when STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY is set.

Live run (alex-neuro, the studio's ComfyUI up under the dispatcher, run where its output folder is
readable): STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY=http://127.0.0.1:8189 STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY_OUTPUT=/home/alex/Outputs/comfy-h3
STUDIO_LIVE_DISPATCHER=http://127.0.0.1:8790 STUDIO_LIVE_TEMPLATES=krea2-t2i[,h3-fl2va,h3-ref2va].

Flat in tests/ on purpose (not tests/contract/): tests/ has no __init__.py and pytest imports in
`prepend` mode, so a subfolder would not see the fakes next to it."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import io
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from PIL import Image

from _fake_comfy_studio import FakeComfyStudio, read_comment
from _fake_dispatcher import FakeDispatcher
from h3_48gb.engines.comfy_api import ComfyApi
from h3_48gb.engines.dispatcher_client import DispatcherClient
from h3_48gb.studio import ids, templates
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import ExecutorError, TaskSpec
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.comfy import ComfyExecutor

LIVE = os.environ.get("STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY")
LIVE_TEMPLATES = [t for t in os.environ.get("STUDIO_LIVE_TEMPLATES", "krea2-t2i").split(",") if t]


KREA_PROMPT = "A woman in a linen dress on a forest trail, morning."
H3_PROMPT = "She walks toward the camera and smiles."


def _png(path: Path, rgb: tuple[int, int, int], size=(896, 512)) -> Path:
    Image.new("RGB", size, rgb).save(path)
    return path


def _spec(tmp_path: Path, template: str, *, seed: int = 11, steps: int | None = None,
          task_id: str | None = None) -> TaskSpec:
    task_id = task_id or ids.new_id("k")
    if template == "krea2-t2i":
        values = {"prompt": KREA_PROMPT, "seed": seed,
                  "steps": steps or 8, "width": 1344, "height": 768}
        images = {}
    else:
        values = {"prompt": H3_PROMPT, "seed": seed,
                  "steps": steps or 2, "width": 896, "height": 512, "length": 73}
        if template == "h3-fl2va":
            images = {"first_frame": [_png(tmp_path / f"{task_id}-kf.png", (90, 120, 60))]}
        else:
            images = {"ref_images": [_png(tmp_path / f"{task_id}-r1.png", (200, 40, 40)),
                                     _png(tmp_path / f"{task_id}-r2.png", (40, 40, 200))]}
    return TaskSpec(task_id=task_id, template=template, values=values, images=images,
                    dest_dir=tmp_path / "dest" / task_id)


def _assert_graph(graph: dict, template: str, task_id: str, seed: int, steps: int,
                  names: list[str]) -> None:
    """§12.2: what ComfyUI actually got — values AND links — against literals of this test,
    never against `build_payload` (the code under test, Codex r2 blocker). Shared by the fake
    contract and the live gate."""
    if template == "krea2-t2i":
        assert (graph["4"]["inputs"]["text"], graph["7"]["inputs"]["seed"],
                graph["7"]["inputs"]["steps"], graph["6"]["inputs"]["width"],
                graph["6"]["inputs"]["height"], graph["9"]["inputs"]["filename_prefix"],
                graph["7"]["inputs"]["positive"], graph["7"]["inputs"]["latent_image"],
                graph["8"]["inputs"]["samples"], graph["9"]["inputs"]["images"]) == (
            KREA_PROMPT, seed, steps, 1344, 768, f"studio/{task_id}/f",
            ["4", 0], ["6", 0], ["7", 0], ["8", 0])
        assert names == []
        return
    h3 = graph["6"]["inputs"]
    assert (h3["prompt"], graph["10"]["inputs"]["noise_seed"], graph["7"]["inputs"]["steps"],
            h3["length"], h3["width"], h3["height"], graph["15"]["inputs"]["filename_prefix"],
            graph["11"]["inputs"]["latent_image"], graph["8"]["inputs"]["conditioning"],
            graph["14"]["inputs"]["images"], graph["15"]["inputs"]["video"]) == (
        H3_PROMPT, seed, steps, 73, 896, 512, f"studio/{task_id}/f",
        ["6", 1], ["6", 0], ["12", 0], ["14", 0])
    # the links, not just the nodes: which picture feeds which input, in order (Codex-8)
    if template == "h3-fl2va":
        assert (h3["first_frame"], graph["5"]["inputs"]["image"], len(names)) == (
            ["5", 0], names[0], 1)
    else:
        assert [h3[f"ref_images.ref_image_{i}"] for i in range(len(names))] == \
            [[f"5_{i}", 0] for i in range(len(names))]
        assert [graph[f"5_{i}"]["inputs"]["image"] for i in range(len(names))] == names
        assert sorted(k for k in h3 if k.startswith("ref_images.")) == \
            [f"ref_images.ref_image_{i}" for i in range(len(names))]


def _wait(executor, ref, *, timeout=900.0, poll=0.05):
    seen, deadline = [], time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        status = executor.status(ref)
        if status["progress"] is not None:
            seen.append(status["progress"])
        if status["state"] in ("done", "failed", "cancelled", "lost"):
            return status, seen
        time.sleep(poll)
    raise AssertionError(f"{ref} did not finish in {timeout} s")


@pytest.fixture
def fake(tmp_path):
    comfy = FakeComfyStudio(tmp_path / "comfy-out")
    yield comfy
    comfy.close()


def _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path, dispatcher=None):
    return ComfyExecutor(url=fake.url, output_dir=fake.output_dir, scope="local-mac",
                         dispatcher=dispatcher)


# -- declared capabilities ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_the_adapter_declares_what_spec_8_2_says():
    assert ComfyExecutor.capabilities == {"find": "exact", "restart_evidence": True,
                                          "progress": True, "cancel": "hard",
                                          "async_submit": True}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("template", ["krea2-t2i", "h3-fl2va", "h3-ref2va"])
def test_submit_status_result_and_the_actual_payload(fake, tmp_path, template):
    """§12.2: the graph ComfyUI got holds our prompt, seed, steps, length and the uploaded file
    names; the uploaded bytes are the input files; the result is the engine's file."""
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, template, seed=4242)
    assert executor.probe(spec) == {"alive": True, "ready": True, "detail": "готов"}
    ref = executor.submit(spec)
    status, progress = _wait(executor, ref)
    assert status == {"state": "done", "progress": status["progress"], "error": None}
    steps = spec.values["steps"]
    assert progress == sorted(progress) and progress[-1] == (steps, steps)   # monotone, to the end
    [job] = fake.prompts
    assert (job["prompt_id"], job["client_id"], job["extra_data"]) == (
        ref, spec.task_id, {"client_id": spec.task_id, "studio_task_id": spec.task_id})
    graph = job["prompt"]
    files = [p for paths in spec.images.values() for p in paths]
    names = [f"{spec.task_id}-{n}.png" for n in range(1, len(files) + 1)]
    assert {n: hashlib.sha256(fake.uploads[n]).hexdigest() for n in names} == \
        {n: hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for n, p in zip(names, files)}
    assert fake.upload_fields == {n: {"type": "input", "overwrite": "true"} for n in names}
    _assert_graph(graph, template, spec.task_id, 4242, 8 if template == "krea2-t2i" else 2,
                  names)
    [out] = executor.result(ref, spec.dest_dir)
    expected = "f_00001_.png" if template == "krea2-t2i" else "f_00001_.mp4"
    assert out == spec.dest_dir / expected
    assert out.read_bytes() == (fake.output_dir / "studio" / spec.task_id / expected).read_bytes()
    if template != "krea2-t2i":
        assert read_comment(out)["pictures"] == [
            {"name": n, "sha256": hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
            for n, p in zip(names, files)]


def test_find_locates_a_task_whose_prompt_id_was_lost(fake, tmp_path):
    """The client died right after POST /prompt (GPU-DAY-21 п.6): find() by extra_data in the
    queue, then in the history, then — after a restart — by the files."""
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    fake.hold = True
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va")
    ref = executor.submit(spec)
    assert executor.find(spec) == ref                     # in /queue (running)
    other = _spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va")
    assert executor.find(other) is None
    fake.release()
    _wait(executor, ref)
    assert executor.find(spec) == ref                     # in /history
    fake.restart()
    assert executor.status(ref)["state"] == "lost"
    assert executor.find(spec) == f"file:{spec.task_id}"  # history gone, files stay
    assert executor.status(f"file:{spec.task_id}")["state"] == "done"
    assert [p.name for p in executor.result(f"file:{spec.task_id}", spec.dest_dir)] == \
        ["f_00001_.mp4"]


def test_a_task_killed_by_a_restart_is_not_found(fake, tmp_path):
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    fake.hold = True
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va")
    ref = executor.submit(spec)
    fake.restart()
    assert (executor.status(ref)["state"], executor.find(spec)) == ("lost", None)


def test_restarted_since_reads_the_dispatchers_started_at(tmp_path, fake):
    dispatcher = FakeDispatcher(own={"comfy-studio": {"pid": 1, "owner": "studio-worker",
                                                      "started_at": 1000.0, "ready": True}})
    try:
        executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path,
                                  DispatcherClient(dispatcher.url, client="studio-worker"))
        assert (executor.restarted_since(999.0), executor.restarted_since(1000.0),
                executor.restarted_since(1001.0)) == (True, False, False)
        dispatcher.own = {}
        assert executor.restarted_since(1001.0) is True          # no live record: queue lost
    finally:
        dispatcher.close()
    assert _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path).restarted_since(0.0) is None   # no dispatcher: unknown
    gone = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path, DispatcherClient("http://127.0.0.1:9", timeout=0.5))
    assert gone.restarted_since(0.0) is None


def test_hard_cancel_of_a_running_task_stops_it_and_frees_the_queue(fake, tmp_path):
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    fake.hold = True
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va")
    ref = executor.submit(spec)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
    while executor.status(ref)["state"] != "running" and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        time.sleep(0.01)
    executor.cancel(ref)
    status, _ = _wait(executor, ref)
    assert (status["state"], fake.interrupts) == ("cancelled", 1)
    assert ComfyApi(fake.url).queue() == {"queue_running": [], "queue_pending": []}
    assert not (fake.output_dir / "studio" / spec.task_id).exists()


def test_cancel_of_a_waiting_task_deletes_it_from_the_queue(fake, tmp_path):
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    fake.hold = True
    first, second = _spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va"), _spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va")
    executor.submit(first)
    ref = executor.submit(second)
    executor.cancel(ref)
    assert (fake.deleted, fake.interrupts) == ([ref], 0)
    fake.release()
    fake.wait_idle()
    assert executor.status(ref)["state"] == "lost"


def test_probe_names_what_is_missing(fake, tmp_path):
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, "h3-ref2va")
    fake.classes.remove("MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo")
    assert executor.probe(spec) == {"alive": True, "ready": False,
                                    "detail": "в ComfyUI нет узлов: MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo"}
    fake.classes.append("MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo")
    fake.models["diffusion_models"].remove("minimax_h3_ref2va_pruned_int8_convrot.safetensors")
    assert executor.probe(spec) == {
        "alive": True, "ready": False,
        "detail": "в ComfyUI нет моделей diffusion_models: "
                  "minimax_h3_ref2va_pruned_int8_convrot.safetensors"}
    fake.models["diffusion_models"].append("minimax_h3_ref2va_pruned_int8_convrot.safetensors")
    fake.foreign_running = True
    assert executor.probe(spec) == {"alive": True, "ready": False,
                                    "detail": "в очереди ComfyUI чужие задачи: 1"}
    dead = ComfyExecutor(url="http://127.0.0.1:9", output_dir=tmp_path, scope="local-mac")
    probe = dead.probe(spec)
    assert (probe["alive"], probe["ready"]) == (False, False)


def test_an_engine_error_is_failed_with_its_text(tmp_path):
    comfy = FakeComfyStudio(tmp_path / "out", fail="CUDA out of memory")
    try:
        executor = _fake_executor(comfy, tmp_path)
        status, _ = _wait(executor, executor.submit(_spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va")))
        assert status == {"state": "failed", "progress": None,
                          "error": "ComfyUI: CUDA out of memory"}
    finally:
        comfy.close()


def test_a_template_error_never_reaches_the_engine(fake, tmp_path):
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, "h3-ref2va")
    seven = TaskSpec(task_id=spec.task_id, template="h3-ref2va", values=spec.values,
                     images={"ref_images": [spec.images["ref_images"][0]] * 7},
                     dest_dir=spec.dest_dir)
    with pytest.raises(ExecutorError, match="7 images > limit 6 at 73 frames"):
        executor.submit(seven)
    assert fake.prompts == []


def test_a_dropped_socket_costs_the_progress_not_the_result(tmp_path):
    comfy = FakeComfyStudio(tmp_path / "out", drop_ws=True)
    try:
        executor = _fake_executor(comfy, tmp_path)
        status, progress = _wait(executor, executor.submit(_spec(tmp_path, "h3-fl2va")))
        assert (status["state"], progress) == ("done", [])
    finally:
        comfy.close()


# -- live -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.mark.skipif(not LIVE, reason="STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY not set")
@pytest.mark.parametrize("template", LIVE_TEMPLATES)
def test_live_contract(tmp_path, template):
    output = Path(os.environ["STUDIO_LIVE_COMFY_OUTPUT"])
    disp = os.environ.get("STUDIO_LIVE_DISPATCHER")
    executor = ComfyExecutor(url=LIVE, output_dir=output, scope="live",
                             dispatcher=DispatcherClient(disp, client="studio-worker")
                             if disp else None)
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, template, seed=4242)
    probe = executor.probe(spec)
    assert probe == {"alive": True, "ready": True, "detail": "готов"}, probe
    began = time.time()
    ref = executor.submit(spec)
    assert executor.find(spec) == ref
    status, progress = _wait(executor, ref)
    assert status["state"] == "done", status
    steps = spec.values["steps"]
    assert progress and progress == sorted(progress) and progress[-1] == (steps, steps)
    api = ComfyApi(LIVE)
    item = api.history(ref)
    graph, extra = item["prompt"][2], item["prompt"][3]
    assert extra["studio_task_id"] == spec.task_id
    files = [p for paths in spec.images.values() for p in paths]
    names = [f"{spec.task_id}-{n}.png" for n in range(1, len(files) + 1)]
    # every input of §12.2, values and links, against this test's literals (not build_payload)
    _assert_graph(graph, template, spec.task_id, 4242, steps, names)
    for n, path in enumerate(files, start=1):
        assert api.view(f"{spec.task_id}-{n}.png") == path.read_bytes()
    files = executor.result(ref, spec.dest_dir)
    assert [f.parent for f in files] == [spec.dest_dir]
    if disp:
        assert executor.restarted_since(began) is False
    if template != "krea2-t2i":
        import subprocess
        frames = subprocess.run(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-count_frames", "-select_streams",
                                 "v:0", "-show_entries", "stream=nb_read_frames", "-of",
                                 "csv=p=0", str(files[0])], capture_output=True, text=True)
        assert int(frames.stdout.strip()) == 73
    cancel_spec = _spec(tmp_path, template, seed=7)
    cref = executor.submit(cancel_spec)
    while executor.status(cref)["state"] == "queued":
        time.sleep(0.5)
    executor.cancel(cref)
    status, _ = _wait(executor, cref, timeout=120)
    assert status["state"] == "cancelled"
    assert ComfyApi(LIVE).queue()["queue_running"] == []


def test_a_refused_graph_is_an_error_and_a_lost_answer_is_uncertain(fake, tmp_path):
    """§8.5: HTTP 4xx on POST /prompt — ComfyUI refused it, it never runs: `failed`. No answer at
    all — it may have been accepted: `submit_unknown`, never a blind retry."""
    from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import SubmitUncertain
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    fake.classes.remove("KSampler")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutorError) as refused:
        executor.submit(_spec(tmp_path, "krea2-t2i"))
    assert type(refused.value) is ExecutorError
    assert "HTTP 400" in str(refused.value)
    dead = ComfyExecutor(url="http://127.0.0.1:9", output_dir=tmp_path, scope="local-mac")
    with pytest.raises(SubmitUncertain, match="ComfyUI POST /prompt недоступен"):
        dead.submit(_spec(tmp_path, "krea2-t2i"))


def test_find_that_cannot_ask_the_engine_says_so(tmp_path):
    """«Could not check» is not «not found»: without files, an unreachable engine raises."""
    dead = ComfyExecutor(url="http://127.0.0.1:9", output_dir=tmp_path, scope="local-mac")
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, "krea2-t2i")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutorError, match="очередь и история ComfyUI недоступны"):
        dead.find(spec)
    done = tmp_path / "studio" / spec.task_id
    done.mkdir(parents=True)
    (done / "f_00001_.png").write_bytes(b"x")
    assert dead.find(spec) == f"file:{spec.task_id}"


def test_a_spec_planned_on_another_template_version_is_refused(fake, tmp_path):
    """Codex-10: the executor runs the graph whose sha256 was recorded, or nothing."""
    executor = _fake_executor(fake, tmp_path)
    spec = _spec(tmp_path, "krea2-t2i")
    stale = TaskSpec(**(spec.model_dump() | {"template_sha256": "0" * 64}))
    with pytest.raises(ExecutorError, match=r"^шаблон krea2-t2i изменился после постановки "
                                            r"\(000000000000 → "):
        executor.submit(stale)
    assert fake.prompts == []
    current = TaskSpec(**(spec.model_dump() | {"template_sha256": templates.load("krea2-t2i").sha256}))
    assert _wait(executor, executor.submit(current))[0]["state"] == "done"
```


- [ ] **Step 2: Увидеть красным**

Run: `env -u NODE_OPTIONS PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 $PY -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_studio_executor_contract.py`
Expected: ошибка сбора `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'h3_48gb.studio.executors.base'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Реализация**

Create `h3_48gb/studio/executors/base.py`:

```python
"""The executor contract (spec §8.1). An executor never reads the database and knows nothing of
scenes: it gets a `TaskSpec` (template, values, files to upload, where results go) and answers in
the terms below. The contract test checks every capability an adapter declares, and only those."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol, TypedDict

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class Capabilities(TypedDict):
    find: Literal["exact", "none"]           # can find our task without engine_ref
    restart_evidence: bool                   # can tell the engine restarted after a moment t
    progress: bool                           # (step, total) while running
    cancel: Literal["hard", "soft", "none"]  # hard stops the computation; soft only drops it
    async_submit: bool                       # submit returns a ref quickly, the work goes on


class Probe(TypedDict):
    alive: bool
    ready: bool          # the spec's template and models are there and no foreign task runs
    detail: str


class EngineStatus(TypedDict):
    state: Literal["queued", "running", "done", "failed", "cancelled", "lost"]
    progress: tuple[int, int] | None
    error: str | None


class TaskSpec(BaseModel):
    """What the worker hands an executor: built from the task's payload (an image task) or the
    take's materialized `inputs_json` (a render), plus the task id and the result folder.
    `values` are the template's scalar inputs (prompt, seed, steps, width, height, length…);
    `images` maps an image input of the template to the files to upload, in order."""
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)

    task_id: str
    template: str
    #: the template version the task was planned with; the executor refuses another one
    template_sha256: str | None = None
    values: dict
    images: dict[str, list[Path]] = {}
    dest_dir: Path


class ExecutorError(Exception):
    """The engine refused or failed in a way the worker reports as the task's error."""


class SubmitUncertain(ExecutorError):
    """The request may or may not have reached the engine (timeout, broken connection after the
    body was sent): the task becomes `submit_unknown` (§8.5; resolved in slice S5)."""


class Executor(Protocol):
    name: str
    capabilities: Capabilities

    def gpu_scope(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> str: ...
    def probe(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> Probe: ...
    def estimate_s(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> float: ...
    def submit(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> str: ...
    def engine_version(self) -> str | None: ...              # recorded with the task at submit
    def find(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> str | None: ...
    def restarted_since(self, t: float) -> bool | None: ...
    def status(self, ref: str) -> EngineStatus: ...
    def result(self, ref: str, dest: Path) -> list[Path]: ...
    def cancel(self, ref: str) -> None: ...
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/executors/comfy.py`:

```python
"""The ComfyUI adapter (spec §8.2) — the only executor of part 1: H3 fl2va/ref2va and Krea 2 on
the studio's own ComfyUI (`comfy-studio`, :8189 on alex-neuro).

Identity: the task id travels as `client_id` and `extra_data.studio_task_id` and names the output
folder `studio/<task_id>/` (`filename_prefix`), so the task is found without its `prompt_id`: in
`/queue` and `/history` while the process lives, by its files after a restart (GPU-DAY-20261021
п.5–6). Progress comes over the WebSocket by `prompt_id`; the state itself always from `/history`
and `/queue`, so a dropped socket costs the progress bar, never the result."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import threading
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb.engines.comfy_api import ComfyApi, ComfyError
from h3_48gb.engines.dispatcher_client import DispatcherUnavailable
from h3_48gb.studio import templates
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import (Capabilities, EngineStatus, ExecutorError, Probe,
                                           SubmitUncertain, TaskSpec)
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.comfy_template import TemplateError

#: `ltx.py:31`: a /history entry without a final status is tolerated this many reads, then failed.
MAX_ODD_HISTORY_POLLS = 12
FILE_REF = "file:"


class ComfyExecutor:
    name = "comfy"
    capabilities: Capabilities = {"find": "exact", "restart_evidence": True, "progress": True,
                                  "cancel": "hard", "async_submit": True}

    def __init__(self, *, url: str, output_dir: Path, scope: str, dispatcher=None,
                 engine: str = "comfy-studio", api: ComfyApi | None = None, ws_connect=None):
        self.url = url.rstrip("/")
        self.output_dir = Path(output_dir)
        self.scope = scope
        self.dispatcher = dispatcher          # DispatcherClient, or None on the Mac (local-mac)
        self.engine = engine
        self.api = api or ComfyApi(self.url, timeout=60.0)
        self._ws_connect = ws_connect
        self._progress: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
        self._odd: dict[str, int] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    # -- contract ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def gpu_scope(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> str:
        return self.scope

    def estimate_s(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> float:
        return templates.load(spec.template).estimate_s

    def probe(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> Probe:
        try:
            self.api.system_stats()
        except ComfyError as err:
            return {"alive": False, "ready": False, "detail": str(err)}
        template = templates.load(spec.template)
        try:
            known = self.api.object_info()
            missing = [c for c in template.requires["class_types"] if c not in known]
            if missing:
                return {"alive": True, "ready": False,
                        "detail": f"в ComfyUI нет узлов: {', '.join(missing)}"}
            for folder, names in template.requires["models"].items():
                have = set(self.api.models(folder))
                absent = [n for n in names if n not in have]
                if absent:
                    return {"alive": True, "ready": False,
                            "detail": f"в ComfyUI нет моделей {folder}: {', '.join(absent)}"}
            queue = self.api.queue()
        except ComfyError as err:
            return {"alive": True, "ready": False, "detail": str(err)}
        foreign = [e for e in (*queue.get("queue_running", []), *queue.get("queue_pending", []))
                   if _studio_task_id(e) != spec.task_id]
        if foreign:
            return {"alive": True, "ready": False,
                    "detail": f"в очереди ComfyUI чужие задачи: {len(foreign)}"}
        return {"alive": True, "ready": True, "detail": "готов"}

    def submit(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> str:
        template = templates.load(spec.template)
        if spec.template_sha256 is not None and spec.template_sha256 != template.sha256:
            # the graph that would run is not the one recorded in the take (Codex-10)
            raise ExecutorError(f"шаблон {spec.template} изменился после постановки "
                                f"({spec.template_sha256[:12]} → {template.sha256[:12]}) — "
                                f"постройте план заново")
        values = {**spec.values, "task_id": spec.task_id}
        n = 0
        try:
            for name, paths in spec.images.items():
                uploaded = []
                for path in paths:
                    n += 1
                    uploaded.append(self.api.upload_image(
                        Path(path).read_bytes(), f"{spec.task_id}-{n}{Path(path).suffix.lower()}"))
                values[name] = uploaded if template.card["inputs"][name]["type"] == "image_list" \
                    else uploaded[0]
            payload = template.build_payload(values)
        except TemplateError as err:
            raise ExecutorError(f"шаблон {spec.template}: {err}") from None
        except ComfyError as err:
            raise ExecutorError(str(err)) from None
        self._watch(spec.task_id)
        try:
            return self.api.submit_prompt(payload)
        except ComfyError as err:
            if ": HTTP 4" in str(err) or "отклонил" in str(err):
                raise ExecutorError(str(err)) from None        # refused: it never ran
            raise SubmitUncertain(str(err)) from None          # may have reached the engine

    def engine_version(self) -> str | None:
        """The ComfyUI version as `/system_stats` reports it now — recorded with the task at
        submit (revision 3); None when the engine does not say."""
        try:
            return (self.api.system_stats().get("system") or {}).get("comfyui_version")
        except ComfyError:
            return None

    def find(self, spec: TaskSpec) -> str | None:
        """The task's ref, or None when it is provably nowhere. Raises ExecutorError when the
        engine could not be asked and no files say «done» — «could not check» is not «not
        found» (a retry on it would run the task twice, Codex-4)."""
        try:
            queue = self.api.queue()
            for entry in (*queue.get("queue_running", []), *queue.get("queue_pending", [])):
                if _studio_task_id(entry) == spec.task_id:
                    return entry[1]
            for prompt_id, item in self.api.all_history().items():
                if _studio_task_id(item.get("prompt") or []) == spec.task_id:
                    return prompt_id
            unreachable = None
        except ComfyError as err:
            unreachable = err       # the engine is down: the files below may still be there
        if self._files(spec.task_id):
            return FILE_REF + spec.task_id
        if unreachable is not None:
            raise ExecutorError(f"очередь и история ComfyUI недоступны: {unreachable}")
        return None

    def restarted_since(self, t: float) -> bool | None:
        """True when the dispatcher's record of our engine started after `t`, or there is no
        live record at all (then ComfyUI's in-memory queue is gone); None without a dispatcher
        or when it does not answer — never a guess."""
        if self.dispatcher is None:
            return None
        try:
            record = (self.dispatcher.status().get("own") or {}).get(self.engine)
        except DispatcherUnavailable:
            return None
        if record is None:
            return True
        return float(record["started_at"]) > t

    def status(self, ref: str) -> EngineStatus:
        if ref.startswith(FILE_REF):
            return {"state": "done", "progress": None, "error": None}
        try:
            entry = self.api.history(ref)
            if entry is None:
                queue = self.api.queue()
                if any(e[1] == ref for e in queue.get("queue_running", [])):
                    return {"state": "running", "progress": self._progress.get(ref),
                            "error": None}
                if any(e[1] == ref for e in queue.get("queue_pending", [])):
                    return {"state": "queued", "progress": None, "error": None}
                entry = self.api.history(ref)           # it may have finished between the reads
                if entry is None:
                    return {"state": "lost", "progress": None, "error": None}
        except ComfyError as err:
            raise ExecutorError(str(err)) from None
        status = entry.get("status") or {}
        messages = status.get("messages") or []
        if status.get("status_str") == "success" and status.get("completed"):
            return {"state": "done", "progress": self._progress.get(ref), "error": None}
        if status.get("status_str") == "error":
            if any(m and m[0] == "execution_interrupted" for m in messages):
                return {"state": "cancelled", "progress": None, "error": None}
            message = next((m[1].get("exception_message") for m in messages
                            if m and m[0] == "execution_error"), "ошибка исполнения")
            return {"state": "failed", "progress": None, "error": f"ComfyUI: {message}"}
        with self._lock:
            self._odd[ref] = self._odd.get(ref, 0) + 1
            odd = self._odd[ref]
        if odd >= MAX_ODD_HISTORY_POLLS:
            return {"state": "failed", "progress": None,
                    "error": f"ComfyUI: запись /history без итогового статуса после {odd} опросов"}
        return {"state": "running", "progress": self._progress.get(ref), "error": None}

    def result(self, ref: str, dest: Path) -> list[Path]:
        if ref.startswith(FILE_REF):
            sources = self._files(ref[len(FILE_REF):])
        else:
            entry = self.api.history(ref) or {}
            sources = []
            for output in (entry.get("outputs") or {}).values():
                for items in output.values():
                    for item in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:
                        if isinstance(item, dict) and "filename" in item:
                            sources.append(self.output_dir / item.get("subfolder", "")
                                           / item["filename"])
        dest = Path(dest)
        dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        copied = []
        for src in sources:
            if not src.is_file():
                raise ExecutorError(f"ComfyUI назвал файл, которого нет: {src}")
            target = dest / src.name
            shutil.copy2(src, target)
            copied.append(target)
        if not copied:
            raise ExecutorError("ComfyUI не отдал ни одного файла")
        return copied

    def cancel(self, ref: str) -> None:
        if ref.startswith(FILE_REF):
            return
        try:
            queue = self.api.queue()
            if any(e[1] == ref for e in queue.get("queue_running", [])):
                self.api.interrupt()
            elif any(e[1] == ref for e in queue.get("queue_pending", [])):
                self.api.delete_queued([ref])
        except ComfyError as err:
            raise ExecutorError(str(err)) from None

    # -- helpers ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def progress(self, ref: str) -> tuple[int, int] | None:
        return self._progress.get(ref)

    def _files(self, task_id: str) -> list[Path]:
        folder = self.output_dir / "studio" / task_id
        return sorted(p for p in folder.glob("*") if p.is_file()) if folder.is_dir() else []

    def _watch(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        """Follow `progress` over the socket for this client id; best effort."""
        connect = self._ws_connect
        if connect is None:
            from websockets.sync.client import connect
        ws_url = "ws" + self.url[len("http"):] + f"/ws?clientId={task_id}"
        try:
            # proxy=None: ComfyUI is on the loopback/LAN; websockets would otherwise follow a
            # SOCKS/HTTP proxy from the environment and fail to connect at all.
            socket = connect(ws_url, open_timeout=5, proxy=None)
        except Exception:                     # noqa: BLE001 -- no socket, no progress bar
            return

        def follow():
            try:
                for raw in socket:
                    if not isinstance(raw, str):
                        continue
                    message = json.loads(raw)
                    data = message.get("data") or {}
                    if message.get("type") == "progress" and data.get("prompt_id"):
                        self._progress[data["prompt_id"]] = (int(data["value"]), int(data["max"]))
                    if message.get("type") == "executing" and data.get("node") is None \
                            and data.get("prompt_id"):
                        break
                    if message.get("type") in ("execution_error", "execution_interrupted"):
                        break
            except Exception:                 # noqa: BLE001 -- the socket dropped
                pass
            finally:
                try:
                    socket.close()
                except Exception:             # noqa: BLE001
                    pass

        threading.Thread(target=follow, daemon=True, name=f"comfy-ws-{task_id}").start()


def _studio_task_id(entry) -> str | None:
    """`/queue` entries and `/history[*].prompt` are `[number, prompt_id, graph, extra_data, …]`."""
    try:
        return (entry[3] or {}).get("studio_task_id")
    except (IndexError, TypeError, AttributeError):
        return None
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/migrations/002_tasks.sql`:

```sql
-- Slice S2 (plan 2026-10-22, R-S2-1): what the worker and O12/O13 need that 001 did not carry.
-- A task belongs to a project (the SSE filter, the snapshot's task list, O13); an image task's
-- result is an asset (O13 and the event name it, §7 O12); an asset made by a task names it.
ALTER TABLE task ADD COLUMN project_id TEXT REFERENCES project(id);
ALTER TABLE task ADD COLUMN result_asset_id TEXT REFERENCES asset(id);
-- What actually ran (plan revision 3, Codex r2 finding 10): the ComfyUI version from
-- /system_stats at submit and the template version the graph was built from.
ALTER TABLE task ADD COLUMN engine_version TEXT;
ALTER TABLE task ADD COLUMN template_sha256 TEXT;
ALTER TABLE asset ADD COLUMN origin_task_id TEXT REFERENCES task(id);
CREATE INDEX task_by_status ON task(status, priority, created_at);
CREATE INDEX task_by_project ON task(project_id, created_at);
-- A plan is confirmed once (O11/O12): a second press with a new Idempotency-Key must not queue
-- the same work twice.
ALTER TABLE render_plan ADD COLUMN used_at TEXT;
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/settings.py`:

```python
"""Studio settings (spec §6 `SETTING`, §8.2): addresses of the executor and the dispatcher. A row in
`setting` overrides the default; the defaults are alex-neuro's. On the Mac:
`python -m h3_48gb.studio setting comfy.url http://127.0.0.1:8188` and so on."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3

from h3_48gb.studio import db

DEFAULTS = {
    "comfy.url": "http://127.0.0.1:8189",
    "comfy.output_dir": "/home/alex/Outputs/comfy-h3",
    # dispatcher:<engine> on alex-neuro, local-mac on the Mac (spec §8.4)
    "comfy.scope": "dispatcher:comfy-studio",
    "dispatcher.url": "http://127.0.0.1:8790",
    # the estimate key (§3.1: executor, template, machine, canvas, frames, steps)
    "machine": "alex-neuro",
}


def get(conn: sqlite3.Connection, key: str) -> str:
    if key not in DEFAULTS:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown studio setting {key!r}")
    row = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM setting WHERE key = ?", (key,)).fetchone()
    return row["value"] if row is not None else DEFAULTS[key]


def all_settings(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {key: get(conn, key) for key in DEFAULTS}


def put(conn: sqlite3.Connection, key: str, value: str) -> None:
    if key not in DEFAULTS:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown studio setting {key!r}")
    with db.tx(conn):
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO setting (key, value) VALUES (?, ?) "
                     "ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", (key, value))
```

### Task 6: GPU-гейт студии (§8.4)

**Files:**
- Create: `h3_48gb/studio/gpu.py`
- Test: `tests/test_studio_gpu_gate.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DispatcherClient(url, client="studio-worker")`.
- Produces: `DispatcherGate(client, engine)`: `.acquire(wait) -> bool` (True — отменено), `wait(reason: str, seconds: float) -> bool` — функция воркера; `GpuEngineFailed(reason, log)`; `.acquired: threading.Event`; `.done()`. Параметры старой панели: `ACQUIRE_RETRY_SECONDS=30`, `STARTING_POLL_SECONDS=5`, `HOT_C=80`, `COOL_C=72`. `LocalGate(lock_path)` — flock на время задачи. `IdleRelease(client, gate, *, minutes=15, clock)`: `.tick(busy) -> bool` — `POST /release {"client": "studio-worker", "all": false}` после 15 мин пустой очереди и только если карту брали (или после рестарта `/status` показывает свой движок).

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты**

Create `tests/test_studio_gpu_gate.py`:

```python
"""The studio worker's GPU gate (spec §8.4): the old panel's parameters — 30 s / 5 s retries,
80→72 °C — as exact equalities (test_gpu_gate.py is the specification), plus the Mac flock and
the 15-minute idle release."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from _fake_dispatcher import FakeDispatcher
from h3_48gb.engines.dispatcher_client import DispatcherClient
from h3_48gb.studio.gpu import DispatcherGate, GpuEngineFailed, IdleRelease, LocalGate

READY = {"ok": True, "state": "ready", "engine": "comfy-studio"}


def _gate(fake):
    return DispatcherGate(DispatcherClient(fake.url, client="studio-worker"), "comfy-studio")


def _recorder(cancel_after=None):
    waits = []

    def wait(reason, seconds):
        waits.append((reason, seconds))
        return cancel_after is not None and len(waits) >= cancel_after
    return waits, wait


def test_the_gate_waits_with_the_reason_and_the_panels_intervals():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(acquire=(
        {"ok": True, "state": "wait", "engine": "comfy-studio",
         "reason": "карту держит ComfyUI клиента panel-worker"},
        {"ok": True, "state": "wait_qwen", "engine": "comfy-studio", "reason": "Qwen держит карту"},
        {"ok": True, "state": "starting", "engine": "comfy-studio", "log": "/l.log"},
        READY))
    waits, wait = _recorder()
    try:
        gate = _gate(fake)
        assert gate.acquire(wait) is False
        assert gate.acquired.is_set()
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert waits == [("ждём GPU: карту держит ComfyUI клиента panel-worker", 30.0),
                     ("ждём GPU: Qwen держит карту", 30.0),
                     ("ждём GPU: поднимается comfy-studio", 5.0)]
    assert [c[2] for c in fake.calls if c[1] == "/acquire"] == [
        {"engine": "comfy-studio", "client": "studio-worker"}] * 4


def test_a_hot_card_is_waited_down_to_72():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(acquire=(READY,), temps=(80, 80, 73, 72, 40))
    waits, wait = _recorder()
    try:
        assert _gate(fake).acquire(wait) is False
    finally:
        fake.close()
    # 80 is hot; wait while > 72 (80, 73), stop at 72, ask again, 40 is cool
    assert waits == [("остываем, 80 °C", 30.0), ("остываем, 80 °C", 30.0),
                     ("остываем, 73 °C", 30.0)]


def test_79_degrees_is_not_hot_and_unknown_temperature_never_blocks():
    for kwargs in ({"temps": (79,)}, {"gpu_null": True}):
        fake = FakeDispatcher(acquire=(READY,), **kwargs)
        waits, wait = _recorder()
        try:
            assert _gate(fake).acquire(wait) is False
        finally:
            fake.close()
        assert waits == []


def test_a_cancel_ends_the_wait():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(acquire=({"ok": True, "state": "wait", "engine": "comfy-studio",
                                    "reason": "x"},))
    waits, wait = _recorder(cancel_after=2)
    try:
        assert _gate(fake).acquire(wait) is True
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert waits == [("ждём GPU: x", 30.0)] * 2


def test_a_failed_engine_raises_with_its_log():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(acquire=({"ok": True, "state": "failed", "engine": "comfy-studio",
                                    "reason": "движок не поднялся, смотрите лог",
                                    "log": "/home/alex/Projects/comfy-h3/logs/x.log"},))
    try:
        with pytest.raises(GpuEngineFailed) as err:
            _gate(fake).acquire(_recorder()[1])
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert (err.value.reason, err.value.log) == ("движок не поднялся, смотрите лог",
                                                 "/home/alex/Projects/comfy-h3/logs/x.log")


def test_a_dead_dispatcher_is_a_wait_not_a_failure():
    waits, wait = _recorder(cancel_after=1)
    gate = DispatcherGate(DispatcherClient("http://127.0.0.1:9", timeout=0.5,
                                           client="studio-worker"), "comfy-studio")
    assert gate.acquire(wait) is True
    assert [w[1] for w in waits] == [30.0]
    assert waits[0][0].startswith("ждём GPU: диспетчер недоступен (")


def test_an_answer_without_a_reason_is_shown_as_it_came():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(acquire=({"ok": True, "state": "odd"}, READY))
    waits, wait = _recorder()
    try:
        _gate(fake).acquire(wait)
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert waits == [('ждём GPU: непонятный ответ диспетчера {"ok": true, "state": "odd"}', 30.0)]


def test_the_mac_gate_is_an_exclusive_flock(tmp_path):
    first, second = LocalGate(tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock"), LocalGate(tmp_path / "gpu-mac.lock")
    assert first.acquire(_recorder()[1]) is False
    waits, wait = _recorder(cancel_after=1)
    assert second.acquire(wait) is True
    assert waits == [("ждём GPU: Мак занят (LLM сценариста)", 5.0)]
    first.done()
    assert second.acquire(_recorder()[1]) is False
    second.done()


def test_idle_release_after_15_minutes_of_an_empty_queue_and_only_our_engine():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(acquire=(READY,))
    clock = [0.0]
    try:
        client = DispatcherClient(fake.url, client="studio-worker")
        gate = DispatcherGate(client, "comfy-studio")
        idle = IdleRelease(client, gate, clock=lambda: clock[0])
        gate.acquired.set()
        assert idle.tick(busy=False) is False                 # the countdown starts
        clock[0] = 899.0
        assert idle.tick(busy=False) is False
        clock[0] = 905.0
        assert idle.tick(busy=True) is False                  # a task resets it
        clock[0] = 1000.0
        assert idle.tick(busy=False) is False
        clock[0] = 1900.0
        assert idle.tick(busy=False) is True
        assert not gate.acquired.is_set()
        clock[0] = 9000.0
        assert idle.tick(busy=False) is False                 # nothing taken since: no release
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert [c for c in fake.calls if c[1] == "/release"] == [
        ("POST", "/release", {"client": "studio-worker", "all": False})]


def test_a_restarted_worker_releases_an_engine_it_still_owns():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(own={"comfy-studio": {"owner": "studio-worker", "ready": True}})
    clock = [0.0]
    try:
        client = DispatcherClient(fake.url, client="studio-worker")
        idle = IdleRelease(client, DispatcherGate(client, "comfy-studio"), clock=lambda: clock[0])
        idle.tick(busy=False)
        clock[0] = 900.0
        assert idle.tick(busy=False) is True
    finally:
        fake.close()


def test_a_restarted_worker_leaves_the_panels_engine_alone():
    fake = FakeDispatcher(own={"ltx": {"owner": "panel-worker", "ready": True}})
    clock = [0.0]
    try:
        client = DispatcherClient(fake.url, client="studio-worker")
        idle = IdleRelease(client, DispatcherGate(client, "comfy-studio"), clock=lambda: clock[0])
        idle.tick(busy=False)
        clock[0] = 900.0
        assert idle.tick(busy=False) is False
    finally:
        fake.close()
    assert [c for c in fake.calls if c[1] == "/release"] == []
```


- [ ] **Step 2: Увидеть красным** — `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'h3_48gb.studio.gpu'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Реализация**

Create `h3_48gb/studio/gpu.py`:

```python
"""The GPU gate of the studio worker (spec §8.4): one task at a time per GPU scope. On alex-neuro
the dispatcher owns the card (`acquire("comfy-studio")`); on the Mac a flock the API also takes
for a local LLM (`<outdir>/studio/gpu-mac.lock`, plan S0–S1 R10).

The dispatcher gate repeats the old panel's parameters (`worker.make_gpu_gate`, `worker.py:524`;
its tests `test_gpu_gate.py` are the specification): ask every 30 s, every 5 s while our engine
is starting, no timeout — only a cancel ends the wait; once ready, a card at ≥ 80 °C is waited
down to 72 °C. It does not import the old worker: that gate writes into the file queue."""
from __future__ import annotations

import fcntl
import json
import os
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb.engines.dispatcher_client import DispatcherUnavailable

ACQUIRE_RETRY_SECONDS = 30.0
STARTING_POLL_SECONDS = 5.0
HOT_C = 80
COOL_C = 72
IDLE_RELEASE_MINUTES = 15.0


class GpuEngineFailed(Exception):
    def __init__(self, reason: str, log: str | None):
        super().__init__(reason)
        self.reason = reason
        self.log = log


class DispatcherGate:
    """`wait(reason, seconds) -> bool` is the worker's: it shows the reason and sleeps, and
    answers True when the task was cancelled meanwhile."""

    def __init__(self, client, engine: str):
        self.client = client
        self.engine = engine
        self.acquired = threading.Event()

    def _temperature(self) -> int | None:
        # Unknown temperature never blocks: a thermal wait with no number would hang for ever.
        try:
            return self.client.status()["gpu"]["temperature_c"]
        except (DispatcherUnavailable, KeyError, TypeError):
            return None

    def acquire(self, wait) -> bool:
        """True = cancelled while waiting; False = the card is ours and cool."""
        while True:
            try:
                answer = self.client.acquire(self.engine)
            except DispatcherUnavailable as exc:
                if wait(f"ждём GPU: диспетчер недоступен ({exc})", ACQUIRE_RETRY_SECONDS):
                    return True
                continue
            state = answer.get("state")
            if state == "failed":
                raise GpuEngineFailed(answer.get("reason") or "движок не поднялся",
                                      answer.get("log"))
            if state == "ready":
                self.acquired.set()
                temp = self._temperature()
                if temp is None or temp < HOT_C:
                    return False
                while temp is not None and temp > COOL_C:
                    if wait(f"остываем, {temp} °C", ACQUIRE_RETRY_SECONDS):
                        return True
                    temp = self._temperature()
                continue
            if state == "starting":
                reason, seconds = f"ждём GPU: поднимается {self.engine}", STARTING_POLL_SECONDS
            elif state == "wait_qwen":
                reason, seconds = "ждём GPU: Qwen держит карту", ACQUIRE_RETRY_SECONDS
            else:
                said = answer.get("reason") or (
                    f"непонятный ответ диспетчера {json.dumps(answer, ensure_ascii=False)[:200]}")
                reason, seconds = f"ждём GPU: {said}", ACQUIRE_RETRY_SECONDS
            if wait(reason, seconds):
                return True

    def done(self) -> None:
        """The engine stays up after a task (§8.4); the idle release gives it back."""


class LocalGate:
    """`local-mac`: an exclusive flock for the task's duration."""

    WAIT_SECONDS = 5.0

    def __init__(self, lock_path: Path):
        self.lock_path = Path(lock_path)
        self.fd: int | None = None
        self.acquired = threading.Event()

    def acquire(self, wait) -> bool:
        self.lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        while True:
            fd = os.open(self.lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o664)
            try:
                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            except BlockingIOError:
                os.close(fd)
                if wait("ждём GPU: Мак занят (LLM сценариста)", self.WAIT_SECONDS):
                    return True
                continue
            self.fd = fd
            return False

    def done(self) -> None:
        if self.fd is not None:
            os.close(self.fd)
            self.fd = None


class IdleRelease:
    """POST /release (this client's engines only) once the studio has had no task to run for 15
    minutes — counted from an empty queue, not from an idle card (`worker._IdleRelease`, :603).
    Sent only if the gate took the card since the last release; a restarted worker asks /status
    once whether an engine of its own is still up."""

    def __init__(self, client, gate, *, minutes: float = IDLE_RELEASE_MINUTES,
                 clock=time.monotonic):
        self.client = client
        self.gate = gate
        self.limit = minutes * 60.0
        self.clock = clock
        self.since: float | None = None
        self._asked = False

    def _owns_an_engine(self) -> bool:
        if not self.gate.acquired.is_set() and not self._asked:
            self._asked = True
            try:
                own = self.client.status().get("own") or {}
            except DispatcherUnavailable:
                return False
            if any(r.get("owner") == self.client.client for r in own.values()):
                self.gate.acquired.set()
        return self.gate.acquired.is_set()

    def tick(self, busy: bool) -> bool:
        """True when it released the card on this tick."""
        if busy:
            self.since = None
            return False
        now = self.clock()
        if self.since is None:
            self.since = now
        if now - self.since >= self.limit and self._owns_an_engine():
            try:
                self.client.release()
            except DispatcherUnavailable:
                return False
            self.gate.acquired.clear()
            self.since = None
            return True
        return False
```

Ответ O10: `{plan_id, expires_at, kind, items, total_s, checkpoint_switches, lint, blocked, invalid}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты**

Create `tests/test_studio_keyframe_api.py`:

```python
"""Slice S2, the API of the keyframe (spec §7 O10 image, O12, O13, O14, O22, O23; §9.1 the
keyframe prompt; §7 Idempotency-Key). The worker is not here: test_studio_worker.py runs it."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from _fake_comfy_studio import FakeComfyStudio
from _fake_dispatcher import FakeDispatcher
from _studio_fixtures import BASE_URL, ScriptedLlm
from h3_48gb.engines.dispatcher_client import DispatcherClient
from h3_48gb.studio import config, db, settings
from h3_48gb.studio.app import create_app
from h3_48gb.studio.director.keyframe_prompt import keyframe_prompt

PORTRAIT = "a young woman with a loose dark braid in a pale linen dress"


class ApiRig:
    """The API with a FakeDispatcher and a FakeComfyStudio behind the settings; no worker."""

    def __init__(self, tmp_path):
        self.outdir = tmp_path / "out"
        self.comfy = FakeComfyStudio(tmp_path / "comfy-out")
        self.dispatcher = FakeDispatcher()
        self.client = TestClient(
            create_app(self.outdir, llm=ScriptedLlm([]),
                       dispatcher_factory=lambda url, client: DispatcherClient(
                           url, client=client, timeout=5)),
            base_url=BASE_URL)
        self.conn = db.connect(config.studio_dir(self.outdir) / "studio.sqlite")
        settings.put(self.conn, "comfy.url", self.comfy.url)
        settings.put(self.conn, "comfy.output_dir", str(self.comfy.output_dir))
        settings.put(self.conn, "dispatcher.url", self.dispatcher.url)

    def close(self):
        self.comfy.close()
        self.dispatcher.close()
        self.conn.close()

    def project(self, frame_format="16:9") -> dict:
        r = self.client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес",
                                                           "frame_format": frame_format})
        assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
        return r.json()

    def post(self, path, body, key=None, expect=200):
        headers = {"Idempotency-Key": key} if key else {}
        r = self.client.post(f"/api/studio{path}", json=body, headers=headers)
        assert r.status_code == expect, (r.status_code, r.text)
        return r.json()


@pytest.fixture
def rig(tmp_path):
    r = ApiRig(tmp_path)
    yield r
    r.close()


def _scene1(rig, *, frame_format="16:9", state_in="She stands at the edge of the lake at dawn."):
    p = rig.project(frame_format)
    cast = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "девушка",
        "description_en": "a young woman", "portrait_phrase": PORTRAIT}).json()
    scenes = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": cast["project"]["revision"], "scenes": [{
            "mode": "from_frame", "action_text": f"{PORTRAIT} walks to the water.",
            "state_in_text": state_in, "requested_s": 8, "refs": ["anna"]}]}).json()
    return p, scenes["scenes"][0]


def test_the_keyframe_prompt_is_style_portraits_first_moment_and_framing():
    assert keyframe_prompt(style_block="", portrait_phrases=[PORTRAIT],
                           state_in="she stands at the edge of the lake at dawn",
                           action_text="ignored") == (
        "Live-action photograph, natural light, natural colors. "
        "A young woman with a loose dark braid in a pale linen dress. "
        "She stands at the edge of the lake at dawn. "
        "Medium shot at eye level, the face fully visible, sharp focus on the face.")
    assert keyframe_prompt(style_block="Soft 35 mm look", portrait_phrases=[],
                           state_in=" ", action_text="She runs. Then she stops.") == (
        "Soft 35 mm look. She runs. "
        "Medium shot at eye level, the face fully visible, sharp focus on the face.")


def test_a_scene_keyframe_plan(rig):
    p, s1 = _scene1(rig)
    plan = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"]})
    [item] = plan["items"]
    assert {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k != "seed"} == {
        "kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"], "template": "krea2-t2i",
        # sha256 of krea2-t2i.card.json + .graph.json (22.10) — the version the task must run
        "template_sha256": "b16051ff44452751a5bf5f9773e1d46ad1e3a2dbd5b2358b0237e443b1f00fb2",
        "prompt": "Live-action photograph, natural light, natural colors. "
                  "A young woman with a loose dark braid in a pale linen dress. "
                  "She stands at the edge of the lake at dawn. "
                  "Medium shot at eye level, the face fully visible, sharp focus on the face.",
        "width": 1344, "height": 768, "steps": 8, "estimate_s": 6.0, "estimate_source": "card",
        "status": "ready", "reasons": []}
    assert 1 <= item["seed"] < 2**31
    assert (plan["kind"], plan["total_s"], plan["checkpoint_switches"], plan["blocked"],
            plan["invalid"]) == ("image", 6.0, [], [], [])


def test_portrait_frames_are_768_by_1344_and_the_switch_from_h3_is_counted(rig):
    p, s1 = _scene1(rig, frame_format="9:16")
    rig.conn.execute("INSERT INTO task (id, kind, executor, gpu_scope, payload_json, status, "
                     "engine_submitted_at, created_at, updated_at) VALUES ('k_old', 'render', "
                     "'comfy', 'x', '{\"template\": \"h3-fl2va\"}', 'succeeded', 1.0, 'n', 'n')")
    plan = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"]})
    assert (plan["items"][0]["width"], plan["items"][0]["height"]) == (768, 1344)
    assert plan["checkpoint_switches"] == [{"from": "h3-fl2va", "to": "krea2-t2i",
                                            "seconds": 17.0}]
    assert plan["total_s"] == 23.0


def test_a_free_keyframe_needs_a_size_on_the_64_grid(rig):
    p = rig.project()
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/plan",
                        json={"kind": "image", "prompt": "x", "width": 1000, "height": 768})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (400, "size_invalid")
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/plan", json={"kind": "image"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (400, "args_invalid")


def test_confirming_is_idempotent_and_happens_once(rig):
    p, s1 = _scene1(rig)
    plan = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"]})
    body = {"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]}
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/image", json=body)
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (400, "idempotency_key_required")
    first = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/image", body, key="k1", expect=201)
    assert first == {"task_id": first["task_id"], "status": "queued"}
    assert rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/image", body, key="k1", expect=201) == first
    other = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"]})
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/image",
                        json={"plan_id": other["plan_id"]}, headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k1"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (422, "idempotency_mismatch")
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/image", json=body,
                        headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k2"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "plan_used")
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM task").fetchone()[0] == 1
    stale = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)).isoformat()
    rig.conn.execute("UPDATE render_plan SET expires_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                     (stale, other["plan_id"]))
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/image",
                        json={"plan_id": other["plan_id"]}, headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k3"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "plan_expired")
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/image",
                        json={"plan_id": other["plan_id"]}, headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k4"})
    assert r.json()["error"]["code"] == "plan_expired"      # a failed confirm stores nothing


def test_a_queued_keyframe_is_cancelled_at_once(rig):
    p, s1 = _scene1(rig)
    plan = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"]})
    task_id = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/image", {"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]}, key="k",
                       expect=201)["task_id"]
    task = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}").json()
    assert {k: task[k] for k in ("status", "kind", "template", "cancel_requested")} == {
        "status": "queued", "kind": "image", "template": "krea2-t2i", "cancel_requested": False}
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}/cancel", json={"reason": "передумал"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["status"], r.json()["cancel_requested"]) == (
        200, "cancelled", True)
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}/cancel", json={"reason": ""})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "task_finished")
    kinds = [r[0] for r in rig.conn.execute("SELECT kind FROM event WHERE task_id = ? "
                                            "ORDER BY seq", (task_id,))]
    assert kinds == ["task_queued", "task_cancel_requested", "task_cancelled"]
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert [(t["id"], t["status"]) for t in snap["tasks"]] == [(task_id, "cancelled")]
    r = rig.client.get("/api/studio/tasks/k_nope")
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (404, "task_not_found")


def test_executor_and_gpu_operations(rig):
    [ex] = rig.client.get("/api/studio/executors").json()
    assert {k: ex[k] for k in ("name", "address", "scope", "alive", "ready", "capabilities",
                               "detail")} == {
        "name": "comfy", "address": rig.comfy.url, "scope": "dispatcher:comfy-studio",
        "alive": True, "ready": True,
        "capabilities": {"find": "exact", "restart_evidence": True, "progress": True,
                         "cancel": "hard", "async_submit": True}, "detail": "готов"}
    rig.comfy.classes.remove("MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo")
    [ex] = rig.client.get("/api/studio/executors").json()
    assert (ex["ready"], ex["detail"]) == (
        False, "h3-ref2va: в ComfyUI нет узлов: MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo")
    gpu = rig.client.get("/api/studio/gpu").json()
    assert gpu == rig.dispatcher.status_body() | {"gpu": gpu["gpu"]}
    assert rig.client.post("/api/studio/gpu/release").json() == {"ok": True, "stopped": ["h3"]}
    r = rig.client.post("/api/studio/gpu/qwen/unload")
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()) == (200, {"ok": True, "was_running": True, "exit_code": 0})
    assert [(c[1], c[2]) for c in rig.dispatcher.calls if c[0] == "POST"] == [
        ("/release", {"client": "studio-worker", "all": False}), ("/qwen/unload", {})]
    assert [h.get("Origin") for h in rig.dispatcher.headers] == [None] * len(rig.dispatcher.headers)
    settings.put(rig.conn, "comfy.scope", "local-mac")
    r = rig.client.get("/api/studio/gpu")
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "no_dispatcher")
    settings.put(rig.conn, "comfy.scope", "dispatcher:comfy-studio")
    settings.put(rig.conn, "dispatcher.url", "http://127.0.0.1:9")
    r = rig.client.get("/api/studio/gpu")
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (503, "dispatcher_unavailable")

+
+    @app.get(f"{api}/executors")
+    def executors(conn=Depends(get_conn)):
+        conf = settings.all_settings(conn)
+        executor = ComfyExecutor(url=conf["comfy.url"], output_dir=Path(conf["comfy.output_dir"]),
+                                 scope=conf["comfy.scope"])
+        from h3_48gb.studio import templates
+        from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import TaskSpec
+        probes = {t: executor.probe(TaskSpec(task_id="probe", template=t, values={},
+                                             dest_dir=home))
+                  for t in templates.IDS}
+        alive = all(p["alive"] for p in probes.values())
+        ready = all(p["ready"] for p in probes.values())
+        detail = "; ".join(f"{t}: {p['detail']}" for t, p in probes.items() if not p["ready"])
+        return [{"name": executor.name, "address": conf["comfy.url"], "scope": conf["comfy.scope"],
+                 "alive": alive, "ready": ready, "capabilities": executor.capabilities,
+                 "detail": detail or "готов", "templates": probes}]
+
+    def dispatcher_for(conn, client: str):
+        conf = settings.all_settings(conn)
+        if not conf["comfy.scope"].startswith("dispatcher:"):
+            raise StudioError(409, "no_dispatcher", "На этой машине карту держит не диспетчер",
+                              {"scope": conf["comfy.scope"]})
+        return make_dispatcher(conf["dispatcher.url"], client)
+
+    def dispatcher_call(call):
+        try:
+            return call()
+        except DispatcherUnavailable as err:
+            raise StudioError(503, "dispatcher_unavailable", "Диспетчер GPU не отвечает",
+                              {"reason": str(err)}) from None
+
+    @app.get(f"{api}/gpu")
+    def gpu_status(conn=Depends(get_conn)):
+        client = dispatcher_for(conn, STUDIO_WEB)
+        return dispatcher_call(client.status)
+
+    @app.post(f"{api}/gpu/release")
+    def gpu_release(conn=Depends(get_conn)):
+        client = dispatcher_for(conn, STUDIO_WORKER)
+        return dispatcher_call(lambda: client.release(everything=False))
+
+    @app.post(f"{api}/gpu/qwen/unload")
+    def gpu_qwen_unload(conn=Depends(get_conn)):
+        client = dispatcher_for(conn, STUDIO_WEB)
+        status, body = dispatcher_call(client.qwen_unload)
+        return JSONResponse(body, status_code=status)
+
     @app.get(f"{api}/events")
     async def stream_events(request: Request, after: int = 0,
                             last_event_id: int | None = Header(default=None)):
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/director/keyframe_prompt.py`:

```python
"""The Krea 2 keyframe of a scene (spec §9.1): the project's style, the verbatim portrait of each
hero on screen, the scene's first moment (`state_in`) and a fixed framing — no sound. Size by the
frame format, sides a multiple of 64 (1344×768 measured, GPU-DAY-20261020 п.2)."""
from __future__ import annotations

SIZES = {"16:9": (1344, 768), "9:16": (768, 1344)}
DEFAULT_STYLE = "Live-action photograph, natural light, natural colors."
FRAMING = "Medium shot at eye level, the face fully visible, sharp focus on the face."


def _sentence(text: str) -> str:
    text = " ".join(text.split()).strip()
    if not text:
        return ""
    text = text[0].upper() + text[1:]
    return text if text.endswith((".", "!", "?")) else text + "."


def _first_sentence(text: str) -> str:
    text = " ".join(text.split())
    for mark in (". ", "! ", "? "):
        if mark in text:
            return text[:text.index(mark) + 1]
    return text


def keyframe_prompt(*, style_block: str, portrait_phrases: list[str], state_in: str,
                    action_text: str) -> str:
    """`state_in` is the scene's first moment; without it, the first sentence of the action."""
    moment = state_in.strip() or _first_sentence(action_text)
    parts = [_sentence(style_block) or DEFAULT_STYLE,
             *(_sentence(p) for p in portrait_phrases), _sentence(moment), FRAMING]
    return " ".join(p for p in parts if p)


def keyframe_size(frame_format: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    return SIZES[frame_format]
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/estimates.py`:

```python
"""Time estimates (spec §3.1 `estimate.py` → table `estimate_sample`): the median wall time of the
last ten runs with the same (executor, template, machine, width, height, frames, steps); until
there is history, the template card's `estimate_s` — measured on 3 s / 20 steps for H3 (card,
22.10), so a video estimate is scaled by steps / 20 and frames / 73 (GPU-DAY-20261021 п.1:
8 s / 50 steps took 693–722 s; the scaled card gives 592–658 s)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import statistics

from h3_48gb.studio import templates

WINDOW = 10
BASE_STEPS = 20
BASE_FRAMES = 73
#: Measured cost of a model switch inside one ComfyUI process (GPU-DAY-20261020 п.1,
#: GPU-DAY-20261021 п.4): seconds added to the first task after the switch.
SWITCH_S = {("krea", "h3"): 38.0, ("h3", "krea"): 17.0, ("h3-fl2va", "h3-ref2va"): 16.0,
            ("h3-ref2va", "h3-fl2va"): 16.0}


def _family(template: str) -> str:
    return "krea" if template == templates.IMAGE_TEMPLATE else "h3"


def switch_cost(before: str | None, after: str) -> float:
    if before is None or before == after:
        return 0.0
    if _family(before) != _family(after):
        return SWITCH_S[(_family(before), _family(after))]
    return SWITCH_S[(before, after)]


def estimate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, executor: str, template: str, machine: str,
             width: int, height: int, frames: int, steps: int) -> dict:
    rows = conn.execute(
        "SELECT wall_s FROM estimate_sample WHERE executor = ? AND template = ? AND machine = ? "
        "AND width = ? AND height = ? AND frames = ? AND steps = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?",
        (executor, template, machine, width, height, frames, steps, WINDOW)).fetchall()
    if rows:
        return {"seconds": float(statistics.median(r["wall_s"] for r in rows)),
                "source": "history", "samples": len(rows)}
    card = templates.load(template)
    seconds = card.estimate_s
    if card.kind == "video":
        seconds = seconds * steps / BASE_STEPS * frames / BASE_FRAMES
    return {"seconds": round(seconds, 1), "source": "card", "samples": 0}


def record(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, executor: str, template: str, machine: str, width: int,
           height: int, frames: int, steps: int, wall_s: float) -> None:
    """Called inside the transaction that finishes the task."""
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO estimate_sample (executor, template, machine, width, height, "
                 "frames, steps, wall_s) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                 (executor, template, machine, width, height, frames, steps, float(wall_s)))
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/idempotency.py`:

```python
"""`Idempotency-Key` (spec §7): O11, O12 (and O15, O19, O24 later) run at most once per key. The
check, the operation and the stored answer are one transaction, so a retry after a crash either
finds the answer or finds nothing and runs the operation afresh."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from h3_48gb.studio import db, ids
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError

KEEP = timedelta(hours=24)


def request_sha256(route: str, body: dict) -> str:
    canonical = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    return hashlib.sha256(f"{route}\n{canonical}".encode()).hexdigest()


def run_once(conn: sqlite3.Connection, key: str | None, request_sha: str, operation,
             *, status: int = 201) -> tuple[int, dict]:
    """`operation(conn) -> dict` runs inside the transaction. The same key with the same request
    returns the stored answer; with another request — 422 `idempotency_mismatch`. A failing
    operation stores nothing: the key stays free for a corrected retry."""
    if not key or not key.strip():
        raise StudioError(400, "idempotency_key_required", "Нужен заголовок Idempotency-Key")
    with db.tx(conn):
        cutoff = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - KEEP).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds")
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM op_log WHERE created_at < ?", (cutoff,))
        row = conn.execute("SELECT request_sha256, response_json, http_status FROM op_log "
                           "WHERE idempotency_key = ?", (key,)).fetchone()
        if row is not None:
            if row["request_sha256"] != request_sha:
                raise StudioError(422, "idempotency_mismatch",
                                  "Этот ключ уже использован для другого запроса", {"key": key})
            return row["http_status"], json.loads(row["response_json"])
        answer = operation(conn)
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO op_log (idempotency_key, request_sha256, response_json, "
                     "http_status, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                     (key, request_sha, json.dumps(answer, ensure_ascii=False), status,
                      ids.now_iso()))
        return status, answer
```
