Create `h3_48gb/studio/plans.py`:

```python
"""Dry plans and their confirmation (spec §7 O10–O12). A plan says what will run, how long it will
take and what blocks it; it lives 10 minutes and is confirmed once. Slice S2: the keyframe
(`kind=image`, Krea 2)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import secrets
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from h3_48gb.studio import db, estimates, events, ids, settings, templates
from h3_48gb.studio.director import keyframe_prompt
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError

PLAN_TTL = timedelta(minutes=10)
EXECUTOR = "comfy"
IMAGE_STEPS = 8          # Krea 2 Turbo, GPU-DAY-20261020 п.2 (card default)


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def new_seed() -> int:
    return secrets.randbelow(2**31 - 1) + 1


def last_engine_template(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> str | None:
    """The model the engine holds now, as far as the studio knows: the template of the task it
    submitted last (§8.4: a switch costs 16–38 s and the plan shows it)."""
    row = conn.execute("SELECT payload_json FROM task WHERE engine_submitted_at IS NOT NULL "
                       "ORDER BY engine_submitted_at DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    return json.loads(row["payload_json"]).get("template") if row else None


def switches(conn: sqlite3.Connection, order: list[str]) -> list[dict]:
    out, before = [], last_engine_template(conn)
    for template in order:
        cost = estimates.switch_cost(before, template)
        if cost:
            out.append({"from": before, "to": template, "seconds": cost})
        before = template
    return out


def _save_plan(conn, project_id: str, *, tier: str | None, items: list[dict],
               versions: dict) -> tuple[str, str]:
    plan_id = ids.new_id("pl")
    expires = (_now() + PLAN_TTL).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds")
    with db.tx(conn):
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO render_plan (id, project_id, tier, items_json, versions_json, "
                     "expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                     (plan_id, project_id, tier, json.dumps(items, ensure_ascii=False),
                      json.dumps(versions), expires))
    return plan_id, expires


def load_plan(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, plan_id: str, kind: str) -> dict:
    """Inside the confirming transaction: the plan, refused if unknown, of another kind,
    expired or already confirmed."""
    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM render_plan WHERE id = ? AND project_id = ?",
                       (plan_id, project_id)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise StudioError(404, "plan_not_found", "План не найден", {"plan_id": plan_id})
    items = json.loads(row["items_json"])
    if not items or items[0]["kind"] != kind:
        raise StudioError(400, "plan_kind_mismatch", "Этот план для другой операции",
                          {"plan_id": plan_id})
    if row["used_at"] is not None:
        raise StudioError(409, "plan_used", "Этот план уже подтверждён",
                          {"plan_id": plan_id, "used_at": row["used_at"]})
    if datetime.fromisoformat(row["expires_at"]) < _now():
        raise StudioError(409, "plan_expired", "План устарел (живёт 10 минут) — постройте заново",
                          {"plan_id": plan_id, "expires_at": row["expires_at"]})
    conn.execute("UPDATE render_plan SET used_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (ids.now_iso(), plan_id))
    return {"items": items, "versions": json.loads(row["versions_json"]), "tier": row["tier"]}


# -- O10 kind=image ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def plan_image(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, *, scene_id: str | None,
               prompt: str | None, width: int | None, height: int | None) -> dict:
    """The keyframe of a scene (prompt from `keyframe_prompt`, size by the frame format) or a
    free one (prompt and size given)."""
    project = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM project WHERE id = ?", (project_id,)).fetchone()
    if scene_id is not None:
        scene = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM scene WHERE id = ? AND project_id = ? "
                             "AND deleted_at IS NULL", (scene_id, project_id)).fetchone()
        if scene is None:
            raise StudioError(404, "scene_not_found", "Сцена не найдена", {"scene_id": scene_id})
        phrases = [r["portrait_phrase"] for r in conn.execute(
            "SELECT c.portrait_phrase FROM scene_ref r JOIN cast_member c ON c.id = r.cast_id "
            "WHERE r.scene_id = ? ORDER BY r.ord", (scene_id,))]
        prompt = keyframe_prompt.keyframe_prompt(
            style_block=project["style_block"], portrait_phrases=phrases,
            state_in=scene["state_in_text"], action_text=scene["action_text"])
        width, height = keyframe_prompt.keyframe_size(project["frame_format"])
    elif not prompt or width is None or height is None:
        raise StudioError(400, "args_invalid", "Нужна сцена или промпт с размером кадра")
    limits = templates.load(templates.IMAGE_TEMPLATE).card["inputs"]
    for name, value in (("width", width), ("height", height)):
        spec = limits[name]
        if not (spec["min"] <= value <= spec["max"]) or value % spec["multiple_of"]:
            raise StudioError(400, "size_invalid",
                              f"Сторона кадра {value}: нужно {spec['min']}–{spec['max']}, "
                              f"кратно {spec['multiple_of']}", {name: value})
    estimate = estimates.estimate(conn, executor=EXECUTOR, template=templates.IMAGE_TEMPLATE,
                                  machine=settings.get(conn, "machine"), width=width,
                                  height=height, frames=0, steps=IMAGE_STEPS)
    item = {"kind": "image", "scene_id": scene_id, "template": templates.IMAGE_TEMPLATE,
            "template_sha256": templates.load(templates.IMAGE_TEMPLATE).sha256,
            "prompt": prompt, "width": width, "height": height, "seed": new_seed(),
            "steps": IMAGE_STEPS, "estimate_s": estimate["seconds"],
            "estimate_source": estimate["source"], "status": "ready", "reasons": []}
    switch = switches(conn, [templates.IMAGE_TEMPLATE])
    plan_id, expires = _save_plan(conn, project_id, tier=None, items=[item], versions={})
    return {"plan_id": plan_id, "expires_at": expires, "kind": "image", "items": [item],
            "total_s": round(item["estimate_s"] + sum(s["seconds"] for s in switch), 1),
            "checkpoint_switches": switch, "lint": {}, "blocked": [], "invalid": []}


# -- O12 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

def enqueue_image(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, plan_id: str, key: str) -> dict:
    """Inside `idempotency.run_once`'s transaction: one task, `queued`."""
    plan = load_plan(conn, project_id, plan_id, "image")
    item = plan["items"][0]
    task_id = ids.new_id("k")
    now = ids.now_iso()
    payload = {k: item[k] for k in ("template", "template_sha256", "prompt", "width", "height",
                                    "seed", "steps", "scene_id")}
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO task (id, kind, executor, gpu_scope, payload_json, idempotency_key, status, "
        "project_id, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, 'image', ?, ?, ?, ?, 'queued', ?, ?, ?)",
        (task_id, EXECUTOR, settings.get(conn, "comfy.scope"),
         json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False), key, project_id, now, now))
    events.emit(conn, "task_queued", project_id=project_id, task_id=task_id,
                payload={"kind": "image", "plan_id": plan_id})
    return {"task_id": task_id, "status": "queued"}
```

+

+
+
+class CancelRequest(_Body):
+    reason: str = Field(default="", max_length=500)

Create `h3_48gb/studio/tasks.py`:

```python
"""Tasks (spec §8.5): their public shape (O13), the project's list for the snapshot (O3) and the
logical cancel (O14). The worker owns every other state change."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
import time

from h3_48gb.studio import db, events, ids
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError

TERMINAL = ("succeeded", "failed", "cancelled")
#: Not yet taken by the worker: O14 ends them at once. Everything else is the worker's to stop
#: (it watches `cancel_requested_at` in every wait and while the engine runs).
NOT_STARTED = ("queued", "blocked")


def task_out(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict:
    payload = json.loads(row["payload_json"])
    asset = row["result_asset_id"]
    return {"id": row["id"], "kind": row["kind"], "project_id": row["project_id"],
            "status": row["status"], "template": payload.get("template"),
            "wait_reason": row["wait_reason"], "progress_step": row["progress_step"],
            "progress_total": row["progress_total"], "error": row["error"],
            "attempts": row["attempts"], "engine_stop": row["engine_stop"],
            "engine_version": row["engine_version"], "template_sha256": row["template_sha256"],
            "cancel_requested": row["cancel_requested_at"] is not None,
            "depends_on_task_id": row["depends_on_task_id"],
            "result_asset_id": asset,
            "result_url": f"/api/studio/assets/{asset}/file" if asset else None,
            "created_at": row["created_at"], "updated_at": row["updated_at"]}


def task_row(conn: sqlite3.Connection, task_id: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM task WHERE id = ?", (task_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise StudioError(404, "task_not_found", "Задача не найдена", {"task_id": task_id})
    return row


def get_task(conn: sqlite3.Connection, task_id: str) -> dict:
    return task_out(task_row(conn, task_id))


def project_tasks(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    # rowid = insertion order: the tasks of one O11 share their created_at to the millisecond
    rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM task WHERE project_id = ? ORDER BY rowid",
                        (project_id,)).fetchall()
    return [task_out(r) for r in rows]


def cancel_task(conn: sqlite3.Connection, task_id: str, *, reason: str) -> dict:
    """O14: the logical cancel — from now on the task's result is never attached, even if the
    engine finishes it (§8.5). A task the worker has not taken ends here; one it has taken is
    stopped by the worker (engine cancel, up to 30 s)."""
    with db.tx(conn):
        row = task_row(conn, task_id)
        if row["status"] in TERMINAL:
            raise StudioError(409, "task_finished", "Задача уже завершена",
                              {"task": task_out(row)})
        now = ids.now_iso()
        conn.execute("UPDATE task SET cancel_requested_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                     (time.time(), now, task_id))
        events.emit(conn, "task_cancel_requested", project_id=row["project_id"], task_id=task_id,
                    payload={"reason": reason})
        if row["status"] in NOT_STARTED:
            finish_cancelled(conn, task_id, project_id=row["project_id"], error=None)
        return task_out(task_row(conn, task_id))


def finish_cancelled(conn: sqlite3.Connection, task_id: str, *, project_id: str | None,
                     error: str | None, engine_stop: str | None = None) -> None:
    """Inside a transaction: the task and its take (slice S3) end as cancelled."""
    conn.execute("UPDATE task SET status = 'cancelled', error = COALESCE(?, error), "
                 "engine_stop = ?, wait_reason = NULL, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                 (error, engine_stop, ids.now_iso(), task_id))
    conn.execute("UPDATE take SET status = 'cancelled' WHERE task_id = ? "
                 "AND status IN ('pending_input', 'queued', 'running')", (task_id,))
    events.emit(conn, "task_cancelled", project_id=project_id, task_id=task_id,
                payload={"engine_stop": engine_stop})
```

2. В `run_once` проверку `row["request_sha256"] != request_sha` → `if False:`. Expected: тот же тест красный (`idempotency_mismatch` не отдан).
3. В `cancel_task` `if row["status"] in NOT_STARTED:` → `if False:`. Expected: `FAILED …::test_a_queued_keyframe_is_cancelled_at_once - assert (200, 'queued', True) == (200, 'cancelled', True)`.
4. `SIZES` 9:16 → `(1344, 768)`. Expected: `FAILED …::test_portrait_frames_are_768_by_1344_and_the_switch_from_h3_is_counted - assert (1344, 768) == (768, 1344)`.
5. `SWITCH_S[("h3", "krea")]` 17 → 38. Expected: тот же тест, `seconds': 38.0}] == […17.0}]`.

### Task 8: Воркер — жизненный цикл задачи и кадр Krea 2 (§4.2, §8.5)

**Files:**
- Create: `h3_48gb/studio/worker.py`, `h3_48gb/studio/images.py`, `tests/_studio_rig.py`
- Modify: `h3_48gb/studio/__main__.py` (подкоманды `worker`, `setting`)
- Test: `tests/test_studio_worker.py`, `tests/test_studio_cli.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: всё из задач 2–7.
- Produces:
  - `Worker(db_path, *, executor, gate, kinds, idle=None, poll_s=2, probe_s=5, ready_timeout_s=600, cancel_wait_s=30, clock, sleep)`: `.run_once() -> bool`, `.run_forever(stop)`, `.recover() -> int` (R-S2-15). Статусы задачи: `queued → waiting_gpu → waiting_ready → submitting → running → downloading → verifying → succeeded`, ответвления `failed`, `cancelling → cancelled`, `submit_unknown`. Ред. 2 (§8.5, Codex-4): `lost` → `find`; задачи нигде нет **и** `restarted_since(engine_submitted_at) is True` → `queued` (один раз), иначе (`False`/`None`) — `submit_unknown`, `engine_ref` сохранён; 30 подряд неудачных проверок статуса и `find`, который не смог спросить движок, — тоже `submit_unknown`, не повтор. События: `task_waiting {wait_reason}`, `task_running {engine_ref}`, `task_progress {step, total}`, `task_succeeded {…kind.finish}`, `task_failed {error}`, `task_requeued {reason}`, `task_submit_unknown {error}`, `task_cancelled {engine_stop}`.
  - Вид задачи — объект с `kind`, `prepare(conn)`, `spec(conn, task) -> TaskSpec`, `on_running`, `on_requeued`, `on_failed`, `finish(conn, task, files, spec, wall_s) -> dict` (в транзакции воркера). `images.ImageKind(home, *, machine)`, `images.VerifyError`.
  - `worker.build_worker(outdir, **overrides)` из настроек (`dispatcher:<engine>` → `DispatcherGate` + `IdleRelease`; `local-mac` → `LocalGate(<outdir>/studio/gpu-mac.lock)`); CLI `python -m h3_48gb.studio worker --outdir …` (один на outdir: `flock <outdir>/studio/worker.lock`, второй — код 2) и `python -m h3_48gb.studio setting [KEY [VALUE]] --outdir …`.
  - Файлы кадра: `<outdir>/studio/projects/<pid>/keyframes/<task_id>/f_00001_.png`.
  - `tests/_studio_rig.Rig(tmp_path, *, acquire=(READY,), comfy_kwargs=None, kinds=None, own=None)`: `.client`, `.comfy`, `.dispatcher`, `.executor`, `.gate`, `.worker`, `.conn`, `.home`, `.db_path`, `.project()`, `.post(path, body, key=None|False, expect=200)`, `.events(*kinds)`, `.task(id)`, `.drain()`, `.close()`.

Create `tests/_studio_rig.py`:

```python
"""A studio on fakes for the S2–S3 tests (spec §12.1): the API (TestClient), the real worker with
the real ComfyUI adapter, a FakeComfyStudio and a FakeDispatcher; polls shortened, nothing else
replaced. Imported by name, like `_studio_fixtures`."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

from _fake_comfy_studio import FakeComfyStudio
from _fake_dispatcher import FakeDispatcher
from _studio_fixtures import BASE_URL, ScriptedLlm
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from h3_48gb.engines.dispatcher_client import DispatcherClient
from h3_48gb.studio import config, db, settings
from h3_48gb.studio.app import create_app
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.comfy import ComfyExecutor
from h3_48gb.studio.gpu import DispatcherGate
from h3_48gb.studio.images import ImageKind
from h3_48gb.studio.worker import Worker

READY = {"ok": True, "state": "ready", "engine": "comfy-studio"}


class Rig:
    def __init__(self, tmp_path: Path, *, acquire=(READY,), comfy_kwargs=None, kinds=None,
                 own=None):
        self.outdir = tmp_path / "out"
        self.comfy = FakeComfyStudio(tmp_path / "comfy-out", **(comfy_kwargs or {}))
        self.dispatcher = FakeDispatcher(acquire=acquire, own=own)
        self.client = TestClient(
            create_app(self.outdir, llm=ScriptedLlm([]),
                       dispatcher_factory=lambda url, client: DispatcherClient(
                           self.dispatcher.url, client=client, timeout=5)),
            base_url=BASE_URL)
        self.home = config.studio_dir(self.outdir)
        self.db_path = self.home / "studio.sqlite"
        self.conn = db.connect(self.db_path)
        settings.put(self.conn, "comfy.url", self.comfy.url)
        settings.put(self.conn, "comfy.output_dir", str(self.comfy.output_dir))
        settings.put(self.conn, "dispatcher.url", self.dispatcher.url)
        self.sleeps: list[float] = []
        dclient = DispatcherClient(self.dispatcher.url, client="studio-worker", timeout=5)
        self.executor = ComfyExecutor(url=self.comfy.url, output_dir=self.comfy.output_dir,
                                      scope="dispatcher:comfy-studio", dispatcher=dclient)
        self.gate = DispatcherGate(dclient, "comfy-studio")
        self.worker = Worker(self.db_path, executor=self.executor, gate=self.gate,
                             kinds=kinds(self) if kinds else
                             {"image": ImageKind(self.home, machine="alex-neuro")},
                             poll_s=0.01, probe_s=0.01, cancel_wait_s=2.0,
                             sleep=self._sleep)

    def _sleep(self, seconds):
        import time
        self.sleeps.append(seconds)
        time.sleep(min(seconds, 0.01))

    def close(self):
        self.comfy.close()
        self.dispatcher.close()
        self.conn.close()
        self.worker.conn.close()

    # -- API helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def project(self, frame_format="16:9") -> dict:
        r = self.client.post("/api/studio/projects", json={"title": "Лес",
                                                           "frame_format": frame_format})
        assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
        return r.json()

    def post(self, path, body, key=None, expect=200):
        headers = {"Idempotency-Key": key or str(uuid.uuid4())} if key is not False else {}
        r = self.client.post(f"/api/studio{path}", json=body, headers=headers)
        assert r.status_code == expect, (r.status_code, r.text)
        return r.json()

    def events(self, *kinds) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
        rows = self.conn.execute("SELECT kind, payload_json FROM event ORDER BY seq").fetchall()
        return [(r["kind"], json.loads(r["payload_json"])) for r in rows
                if not kinds or r["kind"] in kinds]

    def task(self, task_id) -> dict:
        return self.client.get(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}").json()

    def drain(self, limit=20) -> int:
        n = 0
        while n < limit and self.worker.run_once():
            n += 1
        return n
```

Create `tests/test_studio_cli.py`:

```python
"""`python -m h3_48gb.studio worker|setting` (spec §4.2, §6 SETTING): one worker per outdir, the
settings it is built from."""
from __future__ import annotations

import fcntl

import pytest

from h3_48gb.studio import config, db, settings
from h3_48gb.studio.__main__ import main
from h3_48gb.studio.gpu import DispatcherGate, LocalGate
from h3_48gb.studio.worker import build_worker


def test_setting_shows_and_sets(tmp_path, capsys):
    out = str(tmp_path / "out")
    assert main(["setting", "--outdir", out, "comfy.url", "http://127.0.0.1:8188"]) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "comfy.url = http://127.0.0.1:8188\n"
    assert main(["setting", "--outdir", out]) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == (
        "comfy.url = http://127.0.0.1:8188\n"
        "comfy.output_dir = /home/alex/Outputs/comfy-h3\n"
        "comfy.scope = dispatcher:comfy-studio\n"
        "dispatcher.url = http://127.0.0.1:8790\n"
        "machine = alex-neuro\n")
    assert main(["setting", "--outdir", out, "nope"]) == 2


def test_a_second_worker_is_refused(tmp_path, capsys):
    home = config.studio_dir(tmp_path / "out")
    home.mkdir(parents=True)
    with open(home / "worker.lock", "w") as held:
        fcntl.flock(held, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        assert main(["worker", "--outdir", str(tmp_path / "out")]) == 2
    assert capsys.readouterr().err == "studio: второй воркер не нужен — worker.lock занят\n"


def test_the_worker_is_built_from_the_settings(tmp_path):
    outdir = tmp_path / "out"
    worker = build_worker(outdir)
    assert isinstance(worker.gate, DispatcherGate) and worker.gate.engine == "comfy-studio"
    assert (worker.executor.url, worker.executor.scope, sorted(worker.kinds)) == (
        "http://127.0.0.1:8189", "dispatcher:comfy-studio", ["image"])
    conn = db.connect(config.studio_dir(outdir) / "studio.sqlite")
    settings.put(conn, "comfy.scope", "local-mac")
    worker = build_worker(outdir)
    assert isinstance(worker.gate, LocalGate) and worker.idle is None
    assert worker.gate.lock_path == config.studio_dir(outdir) / "gpu-mac.lock"
    settings.put(conn, "comfy.scope", "somewhere")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="dispatcher:<engine> или local-mac"):
        build_worker(outdir)
```

Create `tests/test_studio_worker.py`:

```python
"""Slice S2, the worker on the keyframe (spec §8.5 lifecycle, §8.4 GPU gate, И9): the real API,
worker and ComfyUI adapter against FakeComfyStudio and FakeDispatcher (`_studio_rig`)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import time

import pytest

from _studio_rig import READY, Rig

PORTRAIT = "a young woman with a loose dark braid in a pale linen dress"


@pytest.fixture
def rig(tmp_path):
    r = Rig(tmp_path)
    yield r
    r.close()


def _engine_started(rig, at: float) -> None:
    """What the dispatcher's /status says of our engine (restart evidence, §8.2)."""
    rig.dispatcher.own = {"comfy-studio": {"pid": 1, "owner": "studio-worker", "started_at": at,
                                           "ready": True}}


def _scene1(rig, *, frame_format="16:9", state_in="She stands at the edge of the lake at dawn."):
    p = rig.project(frame_format)
    cast = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "девушка",
        "description_en": "a young woman", "portrait_phrase": PORTRAIT}).json()
    scenes = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": cast["project"]["revision"], "scenes": [{
            "mode": "from_frame", "action_text": f"{PORTRAIT} walks to the water.",
            "state_in_text": state_in, "requested_s": 8, "refs": ["anna"]}]}).json()
    return p, scenes["scenes"][0]


def _queued_keyframe(rig):
    p, s1 = _scene1(rig)
    plan = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"]},
                    key=False)
    task = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/image", {"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]}, expect=201)
    return p, s1, plan["items"][0], task["task_id"]


def test_the_keyframe_is_made_recorded_and_attached_to_scene_1(rig):
    p, s1, item, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    assert rig.drain() == 1
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    asset_id = task["result_asset_id"]
    assert {k: task[k] for k in ("status", "kind", "project_id", "template", "progress_step",
                                 "progress_total", "error", "attempts", "result_url")} == {
        "status": "succeeded", "kind": "image", "project_id": p["id"], "template": "krea2-t2i",
        "progress_step": 8, "progress_total": 8, "error": None, "attempts": 0,
        "result_url": f"/api/studio/assets/{asset_id}/file"}
    [job] = rig.comfy.prompts
    graph = job["prompt"]
    assert (graph["4"]["inputs"]["text"], graph["7"]["inputs"]["seed"],
            graph["6"]["inputs"]["width"], graph["6"]["inputs"]["height"],
            job["extra_data"]["studio_task_id"]) == (item["prompt"], item["seed"], 1344, 768,
                                                     task_id)
    path = rig.home / "projects" / p["id"] / "keyframes" / task_id / "f_00001_.png"
    asset = rig.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM asset WHERE id = ?", (asset_id,)).fetchone()
    engine_file = rig.comfy.output_dir / "studio" / task_id / "f_00001_.png"
    assert (asset["kind"], asset["path"], asset["sha256"], asset["origin_kind"],
            asset["origin_task_id"], asset["mime"]) == (
        "image", str(path), hashlib.sha256(engine_file.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), "take_output",
        task_id, "image/png")
    kinds = [k for k, _ in rig.events() if k.startswith("task_")]
    assert kinds == ["task_queued", "task_running"] + ["task_progress"] * len(
        [k for k in kinds if k == "task_progress"]) + ["task_succeeded"]
    assert rig.events("task_succeeded") == [("task_succeeded",
                                             {"kind": "image", "asset_id": asset_id})]
    sample = rig.conn.execute("SELECT executor, template, machine, width, height, frames, steps "
                              "FROM estimate_sample").fetchall()
    assert [tuple(r) for r in sample] == [("comfy", "krea2-t2i", "alex-neuro", 1344, 768, 0, 8)]
    r = rig.client.get(task["result_url"])
    assert r.content == engine_file.read_bytes()
    patched = rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}", json={
        "expected_edit_version": s1["edit_version"],
        "source_frame": {"kind": "asset", "asset_id": asset_id}}).json()
    assert patched["scene"]["source_frame"] == {"kind": "asset", "scene_id": None,
                                                "asset_id": asset_id}
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert [t["id"] for t in snap["tasks"]] == [task_id]


def test_engine_ref_is_written_before_the_first_status_poll(rig):
    """И9: a crash between the answer and the first poll must not lose the ref."""
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    seen = []
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        row = rig.conn.execute("SELECT engine_ref, status, submit_attempt_at FROM task "
                               "WHERE id = ?", (task_id,)).fetchone()
        seen.append((row["engine_ref"], row["status"], row["submit_attempt_at"] is not None))
        return real(ref)
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    [job] = rig.comfy.prompts
    assert seen[0] == (job["prompt_id"], "running", True)


def test_a_running_keyframe_is_cancelled_on_the_engine(rig):
    rig.comfy.hold = True
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "running":
            rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}/cancel", json={"reason": "передумал"})
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["engine_stop"], task["cancel_requested"], task["result_asset_id"],
            rig.comfy.interrupts) == ("cancelled", "confirmed", True, None, 1)
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM asset").fetchone()[0] == 0
    assert rig.events("task_cancel_requested") == [("task_cancel_requested",
                                                    {"reason": "передумал"})]


def test_a_result_after_the_cancel_is_never_attached(rig):
    """§8.5 logical cancel: the engine finished anyway — the picture is not recorded."""
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    real = rig.executor.result

    def result(ref, dest):
        files = real(ref, dest)
        rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}/cancel", json={"reason": "поздно"})
        return files
    rig.executor.result = result
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["engine_stop"], task["result_asset_id"]) == (
        "cancelled", "finished", None)
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM asset").fetchone()[0] == 0


def test_a_queued_keyframe_is_cancelled_at_once_and_never_runs(rig):
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}/cancel", json={"reason": ""})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["status"]) == (200, "cancelled")
    assert rig.drain() == 0
    assert rig.comfy.prompts == []
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/tasks/{task_id}/cancel", json={"reason": ""})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "task_finished")


def test_waiting_for_the_card_shows_why(tmp_path):
    rig = Rig(tmp_path, acquire=({"ok": True, "state": "wait_qwen", "engine": "comfy-studio",
                                  "reason": "Qwen держит карту"},
                                 {"ok": True, "state": "starting", "engine": "comfy-studio"},
                                 READY))
    try:
        _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
        rig.drain()
        assert rig.events("task_waiting") == [
            ("task_waiting", {"wait_reason": "ждём GPU: Qwen держит карту"}),
            ("task_waiting", {"wait_reason": "ждём GPU: поднимается comfy-studio"})]
        assert rig.task(task_id)["status"] == "succeeded"
        assert rig.task(task_id)["wait_reason"] is None
        r = rig.client.post("/api/studio/gpu/qwen/unload")
        assert (r.status_code, r.json()) == (200, {"ok": True, "was_running": True,
                                                   "exit_code": 0})
    finally:
        rig.close()


def test_an_engine_error_fails_the_task_with_its_text(tmp_path):
    rig = Rig(tmp_path, comfy_kwargs={"fail": "CUDA out of memory"})
    try:
        _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
        rig.drain()
        assert (rig.task(task_id)["status"], rig.task(task_id)["error"]) == (
            "failed", "ComfyUI: CUDA out of memory")
        assert rig.events("task_failed") == [("task_failed",
                                              {"error": "ComfyUI: CUDA out of memory"})]
    finally:
        rig.close()


def test_a_failed_engine_start_fails_the_task_with_the_log(tmp_path):
    rig = Rig(tmp_path, acquire=({"ok": True, "state": "failed", "engine": "comfy-studio",
                                  "reason": "движок не поднялся за 600 с, смотрите лог",
                                  "log": "/l.log"},))
    try:
        _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
        rig.drain()
        assert rig.task(task_id)["error"] == \
            "движок не поднялся: движок не поднялся за 600 с, смотрите лог (лог /l.log)"
        assert rig.comfy.prompts == []
    finally:
        rig.close()


def test_a_task_lost_in_a_restart_is_retried_once(rig):
    rig.comfy.hold = True
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    real = rig.executor.status
    restarted = []

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "running" and not restarted:
            restarted.append(ref)
            rig.comfy.restart()             # kill -9: queue, history and the running prompt gone
            rig.comfy.hold = False
            _engine_started(rig, time.time() + 60)   # the dispatcher's proof of the restart
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["attempts"], len(rig.comfy.prompts)) == ("succeeded", 1, 2)
    assert rig.events("task_requeued") == [("task_requeued", {"reason": "lost"})]


def test_a_task_lost_twice_fails(rig):
    """§8.5: lost -> one automatic retry; the second loss is a failure, not a loop."""
    rig.comfy.hold = True
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "running":
            rig.comfy.restart()
            rig.comfy.hold = True
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["attempts"], task["error"], len(rig.comfy.prompts)) == (
        "failed", 1, "ComfyUI потерял задачу дважды", 2)


def test_a_lost_task_whose_files_exist_is_taken_from_the_files(rig):
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "done":
            rig.comfy.restart()             # finished, then killed: history gone, files stay
            return real(ref)
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["attempts"], len(rig.comfy.prompts)) == ("succeeded", 0, 1)
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT engine_ref FROM task").fetchone()[0] == f"file:{task_id}"


def test_a_picture_of_the_wrong_size_fails_verification(rig):
    p, s1 = _scene1(rig)
    plan = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "prompt": "x", "width": 1344,
                                                  "height": 768})
    task_id = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/image", {"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]},
                       expect=201)["task_id"]
    real = rig.executor.result

    def result(ref, dest):
        files = real(ref, dest)
        from PIL import Image
        Image.new("RGB", (64, 64)).save(files[0])
        return files
    rig.executor.result = result
    rig.drain()
    assert (rig.task(task_id)["status"], rig.task(task_id)["error"]) == (
        "failed", "результат не прошёл проверку: кадр 64×64 вместо 1344×768")


def test_a_restarted_worker_follows_the_accepted_task_on_without_resubmitting(rig):
    """Review Focus 1: the container is restarted while ComfyUI renders. The new worker does not
    submit again (the engine has it, `engine_ref` is written — И9) and records the result."""
    from h3_48gb.studio.worker import Worker
    rig.comfy.hold = True
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    real = rig.executor.status

    class Crash(Exception):
        pass

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "running":
            raise Crash                        # the process dies here
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    with pytest.raises(Crash):
        rig.drain()
    assert rig.task(task_id)["status"] == "running"
    rig.executor.status = real
    rig.comfy.release()
    reborn = Worker(rig.db_path, executor=rig.executor, gate=rig.gate, kinds=rig.worker.kinds,
                    poll_s=0.01, probe_s=0.01, sleep=rig._sleep)
    try:
        assert reborn.run_once() is True
        assert reborn.run_once() is False
    finally:
        reborn.conn.close()
    assert (rig.task(task_id)["status"], len(rig.comfy.prompts)) == ("succeeded", 1)


def test_a_restart_puts_waiting_tasks_back_and_never_resubmits_an_unknown_one(rig):
    _, _, _, waiting = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    _, _, _, cut = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    rig.conn.execute("UPDATE task SET status = 'waiting_gpu', wait_reason = 'x' WHERE id = ?",
                     (waiting,))
    rig.conn.execute("UPDATE task SET status = 'submitting', submit_attempt_at = 1 WHERE id = ?",
                     (cut,))
    assert rig.worker.recover() == 0
    assert (rig.task(waiting)["status"], rig.task(waiting)["wait_reason"]) == ("queued", None)
    assert (rig.task(cut)["status"], rig.task(cut)["wait_reason"]) == (
        "submit_unknown", "Воркер перезапущен во время постановки: не знаю, принял ли ComfyUI "
                          "задачу")
    rig.worker._recovered = True
    rig.drain()
    assert (rig.task(waiting)["status"], rig.task(cut)["status"], len(rig.comfy.prompts)) == (
        "succeeded", "submit_unknown", 1)


def test_a_lost_task_without_proof_of_a_restart_is_not_retried(rig):
    """§8.5 / Codex-4: ComfyUI forgot the task, but the dispatcher shows the same process as
    before the submit — no proof the task died. `submit_unknown`, the ref kept, no resubmit."""
    _engine_started(rig, 1.0)
    rig.comfy.hold = True
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "running":
            rig.comfy.restart()
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    [job] = rig.comfy.prompts
    assert (task["status"], task["attempts"], task["wait_reason"]) == (
        "submit_unknown", 0,
        "ComfyUI не знает задачу, а перезапуска движка не было — не повторяю вслепую")
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT engine_ref FROM task").fetchone()[0] == job["prompt_id"]


def test_a_network_outage_while_following_is_not_a_loss(rig):
    """The engine lives (same started_at) but the studio cannot reach it for a while: never a
    retry — the first run may still be rendering."""
    _engine_started(rig, 1.0)
    rig.comfy.hold = True
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import ExecutorError
    real = rig.executor.status
    calls = []

    def status(ref):
        calls.append(ref)
        raise ExecutorError("ComfyUI GET /history/x недоступен: timed out")
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["attempts"], len(rig.comfy.prompts), len(calls)) == (
        "submit_unknown", 0, 1, 30)
    assert task["wait_reason"] == ("ComfyUI не отвечает на проверку задачи: "
                                   "ComfyUI GET /history/x недоступен: timed out")
    rig.executor.status = real


def test_a_loss_that_cannot_be_checked_is_not_retried(rig):
    """find() could not ask the engine (and no files): «could not check» is not «not found»."""
    _engine_started(rig, 1.0)
    rig.comfy.hold = True
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import ExecutorError
    real_status = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        return {"state": "lost", "progress": None, "error": None}

    def find(spec):
        raise ExecutorError("очередь и история ComfyUI недоступны: timed out")
    rig.executor.status, rig.executor.find = status, find
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["attempts"], task["wait_reason"]) == (
        "submit_unknown", 0, "ComfyUI не знает задачу, а проверить не вышло: очередь и история "
                             "ComfyUI недоступны: timed out")
    rig.executor.status = real_status


def test_the_task_records_the_engine_version_and_the_template_it_ran(rig):
    """Revision 3 (Codex r2, finding 10): what ran is on the task — the ComfyUI version from
    /system_stats at submit and the template's sha256, side by side."""
    rig.comfy.comfyui_version = "0.42.0+stub.7"
    _, _, _, task_id = _queued_keyframe(rig)
    rig.drain()
    task = rig.task(task_id)
    assert (task["status"], task["engine_version"], task["template_sha256"]) == (
        "succeeded", "0.42.0+stub.7",
        "b16051ff44452751a5bf5f9773e1d46ad1e3a2dbd5b2358b0237e443b1f00fb2")
```

+def run_worker(outdir: Path) -> int:
+    """One worker per outdir (`flock worker.lock`, like `worker.py:105` of the old panel)."""
+    import fcntl
+    import signal
+    import threading
+

+

+                  file=sys.stderr)

Create `h3_48gb/studio/images.py`:

```python
"""The keyframe task (`task.kind = 'image'`, Krea 2, spec §7 O12, §9.1) for the worker: what the
executor gets, and how a finished picture becomes an asset."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image

from h3_48gb.studio import estimates, ids
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import TaskSpec


class VerifyError(Exception):
    """The engine said done, but the file is not what was asked for: the task fails (§8.5)."""


class ImageKind:
    kind = "image"

    def __init__(self, home: Path, *, machine: str):
        self.home = Path(home)
        self.machine = machine

    def prepare(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        """Nothing waits on a keyframe."""

    def spec(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, task: sqlite3.Row) -> TaskSpec:
        payload = json.loads(task["payload_json"])
        return TaskSpec(task_id=task["id"], template=payload["template"],
                        template_sha256=payload.get("template_sha256"),
                        values={k: payload[k] for k in ("prompt", "seed", "steps", "width",
                                                        "height")},
                        dest_dir=self.home / "projects" / task["project_id"] / "keyframes"
                        / task["id"])

    def on_running(self, conn, task) -> None:
        pass

    def on_requeued(self, conn, task) -> None:
        pass

    def on_failed(self, conn, task) -> None:
        pass

    def finish(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, task: sqlite3.Row, files: list[Path],
               spec: TaskSpec, wall_s: float) -> dict:
        """Inside the worker's transaction. One PNG of exactly the planned size."""
        pngs = [f for f in files if f.suffix.lower() == ".png"]
        if len(pngs) != 1:
            raise VerifyError(f"ждали один PNG, пришло: {', '.join(f.name for f in files)}")
        path = pngs[0]
        try:
            with Image.open(path) as image:
                image.load()
                size = image.size
        except Exception:                      # noqa: BLE001 -- Pillow raises many types
            raise VerifyError(f"кадр не читается: {path.name}") from None
        want = (spec.values["width"], spec.values["height"])
        if size != want:
            raise VerifyError(f"кадр {size[0]}×{size[1]} вместо {want[0]}×{want[1]}")
        data = path.read_bytes()
        asset_id = ids.new_id("a")
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO asset (id, kind, path, sha256, bytes, mime, origin_kind, origin_task_id, "
            "created_at) VALUES (?, 'image', ?, ?, ?, 'image/png', 'take_output', ?, ?)",
            (asset_id, str(path), hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), len(data), task["id"],
             ids.now_iso()))
        conn.execute("UPDATE task SET result_asset_id = ? WHERE id = ?", (asset_id, task["id"]))
        estimates.record(conn, executor=task["executor"], template=spec.template,
                         machine=self.machine, width=want[0], height=want[1], frames=0,
                         steps=spec.values["steps"], wall_s=wall_s)
        return {"kind": "image", "asset_id": asset_id}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/worker.py`:

```python
"""`studio-worker` (spec §4.2, §8.5): take a queued task, wait for the card, wait until the engine
is ready, submit, follow it, fetch and verify the result, record it — one GPU task at a time.
Every state is written to the task row before the next step, so a crash leaves a state the
reconcile table of slice S5 knows. `submitting` is written before the request, `engine_ref`
right after the answer and before the first poll (И9).

Task kinds plug in (`kinds`): slice S2 has `image` (Krea 2 keyframe), slice S3 adds `render`."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sqlite3
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb.studio import db, events, ids
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import ExecutorError, SubmitUncertain
from h3_48gb.studio.gpu import GpuEngineFailed
from h3_48gb.studio.images import VerifyError
from h3_48gb.studio.tasks import finish_cancelled

POLL_SECONDS = 2.0
PROBE_SECONDS = 5.0
READY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 600.0      # the dispatcher's start_timeout for ComfyUI (§8.1)
CANCEL_WAIT_SECONDS = 30.0         # §8.5: then cancelled with engine_stop=unconfirmed
MAX_STATUS_ERRORS = 30             # consecutive failed status reads before submit_unknown
MAX_ATTEMPTS = 2                   # §8.5: lost -> one automatic retry


class Worker:
    def __init__(self, db_path: Path, *, executor, gate, kinds: dict, idle=None,
                 poll_s: float = POLL_SECONDS, probe_s: float = PROBE_SECONDS,
                 ready_timeout_s: float = READY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
                 cancel_wait_s: float = CANCEL_WAIT_SECONDS, clock=time.time, sleep=time.sleep):
        self.conn = db.connect(db_path)
        self.executor = executor
        self.gate = gate
        self.kinds = kinds
        self.idle = idle
        self.poll_s = poll_s
        self.probe_s = probe_s
        self.ready_timeout_s = ready_timeout_s
        self.cancel_wait_s = cancel_wait_s
        self.clock = clock
        self.sleep = sleep
        self.last_template: str | None = None
        self._recovered = False

    # -- loop -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def run_forever(self, stop: threading.Event) -> None:
        while not stop.is_set():
            if not self.run_once():
                stop.wait(self.poll_s)

    def run_once(self) -> bool:
        """Prepare waiting work, take one queued task and run it to its end. False = idle."""
        if not self._recovered:
            self._recovered = True
            if self.recover():
                return True
        task = self._claim()
        if self.idle is not None:
            self.idle.tick(busy=task is not None)
        if task is None:
            return False
        self._execute(task)
        return True

    def _claim(self) -> sqlite3.Row | None:
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            for kind in self.kinds.values():
                kind.prepare(self.conn)
            marks = ", ".join("?" for _ in self.kinds)
            # §8.4: priority, then the model the engine holds (a switch costs 16–38 s), then age
            row = self.conn.execute(
                f"SELECT * FROM task WHERE status = 'queued' AND kind IN ({marks}) "
                "ORDER BY priority DESC, "
                "(json_extract(payload_json, '$.template') IS ?) DESC, rowid LIMIT 1",
                (*self.kinds, self.last_template)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                return None
            self.conn.execute("UPDATE task SET status = 'waiting_gpu', updated_at = ? "
                              "WHERE id = ? AND status = 'queued'", (ids.now_iso(), row["id"]))
            return self._row(row["id"])

    # -- after a restart -------------------------------------------------------------------
    def recover(self) -> int:
        """At start — the worker or its container was restarted mid-task. The rows of the §8.5
        reconcile table that need no guessing: a task waiting for the card or the engine goes
        back to the queue; one cut off while submitting becomes `submit_unknown` (never a blind
        resubmit, Codex-4); one the engine had accepted (`engine_ref` written, И9) is followed
        on from where it was. `find`/`restarted_since` for the rest is slice S5. Returns how
        many tasks were resumed."""
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            for row in self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM task WHERE status IN "
                                         "('waiting_gpu', 'waiting_ready')").fetchall():
                self._update(row["id"], status="queued", wait_reason=None)
            for row in self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM task WHERE status = 'submitting'"
                                         ).fetchall():
                self._update(row["id"], status="submit_unknown",
                             wait_reason="Воркер перезапущен во время постановки: не знаю, "
                                         "принял ли ComfyUI задачу")
                events.emit(self.conn, "task_submit_unknown", project_id=row["project_id"],
                            task_id=row["id"], payload={"error": "worker restarted"})
        rows = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM task WHERE status IN ('running', 'cancelling', 'downloading', "
            "'verifying') AND engine_ref IS NOT NULL ORDER BY rowid").fetchall()
        for row in rows:
            kind = self.kinds[row["kind"]]
            spec = kind.spec(self.conn, row)
            self.last_template = spec.template
            if row["status"] == "cancelling" or row["cancel_requested_at"] is not None:
                self._stop_engine(row["id"], row["engine_ref"])
                continue
            ref = self._follow(row["id"], kind, spec, row["engine_ref"])
            if ref is not None:
                self._collect(row["id"], kind, spec, ref, row["engine_submitted_at"])
        return len(rows)

    # -- one task ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _execute(self, task: sqlite3.Row) -> None:
        kind = self.kinds[task["kind"]]
        try:
            spec = kind.spec(self.conn, task)
        except (StudioError, ExecutorError, KeyError, ValueError) as err:
            self._fail(task["id"], f"входы задачи не собрались: {err}")
            return
        try:
            cancelled = self.gate.acquire(lambda reason, s: self._wait(task["id"], reason, s))
        except GpuEngineFailed as err:
            self._fail(task["id"], f"движок не поднялся: {err.reason}"
                       + (f" (лог {err.log})" if err.log else ""))
            return
        if cancelled:
            self._cancelled(task["id"], None)
            return
        try:
            self._run_on_engine(task["id"], kind, spec)
        finally:
            self.gate.done()

    def _run_on_engine(self, task_id: str, kind, spec) -> None:
        self._set(task_id, status="waiting_ready", wait_reason=None)
        deadline = self.clock() + self.ready_timeout_s
        while True:
            probe = self.executor.probe(spec)
            if probe["ready"]:
                break
            if self.clock() > deadline:
                self._fail(task_id, f"ComfyUI не готов за {self.ready_timeout_s:g} с: "
                           f"{probe['detail']}")
                return
            if self._wait(task_id, f"ждём ComfyUI: {probe['detail']}", self.probe_s):
                self._cancelled(task_id, None)
                return
        self._set(task_id, status="submitting", wait_reason=None,
                  submit_attempt_at=self.clock())
        version = self.executor.engine_version()
        try:
            ref = self.executor.submit(spec)
        except SubmitUncertain as err:
            self._event_set(task_id, "task_submit_unknown", {"error": str(err)},
                            status="submit_unknown",
                            wait_reason=f"Не знаю, принял ли ComfyUI задачу: {err}")
            return
        except ExecutorError as err:
            self._fail(task_id, str(err))
            return
        submitted = self.clock()
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            self._update(task_id, engine_ref=ref, engine_submitted_at=submitted, status="running",
                         engine_version=version, template_sha256=spec.template_sha256)
            task = self._row(task_id)
            kind.on_running(self.conn, task)
            events.emit(self.conn, "task_running", project_id=task["project_id"],
                        task_id=task_id, payload={"engine_ref": ref})
        self.last_template = spec.template
        ref = self._follow(task_id, kind, spec, ref)
        if ref is None:
            return
        self._collect(task_id, kind, spec, ref, submitted)

    def _collect(self, task_id: str, kind, spec, ref: str, submitted: float) -> None:
        """§8.5 downloading → verifying → succeeded (or failed / cancelled)."""
        self._set(task_id, status="downloading")
        try:
            files = self.executor.result(ref, spec.dest_dir)
        except ExecutorError as err:
            self._fail(task_id, str(err))
            return
        self._set(task_id, status="verifying")
        try:
            with db.tx(self.conn):
                task = self._row(task_id)
                if task["cancel_requested_at"] is not None:      # logical cancel: never attach
                    finish_cancelled(self.conn, task_id, project_id=task["project_id"],
                                     error=None, engine_stop="finished")
                    return
                result = kind.finish(self.conn, task, files, spec, self.clock() - submitted)
                self._update(task_id, status="succeeded", wait_reason=None)
                events.emit(self.conn, "task_succeeded", project_id=task["project_id"],
                            task_id=task_id, payload=result)
        except VerifyError as err:
            self._fail(task_id, f"результат не прошёл проверку: {err}")

    def _follow(self, task_id: str, kind, spec, ref: str) -> str | None:
        """Poll until done; returns the ref to fetch from, or None if the task ended otherwise."""
        errors = 0
        while True:
            task = self._row(task_id)
            if task["cancel_requested_at"] is not None:
                self._stop_engine(task_id, ref)
                return None
            try:
                status = self.executor.status(ref)
                errors = 0
            except ExecutorError as err:
                errors += 1
                if errors < MAX_STATUS_ERRORS:
                    self.sleep(self.poll_s)
                    continue
                # «could not check» is not «gone» (§8.5, Codex-4): keep the ref, ask a human
                self._unknown(task_id, f"ComfyUI не отвечает на проверку задачи: {err}")
                return None
            if status["progress"] is not None:
                step, total = status["progress"]
                if (task["progress_step"], task["progress_total"]) != (step, total):
                    self._event_set(task_id, "task_progress", {"step": step, "total": total},
                                    progress_step=step, progress_total=total)
            state = status["state"]
            if state == "done":
                return ref
            if state == "failed":
                self._fail(task_id, status["error"] or "ошибка исполнителя")
                return None
            if state == "cancelled":
                self._cancelled(task_id, "ComfyUI прервал задачу не по нашей просьбе",
                                engine_stop="confirmed")
                return None
            if state == "lost":
                try:
                    found = self.executor.find(spec) \
                        if self.executor.capabilities["find"] == "exact" else None
                except ExecutorError as err:
                    self._unknown(task_id, f"ComfyUI не знает задачу, а проверить не вышло: {err}")
                    return None
                if found is not None and found != ref:
                    ref = found
                    self._set(task_id, engine_ref=found)
                    continue
                if found is None:
                    self._lost(task_id, kind)
                    return None
            self.sleep(self.poll_s)

    def _stop_engine(self, task_id: str, ref: str) -> None:
        """§8.5 actual cancel: ask the engine, wait up to 30 s for it to confirm."""
        self._set(task_id, status="cancelling")
        try:
            self.executor.cancel(ref)
        except ExecutorError:
            pass
        stop = "unconfirmed"
        for _ in range(max(1, math.ceil(self.cancel_wait_s / self.poll_s))):
            try:
                state = self.executor.status(ref)["state"]
            except ExecutorError:
                state = None
            if state in ("cancelled", "lost"):
                stop = "confirmed"
                break
            if state in ("done", "failed"):
                stop = "finished"
                break
            self.sleep(self.poll_s)
        self._cancelled(task_id, None, engine_stop=stop)

    # -- endings ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _lost(self, task_id: str, kind) -> None:
        """Nothing in the queue, the history or the files. A retry only with proof that the
        engine was restarted after we submitted (§8.5: the task cannot have survived a process
        that is gone); without proof — `submit_unknown`, the ref kept, a human decides (S5)."""
        task = self._row(task_id)
        restarted = self.executor.restarted_since(task["engine_submitted_at"] or 0.0) \
            if self.executor.capabilities["restart_evidence"] else None
        if restarted is not True:
            self._unknown(task_id, "ComfyUI не знает задачу, а перезапуска движка не было — "
                                   "не повторяю вслепую")
            return
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            task = self._row(task_id)
            if task["attempts"] + 1 < MAX_ATTEMPTS:
                self._update(task_id, status="queued", attempts=task["attempts"] + 1,
                             engine_ref=None, submit_attempt_at=None, engine_submitted_at=None,
                             progress_step=None, progress_total=None, wait_reason=None)
                kind.on_requeued(self.conn, task)
                events.emit(self.conn, "task_requeued", project_id=task["project_id"],
                            task_id=task_id, payload={"reason": "lost"})
                return
        self._fail(task_id, "ComfyUI потерял задачу дважды")

    def _unknown(self, task_id: str, reason: str) -> None:
        """`submit_unknown` with the ref kept (resolution: O27, slice S5)."""
        self._event_set(task_id, "task_submit_unknown", {"error": reason},
                        status="submit_unknown", wait_reason=reason)

    def _fail(self, task_id: str, error: str) -> None:
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            self._update(task_id, status="failed", error=error, wait_reason=None)
            task = self._row(task_id)
            self.kinds[task["kind"]].on_failed(self.conn, task)
            events.emit(self.conn, "task_failed", project_id=task["project_id"],
                        task_id=task_id, payload={"error": error})

    def _cancelled(self, task_id: str, error: str | None, engine_stop: str | None = None):
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            task = self._row(task_id)
            finish_cancelled(self.conn, task_id, project_id=task["project_id"], error=error,
                             engine_stop=engine_stop)

    # -- waiting ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _wait(self, task_id: str, reason: str, seconds: float) -> bool:
        """Show why the task waits, then sleep `seconds` in slices; True if cancelled."""
        task = self._row(task_id)
        if task["wait_reason"] != reason:
            self._event_set(task_id, "task_waiting", {"wait_reason": reason}, wait_reason=reason)
        piece = min(1.0, seconds) or seconds
        for _ in range(max(1, math.ceil(seconds / piece))):
            if self._row(task_id)["cancel_requested_at"] is not None:
                return True
            self.sleep(piece)
        return self._row(task_id)["cancel_requested_at"] is not None

    # -- rows -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _row(self, task_id: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM task WHERE id = ?", (task_id,)).fetchone()

    def _update(self, task_id: str, **fields) -> None:
        sets = ", ".join(f"{name} = ?" for name in fields)
        self.conn.execute(f"UPDATE task SET {sets}, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                          (*fields.values(), ids.now_iso(), task_id))

    def _set(self, task_id: str, **fields) -> None:
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            self._update(task_id, **fields)

    def _event_set(self, task_id: str, kind: str, payload: dict, **fields) -> None:
        with db.tx(self.conn):
            self._update(task_id, **fields)
            task = self._row(task_id)
            events.emit(self.conn, kind, project_id=task["project_id"], task_id=task_id,
                        payload=payload)


# -- wiring --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

STUDIO_WORKER = "studio-worker"


def build_worker(outdir: Path, **overrides) -> Worker:
    """The real worker from the settings (`settings.DEFAULTS` + rows of `setting`)."""
    from h3_48gb.engines.dispatcher_client import DispatcherClient
    from h3_48gb.studio import config, settings
    from h3_48gb.studio.executors.comfy import ComfyExecutor
    from h3_48gb.studio.gpu import DispatcherGate, IdleRelease, LocalGate
    from h3_48gb.studio.images import ImageKind

    home = config.studio_dir(outdir)
    home.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    db_path = home / "studio.sqlite"
    boot = db.connect(db_path)
    try:
        db.migrate(boot)
        conf = settings.all_settings(boot)
    finally:
        boot.close()
    scope = conf["comfy.scope"]
    dispatcher = None
    if scope.startswith("dispatcher:"):
        dispatcher = DispatcherClient(conf["dispatcher.url"], client=STUDIO_WORKER)
        gate = DispatcherGate(dispatcher, scope.split(":", 1)[1])
        idle = IdleRelease(dispatcher, gate)
    elif scope == "local-mac":
        gate, idle = LocalGate(home / "gpu-mac.lock"), None
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"comfy.scope {scope!r}: dispatcher:<engine> или local-mac")
    executor = ComfyExecutor(url=conf["comfy.url"], output_dir=Path(conf["comfy.output_dir"]),
                             scope=scope, dispatcher=dispatcher,
                             engine=scope.split(":", 1)[1] if dispatcher else "local")
    kinds = {"image": ImageKind(home, machine=conf["machine"])}
    return Worker(db_path, executor=executor, gate=gate, kinds=kinds, idle=idle, **overrides)
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { taskText } from "@/components/TaskLine";
import { stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { scene, snapshot } from "@/test/snapshot";
import { imagePlan, task } from "@/test/task";
import { FrameStep } from "./FrameStep";

const fromFrame = scene({ id: "s_1", mode: "from_frame", edit_version: 4 });

describe("FrameStep", () => {
  it("plans, shows time and the model switch, confirms with a fresh Idempotency-Key", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan": { body: imagePlan() },
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/image": { status: 201, body: { task_id: "k_1", status: "queued" } },
    });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    render(<FrameStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [fromFrame] })} onChanged={onChanged} newKey={() => "key-1"} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Сделать кадр" }));
    const region = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "План кадра" });
    expect(region.querySelector("[aria-label='План']")!.textContent).toBe(
      "Время ≈ 23 с · смена модели h3-fl2va → krea2-t2i: +17 с");
    expect(screen.getByText("1344×768 · Krea 2")).toBeTruthy();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Подтвердить" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(onChanged).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(calls.map((c) => [c.key, c.body])).toEqual([
      ["POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan", { kind: "image", scene_id: "s_1" }],
      ["POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/image", { plan_id: "pl_1" }],
    ]);
    expect(calls[1].headers).toEqual({ "Content-Type": "application/json", "Idempotency-Key": "key-1" });
  });

  it("shows why the task waits and frees the card from Qwen", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({ "POST /api/studio/gpu/qwen/unload": { body: { ok: true } } });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    const waiting = task({ status: "waiting_gpu", wait_reason: "ждём GPU: Qwen держит карту" });
    render(<FrameStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [fromFrame], tasks: [waiting] })} onChanged={onChanged} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toBe("ждём GPU: Qwen держит карту");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Освободить" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(onChanged).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(calls.map((c) => c.key)).toEqual(["POST /api/studio/gpu/qwen/unload"]);
  });

  it("cancels a running task", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({ "POST /api/studio/tasks/k_1/cancel": { body: task({ status: "cancelled" }) } });
    const running = task({ status: "running", progress_step: 3, progress_total: 8 });
    render(<FrameStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [fromFrame], tasks: [running] })} onChanged={() => {}} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toBe("Шаг 3 из 8");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Отменить" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(calls.length).toBe(1));
    expect(calls[0]).toEqual({ key: "POST /api/studio/tasks/k_1/cancel", body: { reason: "владелец" } });
  });

  it("takes the finished frame into scene 1 with the scene's edit_version", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({ "PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1": { body: { scene: fromFrame, cascade: [], lint: [] } } });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    const done = task({ status: "succeeded", result_asset_id: "a_9", result_url: "/api/studio/assets/a_9/file" });
    render(<FrameStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [fromFrame], tasks: [done] })} onChanged={onChanged} />);
    expect((screen.getByAltText("Новый кадр") as HTMLImageElement).getAttribute("src")).toBe("/api/studio/assets/a_9/file");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Взять в сцену 1" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(onChanged).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(calls.map((c) => [c.key, c.body])).toEqual([
      ["PATCH /api/studio/scenes/s_1", { expected_edit_version: 4, source_frame: { kind: "asset", asset_id: "a_9" } }],
    ]);
  });

  it("an attached frame is shown and not offered again", () => {
    stubFetch({});
    const attached = scene({ id: "s_1", mode: "from_frame", source_frame: { kind: "asset", scene_id: null, asset_id: "a_9" } });
    const done = task({ status: "succeeded", result_asset_id: "a_9", result_url: "/api/studio/assets/a_9/file" });
    render(<FrameStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [attached], tasks: [done] })} onChanged={() => {}} />);
    expect((screen.getByAltText("Кадр сцены 1") as HTMLImageElement).getAttribute("src")).toBe("/api/studio/assets/a_9/file");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Взять в сцену 1" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Сделать другой кадр" })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a refs scene needs no frame", () => {
    stubFetch({});
    render(<FrameStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [scene({ mode: "refs" })] })} onChanged={() => {}} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Сцена 1 снимается по фото героя (режим «Референсы») — кадр не нужен.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Сделать кадр" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("names every task state in words", () => {
    expect([
      taskText(task({ status: "queued" })), taskText(task({ status: "blocked" })),
      taskText(task({ status: "waiting_ready", wait_reason: "ждём ComfyUI: в ComfyUI нет узлов: X" })),
      taskText(task({ status: "running" })), taskText(task({ status: "failed", error: "ComfyUI: OOM" })),
      taskText(task({ status: "submit_unknown", wait_reason: null })), taskText(task({ status: "cancelled" })),
    ]).toEqual(["В очереди", "Ждёт кадр предыдущей сцены", "ждём ComfyUI: в ComfyUI нет узлов: X", "Идёт",
                "Ошибка: ComfyUI: OOM", "Не знаю, принял ли ComfyUI задачу", "Отменено"]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type { Plan } from "@/api/types";

export const minutes = (s: number) => (s < 90 ? `≈ ${Math.round(s)} с` : `≈ ${Math.round(s / 60)} мин`);

/** The dry plan before a GPU task (spec §4.4 steps 4–5): time, model switches, what blocks. */
export function PlanSummary({ plan }: { plan: Plan }) {
  return (
    <div className="text-[13px] text-ink2" aria-label="План">
      <span>{`Время ${minutes(plan.total_s)}`}</span>
      {plan.checkpoint_switches.map((s, i) => (
        <span key={i}>{` · смена модели ${s.from ?? "—"} → ${s.to}: +${Math.round(s.seconds)} с`}</span>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/components/TaskLine.tsx`:

```tsx
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Task } from "@/api/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useState } from "react";

export const TERMINAL = new Set(["succeeded", "failed", "cancelled"]);

/** What the owner reads about a task (spec §4.4 step 5): the wait with its reason, the step
 *  counter, the error — never a bare status code. */
export function taskText(t: Task): string {
  switch (t.status) {
    case "queued": return "В очереди";
    case "blocked": return "Ждёт кадр предыдущей сцены";
    case "waiting_gpu": return t.wait_reason ?? "Ждём карту";
    case "waiting_ready": return t.wait_reason ?? "Готовим ComfyUI";
    case "submitting": return "Отправляем в ComfyUI";
    case "submit_unknown": return t.wait_reason ?? "Не знаю, принял ли ComfyUI задачу";
    case "running":
      return t.progress_step !== null && t.progress_total !== null
        ? `Шаг ${t.progress_step} из ${t.progress_total}` : "Идёт";
    case "cancelling": return "Останавливаем";
    case "downloading":
    case "verifying": return "Проверяем результат";
    case "succeeded": return "Готово";
    case "failed": return `Ошибка: ${t.error ?? "без текста"}`;
    case "cancelled": return "Отменено";
  }
}

/** One task: its text, «Освободить» when Qwen holds the card (O23), «Отменить» (O14). */
export function TaskLine({ task, onChanged }: { task: Task; onChanged: () => void }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const run = async (method: string, path: string, body?: unknown) => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await api(method, path, body);
      onChanged();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил");
    }
  };
  const qwen = task.status === "waiting_gpu" && (task.wait_reason ?? "").includes("Qwen");
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[13px]" aria-label="Задача">
      <span role="status" className={task.status === "failed" ? "text-danger" : "text-ink2"}>{taskText(task)}</span>
      {qwen && <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => run("POST", "/gpu/qwen/unload")}>Освободить</Button>}
      {!TERMINAL.has(task.status) && !task.cancel_requested && (
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => run("POST", `/tasks/${task.id}/cancel`, { reason: "владелец" })}>Отменить</Button>
      )}
      {error && <span role="alert" className="text-danger">{error}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/FrameStep.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ImageIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Plan, SceneResult, Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { PlanSummary } from "@/components/PlanSummary";
import { TaskLine } from "@/components/TaskLine";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useIsPhone, useMobileAction } from "@/mobileAction";

/** Step 4 «Первый кадр» (spec §4.4): scene 1 in `from_frame` gets a Krea 2 keyframe —
 *  plan (O10) → «Подтвердить» (O12, a fresh Idempotency-Key per press) → progress (O13 via the
 *  snapshot and SSE) → «Взять в сцену 1» (O8 `source_frame{kind:"asset"}`). */
export function FrameStep({ snap, onChanged, newKey = () => crypto.randomUUID() }: {
  snap: Snapshot;
  onChanged: () => void;
  newKey?: () => string;
}) {
  const pid = snap.project.id;
  const scene = snap.scenes[0];
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState<Plan | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const phone = useIsPhone();
  const task = scene ? [...snap.tasks].reverse().find((t) => t.kind === "image") ?? null : null;
  const attached = scene?.source_frame.kind === "asset" ? scene.source_frame.asset_id : null;
  const result = task?.status === "succeeded" ? task.result_asset_id : null;
  const canTake = result !== null && result !== attached;

  async function run(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  const makePlan = () => run(async () => {
    setPlan(await api<Plan>("POST", `/projects/${pid}/plan`, { kind: "image", scene_id: scene!.id }));
  });
  const confirm = () => run(async () => {
    await api("POST", `/projects/${pid}/image`, { plan_id: plan!.plan_id }, { "Idempotency-Key": newKey() });
    setPlan(null);
    onChanged();
  });
  const take = () => run(async () => {
    await api<SceneResult>("PATCH", `/scenes/${scene!.id}`, {
      expected_edit_version: scene!.edit_version, source_frame: { kind: "asset", asset_id: result },
    });
    onChanged();
  });

  const needed = scene !== undefined && scene.mode === "from_frame";
  useMobileAction("frame", !needed ? null
    : plan ? { label: "Подтвердить", onClick: confirm, disabled: busy, priority: 1 }
    : canTake ? { label: "Взять в сцену 1", onClick: take, disabled: busy, priority: 1 }
    : null);

  if (!scene) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="frame-h" className="mt-10">
      <div className="eyebrow">Шаг 4</div>
      <h2 id="frame-h" className="h-display mt-1 text-[24px]">Первый кадр</h2>
      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 rounded-lg border border-line p-5">
        {!needed ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink2">Сцена 1 снимается по фото героя (режим «Референсы») — кадр не нужен.</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            {attached && (
              <figure className="grid gap-1">
                <img src={`/api/studio/assets/${attached}/file`} alt="Кадр сцены 1" className="aspect-video w-full max-w-[640px] rounded-md border border-line object-cover" />
                <figcaption className="text-[12px] text-ink3">Кадр сцены 1</figcaption>
              </figure>
            )}
            {!plan && (
              <Button variant={attached ? "outline" : "default"} size="lg" onClick={makePlan} disabled={busy} className="justify-self-start">
                <ImageIcon />
                {attached ? "Сделать другой кадр" : "Сделать кадр"}
              </Button>
            )}
            {plan && (
              <div role="region" aria-label="План кадра" className="grid gap-2 rounded-md border border-cobalt-line bg-cobalt-soft p-4">
                <p className="text-[13px]">{plan.items[0].prompt}</p>
                <p className="font-mono text-[12px] text-ink3">{`${plan.items[0].width}×${plan.items[0].height} · Krea 2`}</p>
                <PlanSummary plan={plan} />
                {!phone && (
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <Button size="lg" onClick={confirm} disabled={busy}>Подтвердить</Button>
                    <Button size="lg" variant="outline" onClick={() => setPlan(null)} disabled={busy}>Отмена</Button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
            {task && !(task.status === "succeeded" && task.result_asset_id === attached) && (
              <TaskLine task={task} onChanged={onChanged} />
            )}
            {canTake && (
              <figure className="grid gap-2">
                <img src={task!.result_url!} alt="Новый кадр" className="aspect-video w-full max-w-[640px] rounded-md border border-line object-cover" />
                {!phone && <Button size="lg" onClick={take} disabled={busy} className="justify-self-start">Взять в сцену 1</Button>}
              </figure>
            )}
          </>
        )}
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-[13px] text-danger">{error}</p>}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```
