Create `tests/test_studio_dispatcher_rollback.py`:

```python
"""Rolling the dispatcher back (plan S2–S3 revisions 2–3, Codex blocker 1) on stubs: `docker` and
`sudo` on PATH, and a dispatcher stub that behaves like the NEW version until the unit restart
and like the OLD one after it — the old one fails every /status and /acquire with KeyError while
state.json still holds `comfy-studio` (reproduced on the real old dispatcher.py in scratch).
The studio's engine is a real child process listening on a real port: the rollback must see it
gone (revision 3), not just its record."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import socket
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SCRIPT = ROOT / "scripts" / "studio_dispatcher_rollback.py"

#: A stand-in for the studio's ComfyUI: a process that listens on a port until it is killed.
ENGINE = ("import socket, sys, time\n"
          "s = socket.socket(); s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)\n"
          "s.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0)); s.listen(); print(s.getsockname()[1], flush=True)\n"
          "time.sleep(600)\n")


class TwoDispatchers:
    def __init__(self, tmp: Path, *, kill_engine: bool = True, drop_record: bool = True,
                 acquire_state: str = "starting", panel_holds: str | None = None):
        self.engine = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-c", ENGINE], stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                                       text=True)
        self.port = int(self.engine.stdout.readline())
        self.state = tmp / "state.json"
        engines = {"comfy-studio": {"pid": self.engine.pid, "pgid": self.engine.pid,
                                    "owner": "studio-worker"}}
        if panel_holds:
            engines[panel_holds] = {"pid": 8, "pgid": 8, "owner": "panel-worker"}
        self.state.write_text(json.dumps({"engines": engines}))
        self.restarted = tmp / "restarted"          # written by the `sudo systemctl restart` stub
        self.kill_engine, self.drop_record = kill_engine, drop_record
        self.acquire_state = acquire_state
        self.calls: list[str] = []
        self.httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), self._handler())
        self.url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.httpd.server_address[1]}"
        threading.Thread(target=self.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()

    def engines(self) -> dict:
        return json.loads(self.state.read_text())["engines"]

    def close(self):
        self.httpd.shutdown()
        self.httpd.server_close()
        if self.engine.poll() is None:
            self.engine.kill()
        self.engine.wait()

    def _handler(fake):  # noqa: N805
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *a):
                pass

            def _send(self, status, body):
                raw = json.dumps(body).encode()
                self.send_response(status)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(raw)))
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(raw)

            def _old_and_broken(self) -> bool:
                return fake.restarted.exists() and "comfy-studio" in fake.engines()

            def do_GET(self):
                version = "old" if fake.restarted.exists() else "new"
                fake.calls.append(f"{version} GET {self.path}")
                if self._old_and_broken():
                    return self._send(500, {"ok": False, "error": {
                        "code": "internal", "message": "'comfy-studio'"}})
                self._send(200, {"ok": True, "own": {k: {"owner": v["owner"]}
                                                     for k, v in fake.engines().items()}})

            def do_POST(self):
                body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                version = "old" if fake.restarted.exists() else "new"
                fake.calls.append(f"{version} POST {self.path} {body.get('engine', '')}"
                                  f"{' ' if body.get('engine') else ''}{body['client']}")
                if self._old_and_broken():
                    return self._send(500, {"ok": False, "error": {
                        "code": "internal", "message": "'comfy-studio'"}})
                if self.path == "/release":
                    engines = fake.engines()
                    mine = [k for k, v in engines.items() if v["owner"] == body["client"]]
                    if "comfy-studio" in mine and fake.kill_engine:
                        fake.engine.kill()
                        fake.engine.wait()
                    if fake.drop_record:
                        fake.state.write_text(json.dumps({"engines": {
                            k: v for k, v in engines.items() if k not in mine}}))
                    return self._send(200, {"ok": True, "stopped": mine})
                return self._send(200, {"ok": True, "state": fake.acquire_state,
                                        "engine": body["engine"],
                                        **({"reason": "GPU занята: qwen"}
                                           if fake.acquire_state == "wait" else {})})
        return Handler


def _stubs(tmp: Path, fake: TwoDispatchers) -> dict:
    bin_dir = tmp / "bin"
    bin_dir.mkdir()
    log = tmp / "commands.log"
    for name, extra in (("docker", ""), ("sudo", f'''
case "$*" in *"systemctl restart"*) touch {fake.restarted} ;; esac''')):
        path = bin_dir / name
        path.write_text(f"#!/bin/sh\necho \"{name} $*\" >> {log}{extra}\nexit 0\n")
        path.chmod(path.stat().st_mode | stat.S_IEXEC)
    return {"PATH": f"{bin_dir}:{os.environ['PATH']}", "LOG": str(log)}


def _run(fake: TwoDispatchers, env: dict, script: Path = SCRIPT):
    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(script), "--dispatcher", fake.url, "--state",
                           str(fake.state), "--compose", "compose.studio.yaml", "--override",
                           "/etc/x/override.conf", "--wait", "2", "--studio-port",
                           str(fake.port)],
                          env={**os.environ, "PATH": env["PATH"]}, capture_output=True, text=True)


@pytest.fixture
def rollback(tmp_path):
    made = []

    def make(**kwargs):
        fake = TwoDispatchers(tmp_path, **kwargs)
        made.append(fake)
        return fake, _stubs(tmp_path, fake)
    yield make
    for fake in made:
        fake.close()


def test_the_rollback_releases_the_studio_engine_before_the_old_dispatcher_returns(rollback):
    fake, env = rollback()
    done = _run(fake, env)
    assert (done.returncode, done.stderr) == (0, "")
    assert done.stdout == ("ok: worker stopped\nok: released ['comfy-studio']\n"
                           "ok: comfy-studio gone\nok: unit restored\n"
                           "ok: old dispatcher grants ltx to the panel (starting)\n")
    assert Path(env["LOG"]).read_text().splitlines() == [
        "docker compose -f compose.studio.yaml stop studio-worker",
        "sudo rm -f /etc/x/override.conf", "sudo systemctl daemon-reload",
        "sudo systemctl restart h3-gpu-dispatcher"]
    assert fake.calls == ["new POST /release studio-worker", "new GET /status",
                          "old GET /status", "old POST /acquire ltx panel-worker",
                          "old POST /release panel-worker"]
    assert fake.engines() == {}
    assert fake.engine.poll() is not None              # the process itself is gone


def test_the_panels_own_engine_is_asked_for_and_left_running(rollback):
    fake, env = rollback(acquire_state="ready", panel_holds="h3")
    done = _run(fake, env)
    assert (done.returncode, done.stdout.splitlines()[-1]) == (
        0, "ok: old dispatcher grants h3 to the panel (ready)")
    assert fake.calls[-1] == "old POST /acquire h3 panel-worker"     # no release after it


def test_the_old_order_breaks_the_old_dispatcher_and_the_script_says_so(rollback, tmp_path):
    """The order Codex flagged — restore the unit first: the old dispatcher answers 500."""
    old_order = tmp_path / "old_order.py"
    text = SCRIPT.read_text()
    a = text.index('    status, body = http(base, "POST", "/release", {"client": STUDIO_CLIENT')
    b = text.index('    run(["sudo", "rm", "-f", override])')
    c = text.index('    done.append("unit restored")')
    old_order.write_text(text[:a] + text[b:c] + text[a:b] + text[c:])
    fake, env = rollback()
    done = _run(fake, env, old_order)
    assert done.returncode == 1
    assert done.stderr.startswith("ОТКАТ ОСТАНОВЛЕН: новый диспетчер: /release studio-worker "
                                  "ответил 500")


def test_a_release_that_leaves_the_record_stops_before_touching_the_unit(rollback):
    fake, env = rollback(drop_record=False)
    done = _run(fake, env)
    assert done.returncode == 1
    assert done.stderr == "ОТКАТ ОСТАНОВЛЕН: comfy-studio не ушёл за 2 с: запись в /status\n"
    assert Path(env["LOG"]).read_text().splitlines() == [
        "docker compose -f compose.studio.yaml stop studio-worker"]


def test_a_record_gone_while_the_process_lives_stops_before_touching_the_unit(rollback):
    """Revision 3 (Codex r2, finding 1): the record can vanish while the engine's group lives on
    and keeps the card — the rollback checks the process and the port, not just JSON."""
    fake, env = rollback(kill_engine=False)
    done = _run(fake, env)
    assert done.returncode == 1
    assert done.stderr == (f"ОТКАТ ОСТАНОВЛЕН: comfy-studio не ушёл за 2 с: процесс pid "
                           f"{fake.engine.pid}\n")
    assert Path(env["LOG"]).read_text().splitlines() == [
        "docker compose -f compose.studio.yaml stop studio-worker"]


def test_a_port_still_taken_stops_before_touching_the_unit(rollback, tmp_path):
    fake, env = rollback()
    other = socket.socket()
    other.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    other.listen()
    fake.port = other.getsockname()[1]           # someone else still answers on the port
    try:
        done = _run(fake, env)
    finally:
        other.close()
    assert (done.returncode, done.stderr) == (
        1, f"ОТКАТ ОСТАНОВЛЕН: comfy-studio не ушёл за 2 с: порт {fake.port} занят\n")


def test_an_old_dispatcher_that_only_says_wait_fails_the_rollback(rollback):
    """Revision 3: `wait` does not prove the panel can get the card back — only a grant does."""
    fake, env = rollback(acquire_state="wait")
    done = _run(fake, env)
    assert done.returncode == 1
    assert done.stderr == ("ОТКАТ ОСТАНОВЛЕН: старый диспетчер не выдал карту панели: /acquire "
                           "ltx ответил 200: {'ok': True, 'state': 'wait', 'engine': 'ltx', "
                           "'reason': 'GPU занята: qwen'}\n")
```

Create `scripts/studio_dispatcher_rollback.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Roll the GPU dispatcher back from the studio's version to the old panel's one (plan S2–S3,
revision 2, Codex blocker 1). Order matters: the old dispatcher has no EngineSpec `comfy-studio`
and answers every /status and /acquire with KeyError while state.json still holds that engine
(`KillMode=process` keeps the engine alive across the restart, state.json keeps its record).

1. stop the studio worker — no new submit;
2. through the NEW dispatcher: release the client `studio-worker` — stops the studio's ComfyUI
   (and with it any running studio prompt) and drops its record from state.json;
3. wait until neither /status nor state.json knows `comfy-studio`, its recorded process is
   gone and nothing listens on its port (8189) — a record can vanish while the group lives on;
4. remove the systemd override and restart the unit (the old panel's dispatcher.py, untouched);
5. check the old dispatcher: /status answers JSON, and /acquire as the panel's client is a GRANT
   (`ready` / `starting`); `wait` or anything else fails the rollback. The engine asked for is the
   one the panel already holds (then `ready`, nothing moves), or `ltx` on a free card (then
   `starting`, released again right away).

Exit 0 — rolled back and checked; 1 — a step failed (the message says which; nothing after it ran).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

STUDIO_CLIENT = "studio-worker"
PANEL_CLIENT = "panel-worker"
GRANT = ("ready", "starting")


class Failed(Exception):
    pass


def http(base: str, method: str, path: str, body: dict | None = None, timeout: float = 130.0):
    data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
    request = urllib.request.Request(base + path, data=data, method=method,
                                     headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"} if data else {})
    opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    try:
        with opener.open(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
            return response.status, json.loads(response.read())
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        return exc.code, {"raw": exc.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")[:300]}
    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        return None, {"raw": str(exc)}


def run(cmd: list[str]) -> None:
    done = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    if done.returncode != 0:
        raise Failed(f"{' '.join(cmd)}: код {done.returncode}: {done.stderr.strip()[:300]}")


def _engines(state: Path) -> dict:
    try:
        return json.loads(state.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("engines", {})
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return {}


def pid_alive(pid: int | None) -> bool:
    if not pid:
        return False
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        return False
    except PermissionError:
        return True
    return True


def port_open(port: int) -> bool:
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.settimeout(1.0)
        return sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", port)) == 0


def studio_engine_gone(base: str, state: Path, pid: int | None, port: int) -> str | None:
    """None when the studio's engine is gone everywhere; else what still holds it."""
    status, body = http(base, "GET", "/status", timeout=10)
    if status != 200:
        raise Failed(f"новый диспетчер: /status ответил {status}: {body}")
    if "comfy-studio" in (body.get("own") or {}):
        return "запись в /status"
    if "comfy-studio" in _engines(state):
        return f"запись в {state}"
    if pid_alive(pid):
        return f"процесс pid {pid}"
    if port_open(port):
        return f"порт {port} занят"
    return None


def rollback(*, base: str, state: Path, compose: str, override: str, unit: str,
             wait_s: float, studio_port: int = 8189, sleep=time.sleep) -> list[str]:
    done = []
    run(["docker", "compose", "-f", compose, "stop", "studio-worker"])
    done.append("worker stopped")
    pid = (_engines(state).get("comfy-studio") or {}).get("pid")
    status, body = http(base, "POST", "/release", {"client": STUDIO_CLIENT, "all": False})
    if status != 200:
        raise Failed(f"новый диспетчер: /release {STUDIO_CLIENT} ответил {status}: {body}")
    done.append(f"released {body.get('stopped')}")
    deadline = time.monotonic() + wait_s
    while (left := studio_engine_gone(base, state, pid, studio_port)) is not None:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            raise Failed(f"comfy-studio не ушёл за {wait_s:g} с: {left}")
        sleep(1.0)
    done.append("comfy-studio gone")
    run(["sudo", "rm", "-f", override])
    run(["sudo", "systemctl", "daemon-reload"])
    run(["sudo", "systemctl", "restart", unit])
    done.append("unit restored")
    deadline = time.monotonic() + wait_s
    while True:
        status, body = http(base, "GET", "/status", timeout=10)
        if status == 200 and isinstance(body.get("own"), dict):
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            raise Failed(f"старый диспетчер: /status ответил {status}: {body}")
        sleep(1.0)
    held = [name for name, rec in (body.get("own") or {}).items()
            if rec.get("owner") == PANEL_CLIENT]
    engine = held[0] if held else "ltx"
    status, body = http(base, "POST", "/acquire", {"engine": engine, "client": PANEL_CLIENT})
    if status != 200 or body.get("state") not in GRANT:
        raise Failed(f"старый диспетчер не выдал карту панели: /acquire {engine} ответил "
                     f"{status}: {body}")
    if not held:                  # we started it on a free card: give the card back
        http(base, "POST", "/release", {"client": PANEL_CLIENT, "all": False})
    done.append(f"old dispatcher grants {engine} to the panel ({body.get('state')})")
    return done


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--dispatcher", default="http://127.0.0.1:8790")
    parser.add_argument("--state", type=Path,
                        default=Path.home() / ".local/state/h3-gpu-dispatcher/state.json")
    parser.add_argument("--compose", default="compose.studio.yaml")
    parser.add_argument("--override",
                        default="/etc/systemd/system/h3-gpu-dispatcher.service.d/override.conf")
    parser.add_argument("--unit", default="h3-gpu-dispatcher")
    parser.add_argument("--wait", type=float, default=150.0)
    parser.add_argument("--studio-port", type=int, default=8189)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        for step in rollback(base=args.dispatcher.rstrip("/"), state=args.state,
                             compose=args.compose, override=args.override, unit=args.unit,
                             wait_s=args.wait, studio_port=args.studio_port):
            print(f"ok: {step}")
    except Failed as err:
        print(f"ОТКАТ ОСТАНОВЛЕН: {err}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

```python
"""`prompt_final` (spec §9.4) and the pure numbering it uses (`library.number_references`,
spec §3.1). Expected texts are literals."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from h3_48gb import library
from h3_48gb.studio.director.prompt_final import PromptError, build, three_fields


def test_three_fields_in_the_probed_format():
    assert three_fields(style_block="", action_text="She walks  to the lake.",
                        performance_text="Her gaze drops, then she smiles.",
                        sound_text="Bare heels on wet planks.", music_text="none") == (
        "integrated_multimodal_description: Live-action, natural colors. She walks to the lake. "
        "Her gaze drops, then she smiles.\n\n"
        "overall_soundscape: Bare heels on wet planks.\n\n"
        "non_diegetic_music: N/A")


def test_a_from_frame_scene_has_no_definitions_and_no_pictures():
    final = build(mode="from_frame", style_block="Soft film look.", action_text="She turns.",
                  performance_text="Surprise.", sound_text="A twig snaps.", music_text="Piano.",
                  refs=[{"tag": "anna", "description_en": "a woman", "image_asset_ids": ["a_1"]}])
    assert final.prompt == ("integrated_multimodal_description: Soft film look. She turns. "
                            "Surprise.\n\noverall_soundscape: A twig snaps.\n\n"
                            "non_diegetic_music: Piano.")
    assert final.ref_asset_ids == ()


def test_a_refs_scene_numbers_pictures_in_ref_order():
    final = build(mode="refs", style_block="", action_text="Two women talk.",
                  performance_text="", sound_text="Wind.", music_text="",
                  refs=[{"tag": "anna", "description_en": "a young woman.",
                         "image_asset_ids": ["a_1", "a_2"]},
                        {"tag": "no_photo", "description_en": "a man", "image_asset_ids": []},
                        {"tag": "vera", "description_en": "an old woman",
                         "image_asset_ids": ["a_3"]}])
    assert final.prompt == (
        "subject_definitions:\n"
        "<Subject 1> is a young woman, appearance from <Picture 1>, <Picture 2>.\n"
        "<Subject 2> is an old woman, appearance from <Picture 3>.\n\n"
        "integrated_multimodal_description: Live-action, natural colors. Two women talk.\n\n"
        "overall_soundscape: Wind.\n\nnon_diegetic_music: N/A")
    assert final.ref_asset_ids == ("a_1", "a_2", "a_3")


def test_refs_without_pictures_or_with_a_tag_in_the_text_are_refused():
    with pytest.raises(PromptError, match="нет фото") as err:
        build(mode="refs", style_block="", action_text="x", performance_text="", sound_text="",
              music_text="", refs=[{"tag": "a", "description_en": "a", "image_asset_ids": []}])
    assert err.value.code == "refs_without_images"
    with pytest.raises(PromptError) as err:
        build(mode="refs", style_block="", action_text="@anna walks", performance_text="",
              sound_text="", music_text="",
              refs=[{"tag": "anna", "description_en": "a", "image_asset_ids": ["a_1"]}])
    assert err.value.code == "tag_in_text"


def test_number_references_is_build_ref2va_without_the_file_library():
    cards = {"@anna": {"kind": "person", "description": "a woman.", "assets": ["x.png", "y.png"]},
             "@lake": {"kind": "environment", "description": "a lake", "assets": ["l.png"]},
             "@voice": {"kind": "voice", "description": "a low voice", "assets": ["v.wav"]}}
    scene = library.number_references("@anna stands by @lake and @voice hums.", cards, ["@lake"])
    assert scene == library.Ref2VAScene(
        "subject_definitions:\n"
        "<Subject 1> is a woman, appearance from <Picture 2>, <Picture 3>.\n"
        "<Subject 2> is a lake, appearance from <Picture 1>.\n"
        "<Subject 3> is a low voice, voice from <Audio 1>.\n\n"
        "<Subject 1> stands by <Subject 2> and <Subject 3> hums.",
        ("l.png", "x.png", "y.png"), ("v.wav",), ("@anna", "@lake", "@voice"))
    with pytest.raises(library.LibraryError, match="теги не подключены к проекту: @ghost"):
        library.number_references("@ghost", cards)
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/director/prompt_final.py`:

```python
"""`prompt_final` (spec §9.4): the three-field format the H3 probes were run with (PROMPT-FORMAT
§1, §4) — `integrated_multimodal_description` (style + action + acting), `overall_soundscape`,
`non_diegetic_music` — and, for a `refs` scene, a `subject_definitions:` block in front with the
pictures numbered by `library.number_references` in the order of the scene's refs (SCENE_REF).
The take gets this text verbatim (§6.2); nothing here talks to a model."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass

from h3_48gb import library

DEFAULT_STYLE = "Live-action, natural colors."
_TAG = re.compile(r"@[\w-]+")


class PromptError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.message = message


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FinalPrompt:
    prompt: str
    ref_asset_ids: tuple[str, ...]          # in <Picture k> order (k = index + 1)


def _clean(text: str) -> str:
    return " ".join(text.split())


def three_fields(*, style_block: str, action_text: str, performance_text: str, sound_text: str,
                 music_text: str) -> str:
    integrated = " ".join(p for p in (_clean(style_block) or DEFAULT_STYLE, _clean(action_text),
                                      _clean(performance_text)) if p)
    music = _clean(music_text)
    if music.lower() in ("", "none", "n/a"):
        music = "N/A"
    return (f"integrated_multimodal_description: {integrated}\n\n"
            f"overall_soundscape: {_clean(sound_text)}\n\n"
            f"non_diegetic_music: {music}")


def build(*, mode: str, style_block: str, action_text: str, performance_text: str,
          sound_text: str, music_text: str, refs: list[dict]) -> FinalPrompt:
    """`refs`: the scene's cast in SCENE_REF order, `{"tag", "description_en", "image_asset_ids"}`.
    Only a `refs` scene carries pictures; who is on screen is the refs chips, never an @tag in
    the text (an @tag there is refused, plan ruling R-S3-3)."""
    text = three_fields(style_block=style_block, action_text=action_text,
                        performance_text=performance_text, sound_text=sound_text,
                        music_text=music_text)
    if mode != "refs":
        return FinalPrompt(text, ())
    if _TAG.search(text):
        raise PromptError("tag_in_text", "Уберите @тег из текста: кто в кадре — это чипы "
                                         "«Кто в кадре»")
    with_pictures = [r for r in refs if r["image_asset_ids"]]
    if not with_pictures:
        raise PromptError("refs_without_images", "У героев сцены нет фото — нечем снимать "
                                                 "в режиме «Референсы»")
    cards = {"@" + r["tag"]: {"kind": "person", "description": r["description_en"],
                              "assets": list(r["image_asset_ids"])} for r in with_pictures}
    numbered = library.number_references(text, cards, ["@" + r["tag"] for r in with_pictures])
    lines, k = [], 0
    for number, r in enumerate(with_pictures, start=1):
        labels = []
        for _ in r["image_asset_ids"]:
            k += 1
            labels.append(f"<Picture {k}>")
        description = _clean(r["description_en"]).rstrip(".")
        lines.append(f"<Subject {number}> is {description}, appearance from {', '.join(labels)}.")
    return FinalPrompt("subject_definitions:\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n" + numbered.prompt,
                       tuple(numbered.images))
```

### Task 12: Рендер по плану — O10 (render), O11, материализация, вид задачи `render` (§6.2, §7, §8.3, §8.5, И2–И4, И6)

**Files:**
- Create: `h3_48gb/studio/renders.py`
- Modify: `h3_48gb/studio/plans.py` (дописать `plan_render`, `enqueue_render`), `h3_48gb/studio/app.py` (ветка `render` в O10, маршрут O11), `h3_48gb/studio/worker.py` (`RenderKind` в `build_worker`), `web/openapi.json` (перегенерировать)
- Test: `tests/test_studio_render.py`, `tests/test_studio_cli.py` (виды задач)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `prompt_final` (задача 11), `templates`, `estimates`, `tasks.finish_cancelled`, `assemble._extract_valid_last_frame` (как функция над путями, §3.1).
- Produces:
  - `renders.materialize(conn, take_id) -> bool` (в транзакции; И2 один раз, И3; сцена изменилась → `stale` + задача `cancelled`). Ред. 2 (Codex-5, -6): источник продолжения — только `done` (не `stale`); каждый входной файл (кадр, последний кадр источника, фото героев) существует, и его sha256 равен записанному; формат проекта равен формату плана (`payload.frame_format`); иначе `renders.InputUnavailable` — O11 отвечает 409 `input_unavailable` (весь O11 откатывается), воркер отменяет ждущий дубль с текстом «вход недоступен: …». O11 сверяет и формат плана (409 `plan_stale`, `detail.stale = [{"frame_format": {"planned", "current"}}]`). `RenderKind.finish`: ошибка извлечения кадров (`AssembleError`, `OSError`) и залитый хвост ролика (разброс яркости последнего кадра ≤ порога `assemble`) — `VerifyError` → задача `failed`, воркер идёт дальше; миниатюры проверяются по коду возврата и файлу. `TaskSpec.template_sha256` — из `inputs_json`; `renders.final_prompt(conn, scene, project)`, `renders.scene_refs(conn, scene_id)`, `renders.canonical(dict) -> str`, `CANVAS`, `TIER_STEPS = {"sketch": 20, "final": 50}`, `THUMB_SECONDS = (0, 2, 4, 8)`, `RenderKind(home, *, machine, run=subprocess.run)`.
  - `inputs_json` — канонический JSON §6.2: `executor, template{id, sha256}, tier, steps, seed, prompt, requested_s, frames, canvas, keyframes[{position, asset_id, sha256, upstream_take_id}], refs[{ord, role, asset_id, sha256, label}], audio, scene_revision, head_drop_frames`.
  - `plans.plan_render(conn, project_id, *, scene_ids, tier) -> plan` (элементы: `scene_id, idx, template, tier, steps, frames, width, height, seed, prompt, upstream_take_id, depends_on_scene, estimate_s, estimate_source, status: ready|blocked|invalid, reasons`), `plans.enqueue_render(conn, project_id, plan_id, key) -> {"tasks": [{task_id, take_id, scene_id, status}]}` (409 `plan_has_errors`, 409 `plan_stale` с `detail.stale`).
  - Файлы дубля: `<outdir>/studio/projects/<pid>/takes/<take_id>/{f_00001_.mp4,last.png,thumb-<s>.jpg}`; ресурсы `video` (`take_output`) и `frame` (`extracted_frame`, `origin_asset_id` = видео).
  - События: `take_materialized`, `take_done`, `take_stale`, `take_duplicate_inputs` (L14).
