Create `tests/test_studio_render.py`:

```python
"""Slice S3, rendering, end to end on fakes (spec §7 O10 render, O11; §6.1 И2–И4, И6; §8.3;
§12.3 mutations 3–7): two scenes, the second continuing from the last frame of the first, through
the real API, worker, adapter and ffmpeg. Contents are checked, not shapes: the bytes ComfyUI got,
the seeds and lengths in its graph, the keyframe hash in the take."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import io
import json
import subprocess

import pytest
from PIL import Image

from _studio_rig import Rig
from h3_48gb.studio.images import ImageKind
from h3_48gb.studio.renders import RenderKind

PORTRAIT = "a young woman with a loose dark braid in a pale linen dress"
ACTION = (f"{PORTRAIT} walks along the shore of a still lake in the early morning, the camera "
          "slowly moving backward in front of her at eye level, reeds on the left, mist low "
          "over the water, she brushes the reeds with her hand as she passes them.")
PERFORMANCE = ("Her gaze drops to the water, then lifts; a slow smile grows until her cheeks "
               "round, her hands loosen at her sides.")


def _kinds(rig):
    return {"image": ImageKind(rig.home, machine="alex-neuro"),
            "render": RenderKind(rig.home, machine="alex-neuro")}


@pytest.fixture
def rig(tmp_path):
    r = Rig(tmp_path, kinds=_kinds)
    yield r
    r.close()


def _png(size=(896, 512), rgb=(30, 90, 140)) -> bytes:
    out = io.BytesIO()
    Image.new("RGB", size, rgb).save(out, format="PNG")
    return out.getvalue()


def _upload(rig, data: bytes) -> dict:
    r = rig.client.post("/api/studio/assets", files={"file": ("f.png", data, "image/png")})
    assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
    return r.json()


def _scene_in(**over):
    base = {"mode": "from_frame", "action_text": ACTION, "performance_text": PERFORMANCE,
            "sound_text": "Bare heels on wet planks, reeds rustle.", "music_text": "none",
            "requested_s": 8, "refs": ["anna"]}
    return base | over


def _two_scenes(rig, *, seeds=(11, 22)):
    p = rig.project()
    photo = _upload(rig, _png((512, 512), (200, 150, 120)))
    cast = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "девушка",
        "description_en": "a young woman", "portrait_phrase": PORTRAIT,
        "assets": [{"asset_id": photo["id"], "role": "image"}]}).json()
    frame = _upload(rig, _png())
    out = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": cast["project"]["revision"], "scenes": [
            _scene_in(seed=seeds[0], source_frame={"kind": "asset", "asset_id": frame["id"]}),
            _scene_in(seed=seeds[1], source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"})]}).json()
    return p, out["scenes"], frame


def _plan(rig, p, scene_ids, tier="sketch"):
    return rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "render", "scene_ids": scene_ids,
                                                  "tier": tier}, key=False)


def _render(rig, p, plan, key=None):
    return rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/render", {"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]}, key=key,
                    expect=201)


def _take(rig, take_id):
    return rig.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM take WHERE id = ?", (take_id,)).fetchone()


def _asset_bytes(rig, asset_id):
    row = rig.conn.execute("SELECT path FROM asset WHERE id = ?", (asset_id,)).fetchone()
    return open(row["path"], "rb").read()


def _last_frame_rgb(mp4) -> bytes:
    """The clip's literal last frame, decoded independently of the code under test."""
    frames = int(subprocess.run(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-count_frames", "-select_streams",
                                 "v:0", "-show_entries", "stream=nb_read_frames", "-of",
                                 "csv=p=0", str(mp4)], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout)
    return subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", str(mp4), "-vf",
                           f"select=eq(n\\,{frames - 1})", "-frames:v", "1", "-f", "rawvideo",
                           "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "-"], capture_output=True, check=True).stdout


def _png_rgb(data: bytes) -> bytes:
    return Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)).convert("RGB").tobytes()


def frame_bytes(rig, asset_id):
    return _asset_bytes(rig, asset_id)


def test_the_plan_marks_the_continuation_blocked_not_invalid(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s2["id"], s1["id"]])
    assert [(i["scene_id"], i["template"], i["status"], i["depends_on_scene"], i["steps"],
             i["frames"], i["width"], i["height"], i["seed"], i["reasons"])
            for i in plan["items"]] == [
        (s1["id"], "h3-fl2va", "ready", None, 20, 192, 896, 512, 11, []),
        (s2["id"], "h3-fl2va", "blocked", s1["id"], 20, 192, 896, 512, 22, [])]
    # card 90 s at 3 s / 20 steps, scaled to 192 frames
    assert [i["estimate_s"] for i in plan["items"]] == [236.7, 236.7]
    assert (plan["blocked"], plan["invalid"], plan["total_s"]) == ([s2["id"]], [], 473.4)


def test_sketches_of_two_scenes_the_second_from_the_last_frame_of_the_first(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), frame = _two_scenes(rig)
    tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]]))["tasks"]
    t1, t2 = (_take(rig, t["take_id"]) for t in tasks)
    assert [t["status"] for t in tasks] == ["queued", "blocked"]
    # И3 / mutation 3: the continuation has no inputs before its keyframe exists
    assert (t1["status"], t2["status"], t2["inputs_json"]) == ("queued", "pending_input", None)
    assert t2["upstream_take_id"] == t1["id"]
    assert rig.drain() == 2
    t1, t2 = _take(rig, t1["id"]), _take(rig, t2["id"])
    assert (t1["status"], t2["status"]) == ("done", "done")
    j1, j2 = rig.comfy.prompts
    in1, in2 = json.loads(t1["inputs_json"]), json.loads(t2["inputs_json"])
    # the graph ComfyUI ran is the take's frozen inputs: text, seed (mutation 5), steps, length
    for job, inputs, seed in ((j1, in1, 11), (j2, in2, 22)):
        g = job["prompt"]
        assert (g["6"]["inputs"]["prompt"], g["10"]["inputs"]["noise_seed"],
                g["7"]["inputs"]["steps"], g["6"]["inputs"]["length"],
                (g["6"]["inputs"]["width"], g["6"]["inputs"]["height"])) == (
            inputs["prompt"], seed, 20, 192, (896, 512))
    # scene 1 starts from the uploaded frame; scene 2 from the last frame of take 1 (mutation 4)
    assert rig.comfy.uploads[f"{j1['client_id']}-1.png"] == frame_bytes(rig, frame["id"])
    sent = rig.comfy.uploads[f"{j2['client_id']}-1.png"]
    assert sent == _asset_bytes(rig, t1["last_frame_asset_id"])
    video1 = rig.conn.execute("SELECT path FROM asset WHERE id = ?",
                              (t1["output_asset_id"],)).fetchone()["path"]
    assert _png_rgb(sent) == _last_frame_rgb(video1)
    assert t2["keyframe_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(sent).hexdigest()
    assert (in2["keyframes"], in2["head_drop_frames"], in1["head_drop_frames"]) == (
        [{"position": 0, "asset_id": t1["last_frame_asset_id"],
          "sha256": hashlib.sha256(sent).hexdigest(), "upstream_take_id": t1["id"]}], 1, 0)
    assert t2["inputs_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(in2, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
    ).hexdigest()
    assert [k for k, _ in rig.events("take_materialized", "take_done")] == [
        "take_materialized", "take_done", "take_materialized", "take_done"]


def test_the_same_key_queues_the_plan_once(rig):
    """Mutation 7 / И5."""
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]])
    first = _render(rig, p, plan, key="press-1")
    assert _render(rig, p, plan, key="press-1") == first
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM take").fetchone()[0] == 2


def test_a_scene_edited_after_the_plan_is_plan_stale(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]])
    rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}", json={
        "expected_edit_version": s1["edit_version"], "seed": 99})
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/render", json={"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]},
                        headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "plan_stale")
    assert r.json()["error"]["detail"]["stale"] == [
        {"scene_id": s1["id"], "planned": {"revision": 1, "state_version": 1},
         "current": {"revision": 2, "state_version": 1}}]
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM take").fetchone()[0] == 0


def test_a_continuation_whose_source_is_not_planned_or_chosen_is_invalid(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s2["id"]])
    assert (plan["invalid"], plan["items"][0]["reasons"]) == (
        [s2["id"]], ["L12: кадр сцены 1 не получится — её нет в плане и у неё нет выбранного "
                     "дубля (эскиз)"])
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/render",
                        json={"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]}, headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "plan_has_errors")


def test_invalid_inputs_are_named_before_any_gpu_time(rig):
    p = rig.project()
    cast = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "девушка",
        "description_en": "a young woman", "portrait_phrase": PORTRAIT,
        "assets": [{"asset_id": _upload(rig, _png((512, 512)))["id"], "role": "image"}
                   for _ in range(7)]}).json()
    portrait = _upload(rig, _png((512, 896)))
    scenes = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": cast["project"]["revision"], "scenes": [
            _scene_in(source_frame={"kind": "none"}),
            _scene_in(source_frame={"kind": "asset", "asset_id": portrait["id"]}),
            _scene_in(mode="refs", requested_s=14.375),
            _scene_in(mode="text"),
            _scene_in(source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"}),
            _scene_in(mode="refs", requested_s=3,
                      source_frame={"kind": "asset", "asset_id": portrait["id"]})]}
    ).json()["scenes"]
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s["id"] for s in scenes])
    assert [(i["status"], i["reasons"]) for i in plan["items"]] == [
        ("invalid", ["L12: у сцены нет кадра — сделайте кадр на шаге 4 или выберите источник"]),
        ("invalid", ["L7d: кадр 9:16, а проект 16:9"]),
        ("invalid", ["L7b: картинок-референсов 7, а при 345 кадрах шаблон держит 6"]),
        ("invalid", ["Режим «Текст» не снимается шаблонами части 1"]),
        ("invalid", ["сцена 4 в этом плане с ошибкой"]),
        ("invalid", ["L7b: картинок-референсов 7, а при 73 кадрах шаблон держит 6",
                     "L7d: портретный кадр в режиме «Референсы» модель молча игнорирует — "
                     "уберите кадр или снимайте «От кадра»"])]
    assert rig.comfy.prompts == []


def test_a_failed_first_scene_cancels_its_continuation(tmp_path):
    rig = Rig(tmp_path, kinds=_kinds, comfy_kwargs={"fail": "CUDA out of memory"})
    try:
        p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
        tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]]))["tasks"]
        rig.drain()
        t1, t2 = (rig.task(t["task_id"]) for t in tasks)
        assert (t1["status"], t2["status"], t2["error"]) == (
            "failed", "cancelled", "upstream_failed: предыдущая сцена не снялась")
        assert [_take(rig, t["take_id"])["status"] for t in tasks] == ["failed", "cancelled"]
        assert len(rig.comfy.prompts) == 1
    finally:
        rig.close()


def test_a_reshoot_with_the_same_inputs_is_noted(rig):
    """L14: the same inputs_sha256 twice — allowed, the event says so."""
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    first = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    second = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    assert rig.events("take_duplicate_inputs") == [("take_duplicate_inputs", {
        "take_id": second, "same_as": [first]})]


def test_the_worker_keeps_the_model_it_holds(rig):
    """§8.4: among queued tasks of equal priority, the one on the loaded model goes first (a
    switch costs 16–38 s); otherwise the oldest."""
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))
    rig.drain()                                              # the engine now holds h3-fl2va
    image = rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/plan", {"kind": "image", "scene_id": s1["id"]},
                     key=False)
    rig.post(f"/projects/{p['id']}/image", {"plan_id": image["plan_id"]}, expect=201)
    _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))               # queued after the keyframe
    rig.drain()
    assert [j["prompt"]["1"]["inputs"]["unet_name"] for j in rig.comfy.prompts] == [
        "minimax_h3_fl2va_pruned_int8_convrot.safetensors",
        "minimax_h3_fl2va_pruned_int8_convrot.safetensors",
        "krea2_turbo_int8_convrot.safetensors"]


def test_a_take_that_finishes_after_its_scene_was_edited_is_stale(rig):
    """И4: the late result is kept but can never be chosen."""
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    take1 = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    real = rig.executor.status
    edited = []

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "done" and not edited:
            scene = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["scenes"][0]
            rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}", json={
                "expected_edit_version": scene["edit_version"], "action_text": ACTION + " Then"
                " she stops."})
            edited.append(1)
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    assert _take(rig, take1)["status"] == "stale"
    assert rig.events("take_stale") == [("take_stale", {"take_id": take1, "tier": "sketch",
                                                        "reason": "scene_changed"})]


def test_a_continuation_edited_while_its_source_renders_is_not_shot_with_stale_inputs(rig):
    """Review Focus 2: the owner edits scene 2 while scene 1's sketch is rendering. Scene 2's
    take was planned for the old text: at materialization it becomes `stale` and its task is
    cancelled — it never runs with inputs nobody planned."""
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]]))["tasks"]
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "running" and _take(rig, tasks[1]["take_id"])["status"] == \
                "pending_input" and not getattr(status, "done", False):
            status.done = True
            rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s2['id']}", json={
                "expected_edit_version": s2["edit_version"], "sound_text": "Gravel crunches."})
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    take2 = _take(rig, tasks[1]["take_id"])
    assert (take2["status"], take2["inputs_json"], rig.task(tasks[1]["task_id"])["status"],
            rig.task(tasks[1]["task_id"])["error"], len(rig.comfy.prompts)) == (
        "stale", None, "cancelled", "сцена изменилась после постановки — постройте план заново", 1)
    assert rig.events("take_stale") == [("take_stale", {"take_id": take2["id"],
                                                        "reason": "scene_changed"})]


def test_a_vertical_project_renders_on_512_by_896(rig):
    """Review Focus 5: 9:16 — the canvas is the transpose; a horizontal frame is refused (L7d)."""
    p = rig.project("9:16")
    upright = _upload(rig, _png((512, 896)))
    lying = _upload(rig, _png((896, 512)))
    scenes = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "scenes": [
            _scene_in(refs=[], source_frame={"kind": "asset", "asset_id": upright["id"]}),
            _scene_in(refs=[], source_frame={"kind": "asset", "asset_id": lying["id"]})]}
    ).json()["scenes"]
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s["id"] for s in scenes])
    assert [(i["width"], i["height"], i["status"], i["reasons"]) for i in plan["items"]] == [
        (512, 896, "ready", []), (512, 896, "invalid", ["L7d: кадр 16:9, а проект 9:16"])]


# -- И3, the format and the template between the plan and the run (revision 2) -----------------

def test_a_source_take_gone_stale_cancels_the_continuation(rig):
    """И3: the continuation starts only from a `done` take — not from one whose scene was
    edited while it rendered (`stale`)."""
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]]))["tasks"]
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "done" and _take(rig, tasks[0]["take_id"])["status"] == "running":
            scene = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["scenes"][0]
            rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}", json={
                "expected_edit_version": scene["edit_version"], "sound_text": "Gravel."})
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    t2 = rig.task(tasks[1]["task_id"])
    assert (_take(rig, tasks[0]["take_id"])["status"], t2["status"], t2["error"],
            _take(rig, tasks[1]["take_id"])["status"], len(rig.comfy.prompts)) == (
        "stale", "cancelled", "вход недоступен: дубль предыдущей сцены не готов к продолжению "
                              "(статус stale)", "cancelled", 1)


def test_a_vanished_last_frame_cancels_the_continuation(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]]))["tasks"]
    assert rig.worker.run_once() is True                 # scene 1 only
    last = rig.conn.execute("SELECT path FROM asset WHERE id = ?", (
        _take(rig, tasks[0]["take_id"])["last_frame_asset_id"],)).fetchone()["path"]
    import pathlib
    pathlib.Path(last).unlink()
    rig.drain()
    assert rig.task(tasks[1]["task_id"])["error"] == \
        "вход недоступен: последний кадр источника: файла last.png нет на диске"
    assert len(rig.comfy.prompts) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["missing", "changed"])
def test_a_source_frame_that_is_not_what_was_recorded_is_refused_at_o11(rig, damage):
    p, (s1, s2), frame = _two_scenes(rig)
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]])
    import pathlib
    path = pathlib.Path(rig.conn.execute("SELECT path FROM asset WHERE id = ?",
                                         (frame["id"],)).fetchone()["path"])
    if damage == "missing":
        path.unlink()
    else:
        path.write_bytes(_png(rgb=(1, 2, 3)))
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/render",
                        json={"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]}, headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k"})
    detail = {"missing": "файла {} нет на диске", "changed": "файл {} изменён после записи"}
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"], r.json()["error"]["message"]) == (
        409, "input_unavailable",
        "Вход сцены недоступен: кадр сцены: " + detail[damage].format(path.name))
    assert rig.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM take").fetchone()[0] == 0


def test_a_format_change_after_the_plan_is_plan_stale(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]])
    revision = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["project"]["revision"]
    assert rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}", json={
        "expected_revision": revision, "frame_format": "9:16"}).status_code == 200
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/render",
                        json={"plan_id": plan["plan_id"]}, headers={"Idempotency-Key": "k"})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["detail"]["stale"]) == (
        409, [{"frame_format": {"planned": "16:9", "current": "9:16"}}])


def test_a_format_change_after_o11_cancels_the_waiting_continuation(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]]))["tasks"]
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "running" and not getattr(status, "done", False):
            status.done = True                         # no take is done yet: O4 allows it
            project = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["project"]
            assert rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}", json={
                "expected_revision": project["revision"], "frame_format": "9:16"}
            ).status_code == 200
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    assert rig.task(tasks[1]["task_id"])["error"] == (
        "вход недоступен: формат проекта сменился после плана (16:9 → 9:16) — постройте план "
        "заново")


def test_a_template_changed_after_the_plan_is_refused_not_run(rig, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Codex-10: the graph that runs is the one whose sha256 is in the take, or none."""
    from h3_48gb.studio import templates
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    task = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]
    changed = tmp_path / "tpl"
    changed.mkdir()
    for f in templates.TEMPLATE_DIR.iterdir():
        (changed / f.name).write_bytes(f.read_bytes())
    graph = json.loads((changed / "h3-fl2va.graph.json").read_text())
    graph["9"]["inputs"]["sampler_name"] = "euler"
    (changed / "h3-fl2va.graph.json").write_text(json.dumps(graph))
    monkeypatch.setattr(templates, "TEMPLATE_DIR", changed)
    templates.load.cache_clear()
    try:
        rig.drain()
    finally:
        templates.load.cache_clear()
    error = rig.task(task["task_id"])["error"]
    assert (rig.task(task["task_id"])["status"], rig.comfy.prompts) == ("failed", [])
    assert error.startswith("шаблон h3-fl2va изменился после постановки (")
    assert error.endswith(") — постройте план заново")


def test_a_flat_clip_fails_and_the_next_task_still_runs(tmp_path):
    """§8.5 «кадры залиты»: a flat result is failed, the worker goes on (Codex-7)."""
    rig = Rig(tmp_path, kinds=_kinds, comfy_kwargs={"flat": True})
    try:
        p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
        first = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]
        assert rig.worker.run_once() is True
        assert (rig.task(first["task_id"])["status"], rig.task(first["task_id"])["error"]) == (
            "failed", "результат не прошёл проверку: ролик залит одним цветом в конце — "
                      "последний кадр негоден")
        rig.comfy.flat = False
        second = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]
        rig.drain()
        assert rig.task(second["task_id"])["status"] == "succeeded"
    finally:
        rig.close()


def test_an_unreadable_clip_is_failed_not_a_crash(rig, monkeypatch):
    from h3_48gb import assemble
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    task = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]

    def broken(video, out, *, run):
        raise assemble.AssembleError("ffmpeg tail-frame extraction (n=1) exited 1: moov atom")
    monkeypatch.setattr(assemble, "_extract_valid_last_frame", broken)
    rig.drain()
    assert (rig.task(task["task_id"])["status"], rig.task(task["task_id"])["error"]) == (
        "failed", "результат не прошёл проверку: ролик не читается: ffmpeg tail-frame "
                  "extraction (n=1) exited 1: moov atom")


def test_a_render_task_records_the_template_of_its_take(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    rig.comfy.comfyui_version = "0.42.1"
    task = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]
    rig.drain()
    inputs = json.loads(_take(rig, task["take_id"])["inputs_json"])
    row = rig.conn.execute("SELECT engine_version, template_sha256 FROM task WHERE id = ?",
                           (task["task_id"],)).fetchone()
    assert tuple(row) == ("0.42.1", inputs["template"]["sha256"])
```


- [ ] **Step 2: Увидеть красным** — `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'h3_48gb.studio.renders'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Реализация**

Apply to `h3_48gb/studio/app.py` (`git apply`; контекст — точный текст файла после предыдущих задач):

```diff
diff --git a/h3_48gb/studio/app.py b/h3_48gb/studio/app.py
index 81e6c4f0..f6a83036 100644
--- a/h3_48gb/studio/app.py
+++ b/h3_48gb/studio/app.py
@@ -301,7 +301,9 @@ def create_app(outdir: Path, *, web_dist: Path = WEB_DIST, llm: Llm | None = Non
         if body.kind == "image":
             return plans.plan_image(conn, project_id, scene_id=body.scene_id, prompt=body.prompt,
                                     width=body.width, height=body.height)
-        raise StudioError(400, "plan_kind_unsupported", "План рендера появится в срезе S3")
+        if not body.scene_ids or body.tier is None:
+            raise StudioError(400, "args_invalid", "Для плана рендера нужны scene_ids и tier")
+        return plans.plan_render(conn, project_id, scene_ids=body.scene_ids, tier=body.tier)
 
     @app.post(f"{api}/projects/{{project_id}}/image")
     def confirm_image(body: schemas.PlanConfirm, project_id: str = Depends(project_exists),
@@ -312,6 +314,16 @@ def create_app(outdir: Path, *, web_dist: Path = WEB_DIST, llm: Llm | None = Non
             lambda c: plans.enqueue_image(c, project_id, body.plan_id, idempotency_key))
         return JSONResponse(answer, status_code=status)
 
+    @app.post(f"{api}/projects/{{project_id}}/render")
+    def confirm_render(body: schemas.PlanConfirm, project_id: str = Depends(project_exists),
+                       idempotency_key: str | None = Header(default=None),
+                       conn=Depends(get_conn)):
+        sha = idempotency.request_sha256(f"render:{project_id}", body.model_dump())
+        status, answer = idempotency.run_once(
+            conn, idempotency_key, sha,
+            lambda c: plans.enqueue_render(c, project_id, body.plan_id, idempotency_key))
+        return JSONResponse(answer, status_code=status)
+
     @app.get(f"{api}/tasks/{{task_id}}")
     def get_task(task_id: str, conn=Depends(get_conn)):
         return tasks.get_task(conn, task_id)
```

Apply to `h3_48gb/studio/plans.py` (`git apply`; контекст — точный текст файла после предыдущих задач):

```diff
diff --git a/h3_48gb/studio/plans.py b/h3_48gb/studio/plans.py
index 83290ad6..eb399662 100644
--- a/h3_48gb/studio/plans.py
+++ b/h3_48gb/studio/plans.py
@@ -137,3 +137,189 @@ def enqueue_image(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, plan_id: str, key:
     events.emit(conn, "task_queued", project_id=project_id, task_id=task_id,
                 payload={"kind": "image", "plan_id": plan_id})
     return {"task_id": task_id, "status": "queued"}
+
+
+# -- O10 kind=render, O11 (slice S3) -----------------------------------------------------------
+
+TIER_LABEL = {"sketch": "эскиз", "final": "финал"}
+
+
+def _orientation(width: int, height: int) -> str:
+    return "16:9" if width > height else "9:16" if height > width else "1:1"
+
+
+def plan_render(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, *, scene_ids: list[str],
+                tier: str) -> dict:
+    """What a render of these scenes would be (§7 O10): per scene its template, time, and either
+    `ready`, `blocked` (its keyframe comes from a scene of this same plan — Codex-1: not a
+    finding) or `invalid` with the reasons (lint errors; L7b, L7d, L12, L13 of §10)."""
+    from h3_48gb.studio import ops, renders
+    from h3_48gb.studio.director.prompt_final import PromptError
+    from PIL import Image
+
+    project = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM project WHERE id = ?", (project_id,)).fetchone()
+    live = {r["id"]: r for r in ops._live_scenes(conn, project_id)}
+    unknown = [s for s in scene_ids if s not in live]
+    if unknown or not scene_ids:
+        raise StudioError(404, "scene_not_found", "Сцена не найдена", {"scene_ids": unknown})
+    selected = sorted({live[s]["id"]: live[s] for s in scene_ids}.values(), key=lambda r: r["idx"])
+    in_plan = {s["id"] for s in selected}
+    lint = ops.lint_project(conn, project_id, [s["id"] for s in selected])
+    machine = settings.get(conn, "machine")
+    width, height = renders.CANVAS[project["frame_format"]]
+    steps = renders.TIER_STEPS[tier]
+    items, versions = [], {}
+    for scene in selected:
+        reasons = [f"{f['rule']}: {f['message']}" for f in lint[scene["id"]]
+                   if f["severity"] == "error"]
+        findings = list(lint[scene["id"]])
+        versions[scene["id"]] = {"revision": scene["revision"],
+                                 "state_version": scene["state_version"]}
+        template_id = templates.TEMPLATE_FOR_MODE.get(scene["mode"])
+        upstream, depends, prompt, pictures = None, None, None, 0
+        if template_id is None:
+            reasons.append("Режим «Текст» не снимается шаблонами части 1")
+            template_id = templates.TEMPLATE_FOR_MODE["from_frame"]
+        template = templates.load(template_id)
+        try:
+            final = renders.final_prompt(conn, scene, project)
+            prompt, pictures = final.prompt, len(final.ref_asset_ids)
+        except PromptError as err:
+            reasons.append(err.message)
+        if scene["mode"] == "refs" and pictures > template.ref_image_limit(scene["frames"]):
+            reasons.append(f"L7b: картинок-референсов {pictures}, а при {scene['frames']} кадрах "
+                           f"шаблон держит {template.ref_image_limit(scene['frames'])}")
+        if scene["mode"] == "refs" and scene["source_frame_kind"] == "asset":
+            frame = conn.execute("SELECT path FROM asset WHERE id = ?",
+                                 (scene["source_frame_asset_id"],)).fetchone()
+            with Image.open(frame["path"]) as image:
+                if image.size[1] > image.size[0]:
+                    # README режимов §2 п.4 (A8, A8b): ref2va silently ignores a portrait frame
+                    reasons.append("L7d: портретный кадр в режиме «Референсы» модель молча "
+                                   "игнорирует — уберите кадр или снимайте «От кадра»")
+        if scene["mode"] == "from_frame":
+            kind = scene["source_frame_kind"]
+            if kind == "none":
+                reasons.append("L12: у сцены нет кадра — сделайте кадр на шаге 4 или выберите "
+                               "источник")
+            elif kind == "asset":
+                frame = conn.execute("SELECT path FROM asset WHERE id = ?",
+                                     (scene["source_frame_asset_id"],)).fetchone()
+                with Image.open(frame["path"]) as image:
+                    shape = _orientation(*image.size)
+                if shape != project["frame_format"]:
+                    reasons.append(f"L7d: кадр {shape}, а проект {project['frame_format']}")
+            else:
+                source = live.get(scene["source_scene_id"])
+                if source is None:
+                    pass                         # L12 of the linter already said so
+                elif source["id"] in in_plan:
+                    depends = source["id"]
+                else:
+                    versions[source["id"]] = {"revision": source["revision"],
+                                              "state_version": source["state_version"]}
+                    approved = source[f"approved_{tier}_take_id"]
+                    take = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM take WHERE id = ?",
+                                        (approved,)).fetchone() if approved else None
+                    if take is not None and take["status"] == "done" \
+                            and take["scene_revision"] == source["revision"]:
+                        upstream = approved
+                    else:
+                        reasons.append(f"L12: кадр сцены {source['idx'] + 1} не получится — её нет "
+                                       f"в плане и у неё нет выбранного дубля ({TIER_LABEL[tier]})")
+        if steps < 20:
+            findings.append({"rule": "L13", "severity": "info", "scene_id": scene["id"],
+                             "field": None, "message": f"{steps} шагов — меньше 20"})
+        estimate = estimates.estimate(conn, executor=EXECUTOR, template=template_id,
+                                      machine=machine, width=width, height=height,
+                                      frames=scene["frames"], steps=steps)
+        lint[scene["id"]] = findings
+        items.append({"kind": "render", "scene_id": scene["id"], "idx": scene["idx"],
+                      "template": template_id, "tier": tier, "steps": steps,
+                      "frame_format": project["frame_format"],
+                      "frames": scene["frames"], "width": width, "height": height,
+                      "seed": scene["seed"], "prompt": prompt, "upstream_take_id": upstream,
+                      "depends_on_scene": depends, "estimate_s": estimate["seconds"],
+                      "estimate_source": estimate["source"],
+                      "status": "invalid" if reasons else "blocked" if depends else "ready",
+                      "reasons": reasons})
+    by_scene = {i["scene_id"]: i for i in items}
+    for item in items:                     # a scene waiting on an invalid one cannot run either
+        dep = by_scene.get(item["depends_on_scene"])
+        if dep is not None and dep["status"] == "invalid" and item["status"] != "invalid":
+            item["status"] = "invalid"
+            item["reasons"].append(f"сцена {dep['idx'] + 1} в этом плане с ошибкой")
+    runnable = [i for i in items if i["status"] != "invalid"]
+    switch = switches(conn, [i["template"] for i in runnable])
+    plan_id, expires = _save_plan(conn, project_id, tier=tier, items=items, versions=versions)
+    return {"plan_id": plan_id, "expires_at": expires, "kind": "render", "tier": tier,
+            "items": items,
+            "total_s": round(sum(i["estimate_s"] for i in runnable)
+                             + sum(s["seconds"] for s in switch), 1),
+            "checkpoint_switches": switch, "lint": lint,
+            "blocked": [i["scene_id"] for i in items if i["status"] == "blocked"],
+            "invalid": [i["scene_id"] for i in items if i["status"] == "invalid"]}
+
+
+def enqueue_render(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str, plan_id: str, key: str) -> dict:
+    """O11 inside `idempotency.run_once`'s transaction (И6: every task and take of the plan in
+    one transaction). The plan's scene versions are checked, not `project.revision` (§7)."""
+    from h3_48gb.studio import renders
+    plan = load_plan(conn, project_id, plan_id, "render")
+    invalid = [i["scene_id"] for i in plan["items"] if i["status"] == "invalid"]
+    if invalid:
+        raise StudioError(409, "plan_has_errors", "В плане есть сцены с ошибками",
+                          {"invalid": invalid})
+    stale = []
+    for scene_id, planned in plan["versions"].items():
+        row = conn.execute("SELECT revision, state_version, deleted_at FROM scene WHERE id = ?",
+                           (scene_id,)).fetchone()
+        current = {"revision": row["revision"], "state_version": row["state_version"]}
+        if row["deleted_at"] is not None or current != planned:
+            stale.append({"scene_id": scene_id, "planned": planned, "current": current})
+    project = conn.execute("SELECT frame_format FROM project WHERE id = ?",
+                           (project_id,)).fetchone()
+    planned_format = plan["items"][0]["frame_format"]
+    if planned_format != project["frame_format"]:       # canvas and L7d were for the old one
+        stale.append({"frame_format": {"planned": planned_format,
+                                       "current": project["frame_format"]}})
+    if stale:
+        raise StudioError(409, "plan_stale", "Сцены изменились после плана — постройте заново",
+                          {"stale": stale})
+    scope = settings.get(conn, "comfy.scope")
+    now = ids.now_iso()
+    made: dict[str, dict] = {}
+    out = []
+    for item in plan["items"]:
+        task_id, take_id = ids.new_id("k"), ids.new_id("t")
+        dep = made.get(item["depends_on_scene"])
+        upstream = dep["take_id"] if dep else item["upstream_take_id"]
+        revision = plan["versions"][item["scene_id"]]["revision"]
+        payload = {"template": item["template"], "take_id": take_id, "scene_id": item["scene_id"],
+                   "tier": item["tier"], "frame_format": item["frame_format"]}
+        conn.execute(
+            "INSERT INTO task (id, kind, executor, gpu_scope, payload_json, idempotency_key, "
+            "status, depends_on_task_id, project_id, created_at, updated_at) "
+            "VALUES (?, 'render', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
+            (task_id, EXECUTOR, scope, json.dumps(payload), f"{key}:{item['scene_id']}",
+             "blocked" if dep else "queued", dep["task_id"] if dep else None, project_id,
+             now, now))
+        conn.execute(
+            "INSERT INTO take (id, scene_id, scene_revision, tier, executor, template, "
+            "upstream_take_id, status, task_id, created_at) "
+            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending_input', ?, ?)",
+            (take_id, item["scene_id"], revision, item["tier"], EXECUTOR, item["template"],
+             upstream, task_id, now))
+        events.emit(conn, "task_queued", project_id=project_id, scene_id=item["scene_id"],
+                    task_id=task_id, payload={"kind": "render", "take_id": take_id,
+                                              "plan_id": plan_id, "blocked": bool(dep)})
+        if not dep:
+            try:
+                renders.materialize(conn, take_id)
+            except renders.InputUnavailable as err:      # the whole O11 rolls back (И6)
+                raise StudioError(409, "input_unavailable", f"Вход сцены недоступен: {err}",
+                                  {"scene_id": item["scene_id"]}) from None
+        made[item["scene_id"]] = {"task_id": task_id, "take_id": take_id}
+        out.append({"task_id": task_id, "take_id": take_id, "scene_id": item["scene_id"],
+                    "status": "blocked" if dep else "queued"})
+    return {"tasks": out}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/renders.py`:

```python
"""Render takes (spec §6, §6.2, §8.3, §8.5; slice S3): materialization of a take's inputs (И2, И3),
the worker's `render` task kind, and how a finished clip becomes a take.

A take is created by O11 in `pending_input`. Its `inputs_json` is written exactly once (trigger
И2), when every input exists: at O11 for a scene whose keyframe is already there, or in the
worker's `prepare` the moment the previous scene's take is done (И3). From then on the take is
what it renders: the same text, seed, frames and the sha256 of the very keyframe it starts from."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb import assemble
from h3_48gb.studio import estimates, events, ids, templates
from h3_48gb.studio.director import prompt_final
from h3_48gb.studio.executors.base import TaskSpec
from h3_48gb.studio.images import VerifyError
from h3_48gb.studio.tasks import finish_cancelled

EXECUTOR = "comfy"
TIER_STEPS = {"sketch": 20, "final": 50}
#: The canvas H3 was measured on (GPU-DAY-20261020/21: 896×512); portrait is its transpose.
CANVAS = {"16:9": (896, 512), "9:16": (512, 896)}
THUMB_SECONDS = (0, 2, 4, 8)          # O18 frames_urls (§7)


def canonical(data: dict) -> str:
    return json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))


def sha256_file(path: str | Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def scene_refs(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    """The scene's cast in SCENE_REF order, with its pictures (asset ids, cast_asset order)."""
    out = []
    for r in conn.execute("SELECT c.id, c.tag, c.description_en FROM scene_ref s "
                          "JOIN cast_member c ON c.id = s.cast_id WHERE s.scene_id = ? "
                          "ORDER BY s.ord", (scene_id,)):
        images = [a["asset_id"] for a in conn.execute(
            "SELECT asset_id FROM cast_asset WHERE cast_id = ? AND role = 'image' ORDER BY ord",
            (r["id"],))]
        out.append({"tag": r["tag"], "description_en": r["description_en"],
                    "image_asset_ids": images})
    return out


def final_prompt(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene: sqlite3.Row,
                 project: sqlite3.Row) -> prompt_final.FinalPrompt:
    return prompt_final.build(mode=scene["mode"], style_block=project["style_block"],
                              action_text=scene["action_text"],
                              performance_text=scene["performance_text"],
                              sound_text=scene["sound_text"], music_text=scene["music_text"],
                              refs=scene_refs(conn, scene["id"]))


def _asset(conn, asset_id: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
    return conn.execute("SELECT * FROM asset WHERE id = ?", (asset_id,)).fetchone()


class InputUnavailable(Exception):
    """An input of the take does not exist as recorded (И3): O11 refuses with 409
    `input_unavailable`, the worker cancels the waiting take with this text."""


def _verified(conn, asset_id: str, what: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
    """The asset row, after checking that its file is there and still has its recorded bytes
    (И3: «все входы существуют» — the row alone does not prove the file)."""
    row = _asset(conn, asset_id)
    path = Path(row["path"])
    if not path.is_file():
        raise InputUnavailable(f"{what}: файла {path.name} нет на диске")
    if sha256_file(path) != row["sha256"]:
        raise InputUnavailable(f"{what}: файл {path.name} изменён после записи")
    return row


def materialize(conn: sqlite3.Connection, take_id: str) -> bool:
    """Inside a transaction. Write the take's inputs once (И2) — only if they all exist (И3) and
    the scene is still the revision the take was planned for. False = the take was not queued
    (its scene changed: the take is stale and its task cancelled)."""
    take = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM take WHERE id = ?", (take_id,)).fetchone()
    scene = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM scene WHERE id = ?", (take["scene_id"],)).fetchone()
    project = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM project WHERE id = ?",
                           (scene["project_id"],)).fetchone()
    task = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM task WHERE id = ?", (take["task_id"],)).fetchone()
    if scene["deleted_at"] is not None or scene["revision"] != take["scene_revision"]:
        conn.execute("UPDATE take SET status = 'stale' WHERE id = ?", (take_id,))
        finish_cancelled(conn, task["id"], project_id=project["id"],
                         error="сцена изменилась после постановки — постройте план заново")
        events.emit(conn, "take_stale", project_id=project["id"], scene_id=scene["id"],
                    task_id=task["id"], payload={"take_id": take_id, "reason": "scene_changed"})
        return False
    planned_format = json.loads(task["payload_json"]).get("frame_format")
    if planned_format != project["frame_format"]:
        raise InputUnavailable(f"формат проекта сменился после плана ({planned_format} → "
                               f"{project['frame_format']}) — постройте план заново")
    template = templates.load(take["template"])
    keyframes, keyframe_sha = [], None
    if scene["mode"] == "from_frame":
        if take["upstream_take_id"] is not None:
            upstream = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM take WHERE id = ?",
                                    (take["upstream_take_id"],)).fetchone()
            if upstream["status"] != "done" or not upstream["last_frame_asset_id"]:
                # И3: done — not stale (its scene was edited), not failed, not still running
                raise InputUnavailable(f"дубль предыдущей сцены не готов к продолжению "
                                       f"(статус {upstream['status']})")
            frame = _verified(conn, upstream["last_frame_asset_id"], "последний кадр источника")
        else:
            frame = _verified(conn, scene["source_frame_asset_id"], "кадр сцены")
        keyframe_sha = frame["sha256"]
        keyframes = [{"position": 0, "asset_id": frame["id"], "sha256": frame["sha256"],
                      "upstream_take_id": take["upstream_take_id"]}]
    final = final_prompt(conn, scene, project)
    refs = [{"ord": k, "role": "person", "asset_id": a,
             "sha256": _verified(conn, a, f"фото героя <Picture {k}>")["sha256"],
             "label": f"<Picture {k}>"} for k, a in enumerate(final.ref_asset_ids, start=1)]
    width, height = CANVAS[project["frame_format"]]
    head_drop = template.head_drop_frames if take["upstream_take_id"] is not None else 0
    inputs = {"executor": EXECUTOR, "template": {"id": template.id, "sha256": template.sha256},
              "tier": take["tier"], "steps": TIER_STEPS[take["tier"]], "seed": scene["seed"],
              "prompt": final.prompt, "requested_s": scene["requested_s"],
              "frames": scene["frames"], "canvas": [width, height], "keyframes": keyframes,
              "refs": refs, "audio": [], "scene_revision": take["scene_revision"],
              "head_drop_frames": head_drop}
    text = canonical(inputs)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
    conn.execute("UPDATE take SET inputs_json = ?, inputs_sha256 = ?, keyframe_sha256 = ?, "
                 "status = 'queued' WHERE id = ?", (text, digest, keyframe_sha, take_id))
    conn.execute("UPDATE scene SET prompt_final = ? WHERE id = ?", (final.prompt, scene["id"]))
    conn.execute("UPDATE task SET status = 'queued', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ? "
                 "AND status IN ('blocked', 'queued')", (ids.now_iso(), task["id"]))
    events.emit(conn, "take_materialized", project_id=project["id"], scene_id=scene["id"],
                task_id=task["id"], payload={"take_id": take_id, "inputs_sha256": digest,
                                             "upstream_take_id": take["upstream_take_id"]})
    same = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM take WHERE inputs_sha256 = ? AND id != ? ORDER BY "
                        "created_at", (digest, take_id)).fetchall()
    if same:                                   # L14: a reshoot with the same seed — allowed
        events.emit(conn, "take_duplicate_inputs", project_id=project["id"],
                    scene_id=scene["id"], task_id=task["id"],
                    payload={"take_id": take_id, "same_as": [r["id"] for r in same]})
    return True


def _probe_frames(path: Path, run) -> int:
    out = run(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-count_frames", "-select_streams", "v:0",
               "-show_entries", "stream=nb_read_frames", "-of", "csv=p=0", str(path)],
              capture_output=True, text=True)
    try:
        return int(out.stdout.strip())
    except ValueError:
        raise VerifyError(f"ffprobe не посчитал кадры {path.name}") from None


class RenderKind:
    kind = "render"

    def __init__(self, home: Path, *, machine: str, run=subprocess.run):
        self.home = Path(home)
        self.machine = machine
        self.run = run

    def _take(self, conn, task) -> sqlite3.Row:
        take_id = json.loads(task["payload_json"])["take_id"]
        return conn.execute("SELECT * FROM take WHERE id = ?", (take_id,)).fetchone()

    def prepare(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        """Inside the worker's claim transaction (§8.5 `blocked`): a dependency done → materialize
        (И3); a dependency failed or cancelled → cancelled `upstream_failed`."""
        for task in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM task WHERE status = 'blocked' AND kind = 'render' "
                                 "ORDER BY rowid").fetchall():
            dep = conn.execute("SELECT status FROM task WHERE id = ?",
                               (task["depends_on_task_id"],)).fetchone()
            if dep["status"] == "succeeded":
                try:
                    materialize(conn, self._take(conn, task)["id"])
                except InputUnavailable as err:
                    finish_cancelled(conn, task["id"], project_id=task["project_id"],
                                     error=f"вход недоступен: {err}")
            elif dep["status"] in ("failed", "cancelled"):
                finish_cancelled(conn, task["id"], project_id=task["project_id"],
                                 error="upstream_failed: предыдущая сцена не снялась")

    def spec(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, task: sqlite3.Row) -> TaskSpec:
        take = self._take(conn, task)
        inputs = json.loads(take["inputs_json"])
        width, height = inputs["canvas"]
        path = lambda asset_id: Path(_asset(conn, asset_id)["path"])  # noqa: E731
        if inputs["keyframes"]:
            images = {"first_frame": [path(inputs["keyframes"][0]["asset_id"])]}
        else:
            images = {"ref_images": [path(r["asset_id"]) for r in inputs["refs"]]}
        return TaskSpec(task_id=task["id"], template=inputs["template"]["id"],
                        template_sha256=inputs["template"]["sha256"],
                        values={"prompt": inputs["prompt"], "seed": inputs["seed"],
                                "steps": inputs["steps"], "width": width, "height": height,
                                "length": inputs["frames"]},
                        images=images,
                        dest_dir=self.home / "projects" / task["project_id"] / "takes"
                        / take["id"])

    def on_running(self, conn, task) -> None:
        conn.execute("UPDATE take SET status = 'running' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'queued'",
                     (self._take(conn, task)["id"],))

    def on_requeued(self, conn, task) -> None:
        conn.execute("UPDATE take SET status = 'queued' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'running'",
                     (self._take(conn, task)["id"],))

    def on_failed(self, conn, task) -> None:
        conn.execute("UPDATE take SET status = 'failed' WHERE id = ? AND status IN "
                     "('pending_input', 'queued', 'running')", (self._take(conn, task)["id"],))

    def finish(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, task: sqlite3.Row, files: list[Path],
               spec: TaskSpec, wall_s: float) -> dict:
        """§8.5 `verifying`: one mp4 of exactly `frames` frames; its last valid frame (the next
        scene starts from it) and four thumbnails; the take `done`, or `stale` when its scene
        was edited meanwhile (И4)."""
        take = self._take(conn, task)
        inputs = json.loads(take["inputs_json"])
        videos = [f for f in files if f.suffix.lower() == ".mp4"]
        if len(videos) != 1:
            raise VerifyError(f"ждали один mp4, пришло: {', '.join(f.name for f in files)}")
        video = videos[0]
        if video.stat().st_size == 0:
            raise VerifyError(f"пустой файл {video.name}")
        frames = _probe_frames(video, self.run)
        if frames != inputs["frames"]:
            raise VerifyError(f"{frames} кадров вместо {inputs['frames']}")
        try:
            last = assemble._extract_valid_last_frame(video, spec.dest_dir / "last.png",
                                                      run=self.run)
            # §8.5 «кадры залиты»: the helper falls back to the literal last frame with a warning
            # when the whole tail is flat — for a take that is a failed result, not a frame
            if assemble._frame_luma_spread(last, run=self.run) \
                    <= assemble._FREEZE_FRAME_LUMA_SPREAD_THRESHOLD:
                raise VerifyError("ролик залит одним цветом в конце — последний кадр негоден")
            for second in THUMB_SECONDS:
                if second * 24 < frames:
                    thumb = spec.dest_dir / f"thumb-{second}.jpg"
                    done = self.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-y", "-ss", str(second), "-i",
                                     str(video), "-frames:v", "1", "-q:v", "4", str(thumb)],
                                    capture_output=True, text=True)
                    if done.returncode != 0 or not thumb.is_file():
                        raise VerifyError(f"кадр {second} с не извлёкся: "
                                          f"{(done.stderr or '').strip()[:200]}")
        except (assemble.AssembleError, OSError) as err:
            raise VerifyError(f"ролик не читается: {err}") from None
        now = ids.now_iso()
        video_id, frame_id = ids.new_id("a"), ids.new_id("a")
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO asset (id, kind, path, sha256, bytes, mime, origin_kind, origin_take_id, "
            "origin_task_id, created_at) VALUES (?, 'video', ?, ?, ?, 'video/mp4', 'take_output', "
            "?, ?, ?)", (video_id, str(video), sha256_file(video), video.stat().st_size,
                         take["id"], task["id"], now))
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO asset (id, kind, path, sha256, bytes, mime, origin_kind, origin_take_id, "
            "origin_asset_id, origin_task_id, created_at) VALUES (?, 'frame', ?, ?, ?, "
            "'image/png', 'extracted_frame', ?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (frame_id, str(last), sha256_file(last), Path(last).stat().st_size, take["id"],
             video_id, task["id"], now))
        scene = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM scene WHERE id = ?", (take["scene_id"],)).fetchone()
        status = "stale" if scene["revision"] > take["scene_revision"] else "done"
        conn.execute("UPDATE take SET status = ?, output_asset_id = ?, last_frame_asset_id = ? "
                     "WHERE id = ?", (status, video_id, frame_id, take["id"]))
        events.emit(conn, "take_stale" if status == "stale" else "take_done",
                    project_id=task["project_id"], scene_id=take["scene_id"], task_id=task["id"],
                    payload={"take_id": take["id"], "tier": take["tier"],
                             **({"reason": "scene_changed"} if status == "stale" else {})})
        width, height = inputs["canvas"]
        estimates.record(conn, executor=EXECUTOR, template=take["template"], machine=self.machine,
                         width=width, height=height, frames=frames, steps=inputs["steps"],
                         wall_s=wall_s)
        return {"kind": "render", "take_id": take["id"], "take_status": status}
```

Перегенерировать `h3_48gb/studio/web/openapi.json`: `$PY -m h3_48gb.studio openapi --out h3_48gb/studio/web/openapi.json`.

diff --git a/h3_48gb/studio/worker.py b/h3_48gb/studio/worker.py

--- a/h3_48gb/studio/worker.py
+++ b/h3_48gb/studio/worker.py

     executor = ComfyExecutor(url=conf["comfy.url"], output_dir=Path(conf["comfy.output_dir"]),
                              scope=scope, dispatcher=dispatcher,
                              engine=scope.split(":", 1)[1] if dispatcher else "local")

### Task 13: Выбор дубля — O16, O17, O18, каскад И11 (§6.1, §7, §12.5)

**Files:**
- Create: `h3_48gb/studio/approvals.py`
- Modify: `h3_48gb/studio/plans.py` (источник-продолжение — через `approvals.problem`), `ops.py` (импорт `approvals`; `snapshot.takes`; `cascade` в O8 и O7 в той же транзакции), `app.py` (маршруты O16–O18, кадры дубля), `schemas.py` (дописать), `web/openapi.json`
- Test: `tests/test_studio_approve.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `approvals.problem(conn, scene, take, tier) -> (reason, detail) | None` (`take_of_other_scene`, `tier_mismatch`, `take_stale`, `take_not_done`, `chain_mismatch`); `approvals.recompute_cascade(conn, project_id) -> [{scene_id, tier, take_id, reason}]` (в транзакции; событие `approval_revoked`; ред. 2, Codex-3: проходы повторяются до неподвижной точки — порядок сцен не важен, продолжение может стоять раньше источника); `approve(conn, scene_id, *, tier, take_id, expected_state_version) -> {scene, cascade}` (409 `state_conflict`/`take_stale`/`take_not_done`/`chain_mismatch` c `detail.upstream_take_id, approved_upstream`; 400 `tier_mismatch`; 404 `take_not_found`), `unapprove(…)`; `take_out(conn, take)`, `scene_takes(conn, scene_id)` (O18, новые первыми: `…, asset_url, frames_urls, valid_for_approval, reason, same_inputs_as` — ред. 2: L14 видна владельцу), `project_takes(conn, project_id)`, `thumb_path(home, project_id, take_id, second)`. Маршруты: `POST /scenes/{id}/approve`, `POST /scenes/{id}/unapprove`, `GET /scenes/{id}/takes`, `GET /takes/{id}/frames/{0|2|4|8}`. O7 и O8 отдают `cascade`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Написать падающие тесты**

Create `tests/test_studio_approve.py`:

```python
"""Slice S3, choosing takes (spec §7 O16–O18, §6.1 И4, И11; §4.4 step 6 «Переснять сцену 2 от
выбранного»): approve, the cascade on approve and on edits, the chain conflict, the takes list."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

import pytest

from _studio_rig import Rig
from test_studio_render import (ACTION, PORTRAIT, _asset_bytes, _kinds, _plan, _png, _render,
                                _scene_in, _take, _upload,
                                _two_scenes)


@pytest.fixture
def rig(tmp_path):
    r = Rig(tmp_path, kinds=_kinds)
    yield r
    r.close()


def _shoot_both(rig):
    p, (s1, s2), frame = _two_scenes(rig)
    tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"]]))["tasks"]
    rig.drain()
    return p, s1, s2, tasks[0]["take_id"], tasks[1]["take_id"]


def _approve(rig, scene_id, take_id, tier="sketch", expect=200):
    version = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{rig_project(rig)}").json()
    scene = next(s for s in version["scenes"] if s["id"] == scene_id)
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/scenes/{scene_id}/approve", json={
        "tier": tier, "take_id": take_id, "expected_state_version": scene["state_version"]})
    assert r.status_code == expect, r.text
    return r.json()


def rig_project(rig):
    return rig.conn.execute("SELECT id FROM project").fetchone()[0]


def test_choose_reshoot_scene_1_conflict_and_reshoot_scene_2_from_the_chosen(rig):
    p, s1, s2, take1, take2 = _shoot_both(rig)
    assert _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1)["cascade"] == []
    assert _approve(rig, s2["id"], take2)["scene"]["approved_sketch_take_id"] == take2
    # reshoot scene 1 and choose the new take: scene 2's choice is revoked (И11)
    take1b = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    rig.drain()
    answer = _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1b)
    assert answer["cascade"] == [{"scene_id": s2["id"], "tier": "sketch", "take_id": take2,
                                  "reason": "chain_mismatch"}]
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    scene2 = next(s for s in snap["scenes"] if s["id"] == s2["id"])
    assert (scene2["approved_sketch_take_id"], scene2["review_state"]) == (None, "needs_review")
    assert rig.events("approval_revoked") == [("approval_revoked", {
        "tier": "sketch", "take_id": take2, "reason": "chain_mismatch"})]
    refused = _approve(rig, s2["id"], take2, expect=409)
    assert refused["error"] == {"code": "chain_mismatch",
                                "message": "Сцена снята от другого дубля предыдущей сцены",
                                "detail": {"take_id": take2, "upstream_take_id": take1,
                                           "approved_upstream": take1b}}
    # «Переснять сцену 2 от выбранного»: a plan of scene 2 alone takes the chosen take of scene 1
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s2["id"]])
    assert (plan["items"][0]["status"], plan["items"][0]["upstream_take_id"]) == ("ready", take1b)
    take2b = _render(rig, p, plan)["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    assert _take(rig, take2b)["status"] == "queued"            # materialized at once
    rig.drain()
    assert _take(rig, take2b)["keyframe_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(
        _asset_bytes(rig, _take(rig, take1b)["last_frame_asset_id"])).hexdigest()
    assert _approve(rig, s2["id"], take2b)["cascade"] == []
    takes = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s2['id']}/takes").json()
    assert [(t["id"], t["valid_for_approval"], t["reason"], t["upstream_take_id"])
            for t in takes] == [(take2b, True, None, take1b),
                                (take2, False, "chain_mismatch", take1)]
    assert takes[0]["frames_urls"] == [f"/api/studio/takes/{take2b}/frames/{s}"
                                       for s in (0, 2, 4)]
    jpg = rig.client.get(takes[0]["frames_urls"][1])
    assert (jpg.status_code, jpg.content[:2]) == (200, b"\xff\xd8")


def test_editing_a_chosen_scene_revokes_it_and_its_continuation(rig):
    p, s1, s2, take1, take2 = _shoot_both(rig)
    _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1)
    _approve(rig, s2["id"], take2)
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    edited = rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}", json={
        "expected_edit_version": snap["scenes"][0]["edit_version"],
        "sound_text": "Gravel crunches."}).json()
    assert edited["cascade"] == [
        {"scene_id": s1["id"], "tier": "sketch", "take_id": take1, "reason": "take_stale"},
        {"scene_id": s2["id"], "tier": "sketch", "take_id": take2, "reason": "chain_mismatch"}]
    refused = _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1, expect=409)
    assert refused["error"]["code"] == "take_stale"


def test_choosing_needs_the_current_state_version(rig):
    p, s1, s2, take1, take2 = _shoot_both(rig)
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}/approve", json={
        "tier": "sketch", "take_id": take1, "expected_state_version": 0})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "state_conflict")
    _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1)
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}/approve", json={
        "tier": "final", "take_id": take1, "expected_state_version": 2})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["error"]["code"]) == (400, "tier_mismatch")
    r = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}/unapprove", json={
        "tier": "sketch", "expected_state_version": 2}).json()
    assert (r["scene"]["approved_sketch_take_id"], r["scene"]["state_version"], r["cascade"]) == (
        None, 3, [])


def test_the_snapshot_carries_the_takes(rig):
    p, s1, s2, take1, take2 = _shoot_both(rig)
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert [(t["id"], t["scene_id"], t["tier"], t["status"], t["head_drop_frames"])
            for t in snap["takes"]] == [(take1, s1["id"], "sketch", "done", 0),
                                        (take2, s2["id"], "sketch", "done", 1)]


def test_a_chosen_source_take_that_is_no_longer_valid_is_not_used(rig):
    """Defence in depth: should an invalid approval ever survive (written around the cascade),
    the plan does not chain a continuation to it."""
    p, s1, s2, take1, take2 = _shoot_both(rig)
    _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1)
    rig.conn.execute("UPDATE scene SET revision = revision + 1 WHERE id = ?", (s1["id"],))
    plan = _plan(rig, p, [s2["id"]])
    assert (plan["items"][0]["status"], plan["items"][0]["upstream_take_id"]) == ("invalid", None)


def test_a_stale_take_cannot_be_chosen(rig):
    """И4: a take that finished after its scene was edited is kept, and refused by O16."""
    p, (s1, s2), _ = _two_scenes(rig)
    take1 = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    real = rig.executor.status

    def status(ref):
        answer = real(ref)
        if answer["state"] == "done" and _take(rig, take1)["status"] == "running":
            scene = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()["scenes"][0]
            rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}", json={
                "expected_edit_version": scene["edit_version"],
                "action_text": ACTION + " Then she stops."})
        return answer
    rig.executor.status = status
    rig.drain()
    refused = _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1, expect=409)
    assert refused["error"] == {"code": "take_stale",
                                "message": "Дубль снят до правки сцены — его нельзя выбрать",
                                "detail": {"take_id": take1, "take_revision": 1,
                                           "scene_revision": 2}}


def test_the_cascade_follows_the_sources_not_the_scene_order(rig):
    """Codex-3: a continuation placed BEFORE its source (O7 keeps an explicit source; L12 is only
    a finding) must still lose its choice when the source's choice goes."""
    p, s1, s2, take1, take2 = _shoot_both(rig)
    _approve(rig, s1["id"], take1)
    _approve(rig, s2["id"], take2)
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    by_id = {s["id"]: s for s in snap["scenes"]}

    def scene_in(s, source):
        return {"scene_id": s["id"], "expected_edit_version": s["edit_version"],
                "mode": s["mode"], "action_text": s["action_text"],
                "performance_text": s["performance_text"], "sound_text": s["sound_text"],
                "music_text": s["music_text"], "requested_s": s["requested_s"],
                "seed": s["seed"], "source_frame": source, "refs": s["refs"]}
    moved = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": snap["project"]["revision"], "scenes": [
            scene_in(by_id[s2["id"]], {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": s1["id"]}),
            scene_in(by_id[s1["id"]], by_id[s1["id"]]["source_frame"] | {"scene_id": None})]})
    assert moved.status_code == 200, moved.text
    assert moved.json()["cascade"] == []               # same fingerprints: choices stand
    scene1 = next(s for s in moved.json()["scenes"] if s["id"] == s1["id"])
    edited = rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}", json={
        "expected_edit_version": scene1["edit_version"], "sound_text": "Gravel."}).json()
    assert edited["cascade"] == [
        {"scene_id": s1["id"], "tier": "sketch", "take_id": take1, "reason": "take_stale"},
        {"scene_id": s2["id"], "tier": "sketch", "take_id": take2, "reason": "chain_mismatch"}]
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    assert [(s["id"], s["approved_sketch_take_id"], s["review_state"]) for s in snap["scenes"]] \
        == [(s2["id"], None, "needs_review"), (s1["id"], None, "needs_review")]


def test_a_reshoot_with_the_same_inputs_is_marked_on_the_take(rig):
    """L14 reaches the owner: O18 names the earlier take with the very same inputs."""
    p, s1, s2, take1, take2 = _shoot_both(rig)
    again = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"]]))["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    rig.drain()
    takes = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1['id']}/takes").json()
    assert [(t["id"], t["same_inputs_as"]) for t in takes] == [(again, [take1]), (take1, [])]


# -- the cascade on branches and long chains (revision 3, Codex r2 finding 3) ----------------

def _three_scenes(rig, third_source: str):
    """s1 from an uploaded frame, s2 continuing s1, s3 continuing `third_source` ("s1"/"s2")."""
    p = rig.project()
    photo = _upload(rig, _png((512, 512), (200, 150, 120)))
    cast = rig.client.post(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/cast", json={
        "expected_revision": 1, "tag": "anna", "description_ru": "девушка",
        "description_en": "a young woman", "portrait_phrase": PORTRAIT,
        "assets": [{"asset_id": photo["id"], "role": "image"}]}).json()
    frame = _upload(rig, _png())
    first = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": cast["project"]["revision"], "scenes": [
            _scene_in(seed=11, source_frame={"kind": "asset", "asset_id": frame["id"]}),
            _scene_in(seed=22, source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"}),
            _scene_in(seed=33, source_frame={"kind": "prev_last_frame"})]}).json()
    s1, s2, s3 = first["scenes"]
    if third_source == "s1":
        moved = rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s3['id']}", json={
            "expected_edit_version": s3["edit_version"],
            "source_frame": {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": s1["id"]}}).json()
        s3 = moved["scene"]
    tasks = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1["id"], s2["id"], s3["id"]]))["tasks"]
    rig.drain()
    takes = [t["take_id"] for t in tasks]
    for scene_id, take_id in zip((s1["id"], s2["id"], s3["id"]), takes):
        _approve(rig, scene_id, take_id)
    return p, (s1["id"], s2["id"], s3["id"]), takes


def _versions(rig, p):
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    return {s["id"]: (s["revision"], s["edit_version"], s["state_version"],
                      s["approved_sketch_take_id"], s["review_state"]) for s in snap["scenes"]}


def test_editing_a_source_revokes_both_branches_once_each(rig):
    """Two continuations of one source: an edit of the source revokes the source and BOTH
    branches — each state_version +1 exactly once, their revision and edit_version untouched,
    one `approval_revoked` per choice."""
    p, (s1, s2, s3), (t1, t2, t3) = _three_scenes(rig, third_source="s1")
    before = _versions(rig, p)
    seq = rig.conn.execute("SELECT MAX(seq) FROM event").fetchone()[0]
    edited = rig.client.patch(f"/api/studio/scenes/{s1}", json={
        "expected_edit_version": before[s1][1], "sound_text": "Gravel."}).json()
    assert edited["cascade"] == [
        {"scene_id": s1, "tier": "sketch", "take_id": t1, "reason": "take_stale"},
        {"scene_id": s2, "tier": "sketch", "take_id": t2, "reason": "chain_mismatch"},
        {"scene_id": s3, "tier": "sketch", "take_id": t3, "reason": "chain_mismatch"}]
    after = _versions(rig, p)
    assert after == {
        s1: (before[s1][0] + 1, before[s1][1] + 1, before[s1][2] + 1, None, "needs_review"),
        s2: (before[s2][0], before[s2][1], before[s2][2] + 1, None, "needs_review"),
        s3: (before[s3][0], before[s3][1], before[s3][2] + 1, None, "needs_review")}
    events = rig.conn.execute("SELECT kind, scene_id, payload_json FROM event WHERE seq > ? "
                              "ORDER BY seq", (seq,)).fetchall()
    assert [(e["kind"], e["scene_id"], e["payload_json"]) for e in events] == [
        ("scene_updated", s1, '{"edit_version": %d, "fields": ["sound_text"], "revision": %d}'
         % (before[s1][1] + 1, before[s1][0] + 1)),
        ("approval_revoked", s1, '{"reason": "take_stale", "take_id": "%s", "tier": "sketch"}'
         % t1),
        ("approval_revoked", s2, '{"reason": "chain_mismatch", "take_id": "%s", "tier": '
         '"sketch"}' % t2),
        ("approval_revoked", s3, '{"reason": "chain_mismatch", "take_id": "%s", "tier": '
         '"sketch"}' % t3)]


def test_a_chain_of_three_in_reverse_order_is_revoked_to_the_end(rig):
    """s1 → s2 → s3, then O7 puts them as [s3, s2, s1] with explicit sources: a new choice in
    s1 must revoke s2 and, through it, s3 — three passes, not one."""
    p, (s1, s2, s3), (t1, t2, t3) = _three_scenes(rig, third_source="s2")
    snap = rig.client.get(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}").json()
    by_id = {s["id"]: s for s in snap["scenes"]}

    def scene_in(sid, source):
        s = by_id[sid]
        return {"scene_id": sid, "expected_edit_version": s["edit_version"], "mode": s["mode"],
                "action_text": s["action_text"], "performance_text": s["performance_text"],
                "sound_text": s["sound_text"], "music_text": s["music_text"],
                "requested_s": s["requested_s"], "seed": s["seed"], "source_frame": source,
                "refs": s["refs"]}
    moved = rig.client.put(f"/api/studio/projects/{p['id']}/scenes", json={
        "expected_revision": snap["project"]["revision"], "scenes": [
            scene_in(s3, {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": s2}),
            scene_in(s2, {"kind": "prev_last_frame", "scene_id": s1}),
            scene_in(s1, by_id[s1]["source_frame"] | {"scene_id": None})]}).json()
    assert moved["cascade"] == []
    t1b = _render(rig, p, _plan(rig, p, [s1]))["tasks"][0]["take_id"]
    rig.drain()
    before = _versions(rig, p)
    answer = _approve(rig, s1, t1b)
    assert answer["cascade"] == [
        {"scene_id": s2, "tier": "sketch", "take_id": t2, "reason": "chain_mismatch"},
        {"scene_id": s3, "tier": "sketch", "take_id": t3, "reason": "chain_mismatch"}]
    after = _versions(rig, p)
    assert after == {
        s3: (before[s3][0], before[s3][1], before[s3][2] + 1, None, "needs_review"),
        s2: (before[s2][0], before[s2][1], before[s2][2] + 1, None, "needs_review"),
        s1: (before[s1][0], before[s1][1], before[s1][2] + 1, t1b, "ok")}
    kinds = [r["kind"] for r in rig.conn.execute(
        "SELECT kind FROM event WHERE kind IN ('scene_approved', 'approval_revoked') "
        "ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 3")]
    assert kinds == ["approval_revoked", "approval_revoked", "scene_approved"]
```
