Create `h3_48gb/studio/approvals.py`:

```python
"""Choosing a take (spec §7 O16–O18, §6.1 И4, И11; slice S3). «Выбрать» = approve for the tier.

An approval is valid only while (И11): the take is of this scene and of its current revision, it
is done, and — for a continuation — it was rendered from the take now approved in the source
scene for the same tier. `recompute_cascade` revokes every approval that stopped being valid, in
scene order, so a revoked scene 1 revokes scene 2 in the same pass; it runs in the same
transaction as O7, O8, O16, O17."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from h3_48gb.studio import db, events
from h3_48gb.studio.errors import StudioError

TIERS = ("sketch", "final")
#: What O16 answers for each reason an approval is not valid (status, code).
REFUSALS = {"take_of_other_scene": (404, "take_not_found"), "tier_mismatch": (400, "tier_mismatch"),
            "take_stale": (409, "take_stale"), "take_not_done": (409, "take_not_done"),
            "chain_mismatch": (409, "chain_mismatch")}
MESSAGES = {"take_of_other_scene": "Это дубль другой сцены",
            "tier_mismatch": "Дубль другого вида (эскиз/финал)",
            "take_stale": "Дубль снят до правки сцены — его нельзя выбрать",
            "take_not_done": "Дубль ещё не готов",
            "chain_mismatch": "Сцена снята от другого дубля предыдущей сцены"}


def _take(conn, take_id: str | None) -> sqlite3.Row | None:
    if take_id is None:
        return None
    return conn.execute("SELECT * FROM take WHERE id = ?", (take_id,)).fetchone()


def _scene(conn, scene_id: str) -> sqlite3.Row:
    return conn.execute("SELECT * FROM scene WHERE id = ?", (scene_id,)).fetchone()


def problem(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene: sqlite3.Row, take: sqlite3.Row | None,
            tier: str) -> tuple[str, dict] | None:
    """Why `take` cannot be (or stay) the approved `tier` take of `scene` — None if it can."""
    if take is None or take["scene_id"] != scene["id"]:
        return "take_of_other_scene", {}
    if take["tier"] != tier:
        return "tier_mismatch", {"take_tier": take["tier"]}
    if take["status"] == "stale" or take["scene_revision"] != scene["revision"]:
        return "take_stale", {"take_revision": take["scene_revision"],
                              "scene_revision": scene["revision"]}
    if take["status"] != "done":
        return "take_not_done", {"status": take["status"]}
    if scene["source_frame_kind"] == "prev_last_frame":
        source = _scene(conn, scene["source_scene_id"]) if scene["source_scene_id"] else None
        approved = source[f"approved_{tier}_take_id"] if source is not None else None
        if take["upstream_take_id"] != approved:
            return "chain_mismatch", {"upstream_take_id": take["upstream_take_id"],
                                      "approved_upstream": approved}
    return None


def recompute_cascade(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    """Inside a transaction. Revoke every approval that is no longer valid (И11) — to a fixed
    point: a revocation can invalidate a continuation anywhere in the order (O7 may leave a
    continuation before its source, Codex-3), so passes repeat until one revokes nothing.
    Each pass revokes at least one approval or stops, so it ends after ≤ 2·scenes + 1 passes."""
    revoked = []
    scenes = [r["id"] for r in conn.execute(
        "SELECT id FROM scene WHERE project_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY idx",
        (project_id,))]
    changed = True
    while changed:
        changed = False
        for scene_id in scenes:
            for tier in TIERS:
                scene = _scene(conn, scene_id)
                take_id = scene[f"approved_{tier}_take_id"]
                if take_id is None:
                    continue
                found = problem(conn, scene, _take(conn, take_id), tier)
                if found is None:
                    continue
                reason = found[0]
                conn.execute(f"UPDATE scene SET approved_{tier}_take_id = NULL, "
                             "review_state = 'needs_review', state_version = state_version + 1 "
                             "WHERE id = ?", (scene_id,))
                events.emit(conn, "approval_revoked", project_id=project_id, scene_id=scene_id,
                            payload={"tier": tier, "take_id": take_id, "reason": reason})
                revoked.append({"scene_id": scene_id, "tier": tier, "take_id": take_id,
                                "reason": reason})
                changed = True
    return revoked


def _check_state(conn, scene_id: str, expected: int) -> sqlite3.Row:
    from h3_48gb.studio import ops
    scene = ops._scene_row(conn, scene_id)
    if scene["state_version"] != expected:
        raise StudioError(409, "state_conflict", "Выбор в сцене изменился в другом окне",
                          {"current": ops.scene_out(conn, scene)})
    return scene


def approve(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str, *, tier: str, take_id: str,
            expected_state_version: int) -> dict:
    """O16."""
    from h3_48gb.studio import ops
    with db.tx(conn):
        scene = _check_state(conn, scene_id, expected_state_version)
        found = problem(conn, scene, _take(conn, take_id), tier)
        if found is not None:
            reason, detail = found
            status, code = REFUSALS[reason]
            raise StudioError(status, code, MESSAGES[reason], {"take_id": take_id, **detail})
        conn.execute(f"UPDATE scene SET approved_{tier}_take_id = ?, review_state = 'ok', "
                     "state_version = state_version + 1 WHERE id = ?", (take_id, scene_id))
        events.emit(conn, "scene_approved", project_id=scene["project_id"], scene_id=scene_id,
                    payload={"tier": tier, "take_id": take_id})
        cascade = recompute_cascade(conn, scene["project_id"])
        return {"scene": ops.scene_out(conn, ops._scene_row(conn, scene_id)), "cascade": cascade}


def unapprove(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str, *, tier: str,
              expected_state_version: int) -> dict:
    """O17."""
    from h3_48gb.studio import ops
    with db.tx(conn):
        scene = _check_state(conn, scene_id, expected_state_version)
        if scene[f"approved_{tier}_take_id"] is not None:
            conn.execute(f"UPDATE scene SET approved_{tier}_take_id = NULL, review_state = 'none',"
                         " state_version = state_version + 1 WHERE id = ?", (scene_id,))
            events.emit(conn, "scene_unapproved", project_id=scene["project_id"],
                        scene_id=scene_id, payload={"tier": tier})
        cascade = recompute_cascade(conn, scene["project_id"])
        return {"scene": ops.scene_out(conn, ops._scene_row(conn, scene_id)), "cascade": cascade}


def thumb_path(home: Path, project_id: str, take_id: str, second: int) -> Path:
    return home / "projects" / project_id / "takes" / take_id / f"thumb-{second}.jpg"


def take_out(conn: sqlite3.Connection, take: sqlite3.Row) -> dict:
    scene = _scene(conn, take["scene_id"])
    task = conn.execute("SELECT status, progress_step, progress_total, wait_reason, error "
                        "FROM task WHERE id = ?", (take["task_id"],)).fetchone()
    found = problem(conn, scene, take, take["tier"])
    inputs = json.loads(take["inputs_json"]) if take["inputs_json"] else None
    head = inputs["head_drop_frames"] if inputs else None
    out = take["output_asset_id"]
    # the worker writes a thumbnail for each of 0/2/4/8 s that the clip reaches (renders.py)
    frames = [f"/api/studio/takes/{take['id']}/frames/{s}" for s in (0, 2, 4, 8)
              if out and s * 24 < inputs["frames"]]
    return {"id": take["id"], "scene_id": take["scene_id"], "scene_revision": take["scene_revision"],
            "tier": take["tier"], "template": take["template"], "status": take["status"],
            "upstream_take_id": take["upstream_take_id"], "inputs_sha256": take["inputs_sha256"],
            "keyframe_sha256": take["keyframe_sha256"], "head_drop_frames": head,
            "task_id": take["task_id"], "task_status": task["status"] if task else None,
            "progress_step": task["progress_step"] if task else None,
            "progress_total": task["progress_total"] if task else None,
            "output_asset_id": out, "last_frame_asset_id": take["last_frame_asset_id"],
            "asset_url": f"/api/studio/assets/{out}/file" if out else None, "frames_urls": frames,
            "valid_for_approval": found is None, "reason": found[0] if found else None,
            # L14: earlier takes with the very same inputs — a reshoot with the same seed
            "same_inputs_as": [r["id"] for r in conn.execute(
                "SELECT id FROM take WHERE inputs_sha256 = ? AND rowid < ? ORDER BY rowid",
                (take["inputs_sha256"], take["rowid"]))] if take["inputs_sha256"] else [],
            "created_at": take["created_at"]}


def scene_takes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, scene_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    """O18, newest first."""
    from h3_48gb.studio import ops
    ops._scene_row(conn, scene_id)
    # rowid = insertion order: takes of one O11 share their created_at to the millisecond
    rows = conn.execute("SELECT rowid, * FROM take WHERE scene_id = ? ORDER BY rowid DESC",
                        (scene_id,)).fetchall()
    return [take_out(conn, r) for r in rows]


def project_takes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, project_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    rows = conn.execute("SELECT t.rowid, t.* FROM take t JOIN scene s ON s.id = t.scene_id "
                        "WHERE s.project_id = ? AND s.deleted_at IS NULL "
                        "ORDER BY t.rowid", (project_id,)).fetchall()
    return [take_out(conn, r) for r in rows]
```

```tsx
import { act, render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { useState } from "react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { RenderServer } from "@/test/renderServer";
import { RenderStep } from "./RenderStep";
import { TakesStep } from "./TakesStep";

function Harness({ server }: { server: RenderServer }) {
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState<Snapshot>(() => server.snap());
  const reload = () => setSnap(server.snap());
  return (
    <>
      <RenderStep snap={snap} onChanged={reload} newKey={() => "k"} />
      <TakesStep snap={snap} onChanged={reload} newKey={() => "k"} />
    </>
  );
}

function serve(server: RenderServer) {
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", async (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const reply = server.handle(`${init?.method ?? "GET"} ${url}`, init?.body ? JSON.parse(init.body as string) : null);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(reply.body), { status: reply.status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
  });
}

async function shoot(names: string[]) {
  const panel = screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Какие сцены снять" });
  for (const box of within(panel).getAllByRole("checkbox")) {
    const label = box.closest("label")!.textContent!;
    if ((box as HTMLInputElement).checked !== names.includes(label)) await userEvent.click(box);
  }
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Снять эскизы" }));
  await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Подтвердить" }));
}

async function choose(sceneNumber: number, takeId: string) {
  const group = await screen.findByRole("group", { name: `Дубли сцены ${sceneNumber}` });
  await userEvent.click(within(within(group).getByLabelText(`Дубль ${takeId}`)).getByRole("button", { name: "Выбрать" }));
}

describe("steps 5–6 together (revision 2, Codex blocker 2)", () => {
  it("reshoot scene 1 alone, choose it, the conflict appears by itself, reshoot scene 2 from the chosen", async () => {
    const server = new RenderServer();
    serve(server);
    render(<Harness server={server} />);
    await shoot(["Сцена 1", "Сцена 2"]);                      // t_2 (scene 1), t_3 from t_2
    await choose(1, "t_2");
    await choose(2, "t_3");
    expect(screen.queryByText(/снята от другого дубля/)).toBeNull();
    await shoot(["Сцена 1"]);                                 // t_5, scene 1 only
    await choose(1, "t_5");                                    // revokes scene 2's choice
    const group = screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Дубли сцены 2" });
    expect(within(group).getByRole("status").firstChild!.textContent).toBe("Сцена 2 снята от другого дубля сцены 1");
    await userEvent.click(within(group).getByRole("button", { name: "Переснять сцену 2 от выбранного" }));
    await userEvent.click(await within(group).findByRole("button", { name: "Подтвердить" }));
    await choose(2, "t_7");
    expect(screen.queryByText(/снята от другого дубля/)).toBeNull();
    expect(server.bodies.filter((b) => b.key.endsWith("/plan")).map((b) => (b.body as { scene_ids: string[] }).scene_ids))
      .toEqual([["s_1", "s_2"], ["s_1"], ["s_2"]]);
    expect(server.takes.map((t) => [t.id, t.scene_id, t.upstream_take_id])).toEqual([
      ["t_2", "s_1", null], ["t_3", "s_2", "t_2"], ["t_5", "s_1", null], ["t_7", "s_2", "t_5"]]);
    expect(server.scenes.map((s) => s.approved_sketch_take_id)).toEqual(["t_5", "t_7"]);
  });

  it("offers by default only the scenes without a finished sketch", async () => {
    const server = new RenderServer();
    serve(server);
    render(<Harness server={server} />);
    await shoot(["Сцена 1"]);
    const boxes = within(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Какие сцены снять" })).getAllByRole("checkbox") as HTMLInputElement[];
    expect(boxes.map((b) => b.checked)).toEqual([false, true]);
    await act(async () => {});
  });
});
```

import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { stubFetch } from "@/test/fetchStub";
import { scene, snapshot } from "@/test/snapshot";
import { renderPlan, take, task } from "@/test/task";
import { RenderStep } from "./RenderStep";
import { TakesStep, chainConflict } from "./TakesStep";

const s1 = scene({ id: "s_1", mode: "from_frame", source_frame: { kind: "asset", scene_id: null, asset_id: "a_k" } });
const s2 = scene({ id: "s_2", idx: 1, mode: "from_frame", state_version: 3, source_frame: { kind: "prev_last_frame", scene_id: "s_1", asset_id: null } });

describe("RenderStep", () => {
  it("plans both scenes, shows the wait on scene 1 and confirms with a key", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan": { body: renderPlan() },
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/render": { status: 201, body: { tasks: [] } },
    });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    render(<RenderStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [s1, s2] })} onChanged={onChanged} newKey={() => "key-r"} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Снять эскизы" }));
    const region = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "План эскизов" });
    expect(within(region).getByLabelText("План сцены 1").textContent).toBe("Сцена 1 · От кадра · ≈ 4 мин · готова");
    expect(within(region).getByLabelText("План сцены 2").textContent).toBe("Сцена 2 · От кадра · ≈ 4 мин · после сцены 1");
    expect(within(region).getByLabelText("План").textContent).toBe("Время ≈ 8 мин");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Подтвердить" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(onChanged).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(calls.map((c) => [c.key, c.body])).toEqual([
      ["POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan", { kind: "render", tier: "sketch", scene_ids: ["s_1", "s_2"] }],
      ["POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/render", { plan_id: "pl_r" }],
    ]);
    expect(calls[1].headers).toEqual({ "Content-Type": "application/json", "Idempotency-Key": "key-r" });
  });

  it("an invalid plan names the reasons and cannot be confirmed", async () => {
    stubFetch({ "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan": { body: renderPlan({
      invalid: ["s_1", "s_2"],
      items: [
        { kind: "render", scene_id: "s_1", template: "h3-fl2va", estimate_s: 236.7, status: "invalid", reasons: ["L12: у сцены нет кадра — сделайте кадр на шаге 4 или выберите источник"], depends_on_scene: null },
        { kind: "render", scene_id: "s_2", template: "h3-fl2va", estimate_s: 236.7, status: "invalid", reasons: ["сцена 1 в этом плане с ошибкой"], depends_on_scene: "s_1" },
      ] }) } });
    render(<RenderStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [s1, s2] })} onChanged={() => {}} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Снять эскизы" }));
    const alerts = await screen.findAllByRole("alert");
    expect(alerts.map((a) => a.textContent)).toEqual([
      "L12: у сцены нет кадра — сделайте кадр на шаге 4 или выберите источник", "сцена 1 в этом плане с ошибкой"]);
    expect((screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Подтвердить" }) as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it("shows each scene's sketch task: the continuation waits for the frame", () => {
    stubFetch({});
    const snap = snapshot({
      scenes: [s1, s2],
      takes: [take({ id: "t_1", scene_id: "s_1", task_id: "k_1", status: "running" }),
              take({ id: "t_2", scene_id: "s_2", task_id: "k_2", status: "pending_input" })],
      tasks: [task({ id: "k_1", kind: "render", status: "running", progress_step: 7, progress_total: 20 }),
              task({ id: "k_2", kind: "render", status: "blocked" })],
    });
    render(<RenderStep snap={snap} onChanged={() => {}} />);
    expect(within(screen.getByLabelText("Эскиз сцены 1")).getByRole("status").textContent).toBe("Шаг 7 из 20");
    expect(within(screen.getByLabelText("Эскиз сцены 2")).getByRole("status").textContent).toBe("Ждёт кадр предыдущей сцены");
  });
});

describe("TakesStep", () => {
  it("chooses a take with the scene's state_version", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({ "POST /api/studio/scenes/s_2/approve": { body: { scene: s2, cascade: [] } } });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    const snap = snapshot({ scenes: [s1, s2], takes: [take({ id: "t_2", scene_id: "s_2" })] });
    render(<TakesStep snap={snap} onChanged={onChanged} />);
    const group = screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Дубли сцены 2" });
    expect((within(group).getByAltText("Кадр 2 с") as HTMLImageElement).getAttribute("src")).toBe("/api/studio/takes/t_1/frames/2");
    await userEvent.click(within(group).getByRole("button", { name: "Выбрать" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(onChanged).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(calls.map((c) => [c.key, c.body])).toEqual([
      ["POST /api/studio/scenes/s_2/approve", { tier: "sketch", take_id: "t_2", expected_state_version: 3 }]]);
  });

  it("a continuation shot from another take says so and reshoots from the chosen one", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch({
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan": { body: renderPlan({ items: [renderPlan().items[0]], blocked: [] }) },
      "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/render": { status: 201, body: { tasks: [] } },
    });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    const chosen = { ...s1, approved_sketch_take_id: "t_1b" };
    const snap = snapshot({ scenes: [chosen, s2], takes: [
      take({ id: "t_1b", scene_id: "s_1" }),
      take({ id: "t_2", scene_id: "s_2", upstream_take_id: "t_1", valid_for_approval: false, reason: "chain_mismatch" })] });
    render(<TakesStep snap={snap} onChanged={onChanged} newKey={() => "key-2"} />);
    const group = screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Дубли сцены 2" });
    expect(within(group).getByRole("status").firstChild!.textContent).toBe("Сцена 2 снята от другого дубля сцены 1");
    expect(within(group).getByText("Снят от другого дубля сцены 1")).toBeTruthy();
    expect((within(group).getByRole("button", { name: "Выбрать" }) as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
    await userEvent.click(within(group).getByRole("button", { name: "Переснять сцену 2 от выбранного" }));
    await userEvent.click(await within(group).findByRole("button", { name: "Подтвердить" }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(onChanged).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(calls.map((c) => [c.key, c.body])).toEqual([
      ["POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan", { kind: "render", tier: "sketch", scene_ids: ["s_2"] }],
      ["POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/render", { plan_id: "pl_r" }]]);
    expect(calls[1].headers!["Idempotency-Key"]).toBe("key-2");
  });

  it("no conflict once a take of the continuation comes from the chosen one", () => {
    const chosen = { ...s1, approved_sketch_take_id: "t_1b" };
    const snap = snapshot({ scenes: [chosen, s2], takes: [
      take({ id: "t_2", scene_id: "s_2", upstream_take_id: "t_1" }),
      take({ id: "t_2b", scene_id: "s_2", upstream_take_id: "t_1b", status: "running" })] });
    expect(chainConflict(s2, snap)).toBe(null);
    expect(chainConflict(s2, { ...snap, takes: [snap.takes[0]] })).toBe(1);
    expect(chainConflict(s1, snap)).toBe(null);
  });

  it("L14: a take with the same inputs as an earlier one says so", () => {
    stubFetch({});
    render(<TakesStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [s1, s2], takes: [take({ id: "t_2", scene_id: "s_2", same_inputs_as: ["t_1"] })] })} onChanged={() => {}} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Те же входы и сид, что у t_1")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a refused choice shows the server's words", async () => {
    stubFetch({ "POST /api/studio/scenes/s_2/approve": { status: 409, body: { error: {
      code: "take_stale", message: "Дубль снят до правки сцены — его нельзя выбрать", detail: {} } } } });
    const onChanged = vi.fn();
    render(<TakesStep snap={snapshot({ scenes: [s1, s2], takes: [take({ id: "t_2", scene_id: "s_2" })] })} onChanged={onChanged} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Выбрать" }));
    expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("Дубль снят до правки сцены — его нельзя выбрать");
    expect(onChanged).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/test/renderServer.ts`:

```ts
import type { Plan, Scene, Snapshot, Take } from "@/api/types";
import { scene, snapshot } from "./snapshot";
import { take, task } from "./task";

/** A studio server in miniature for steps 5–6 (revision 2, Codex blocker 2): O10 render plans
 *  exactly the scenes it is given; O11 «renders» them at once — a continuation in the same plan
 *  starts from the plan's new take of its source, otherwise from the source's chosen sketch
 *  (plans.plan_render); O16 chooses and revokes, like approvals.recompute_cascade, every choice
 *  whose upstream is no longer the source's chosen take. Nothing here hands out a conflict: it
 *  appears only from these rules. */
export class RenderServer {
  scenes: Scene[];
  takes: Take[] = [];
  plans = new Map<string, string[]>();
  bodies: { key: string; body: unknown }[] = [];
  private n = 0;

  constructor() {
    this.scenes = [
      scene({ id: "s_1", idx: 0, mode: "from_frame", source_frame: { kind: "asset", scene_id: null, asset_id: "a_k" } }),
      scene({ id: "s_2", idx: 1, mode: "from_frame", source_frame: { kind: "prev_last_frame", scene_id: "s_1", asset_id: null } }),
    ];
  }

  snap(): Snapshot {
    const takes = this.takes.map((t) => ({ ...t, ...this.validity(t) }));
    return snapshot({ scenes: this.scenes.map((s) => ({ ...s })), takes,
                      tasks: takes.map((t) => task({ id: t.task_id, kind: "render", status: "succeeded" })) });
  }

  private source(s: Scene): Scene | undefined {
    return s.source_frame.kind === "prev_last_frame" ? this.scenes.find((x) => x.id === s.source_frame.scene_id) : undefined;
  }

  private validity(t: Take): Pick<Take, "valid_for_approval" | "reason"> {
    const s = this.scenes.find((x) => x.id === t.scene_id)!;
    const src = this.source(s);
    if (src && t.upstream_take_id !== src.approved_sketch_take_id) return { valid_for_approval: false, reason: "chain_mismatch" };
    return { valid_for_approval: true, reason: null };
  }

  handle(key: string, body: any): { status: number; body: unknown } {
    this.bodies.push({ key, body });
    if (key === "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/plan") {
      const id = `pl_${++this.n}`;
      this.plans.set(id, body.scene_ids);
      const plan: Plan = { plan_id: id, expires_at: "x", kind: "render", total_s: 0, checkpoint_switches: [], lint: {},
        blocked: [], invalid: [], items: body.scene_ids.map((sid: string) => ({ kind: "render", scene_id: sid, template: "h3-fl2va",
          estimate_s: 240, status: "ready", reasons: [], depends_on_scene: null })) };
      return { status: 200, body: plan };
    }
    if (key === "POST /api/studio/projects/p_1/render") {
      const made = new Map<string, string>();
      for (const sid of this.plans.get(body.plan_id)!) {
        const s = this.scenes.find((x) => x.id === sid)!;
        const src = this.source(s);
        const upstream = src ? made.get(src.id) ?? src.approved_sketch_take_id : null;
        const id = `t_${++this.n}`;
        made.set(sid, id);
        this.takes.push(take({ id, scene_id: sid, task_id: `k_${id}`, upstream_take_id: upstream, frames_urls: [] }));
      }
      return { status: 201, body: { tasks: [] } };
    }
    const approve = key.match(/^POST \/api\/studio\/scenes\/(s_\d)\/approve$/);
    if (approve) {
      const s = this.scenes.find((x) => x.id === approve[1])!;
      if (body.expected_state_version !== s.state_version) return { status: 409, body: { error: { code: "state_conflict", message: "conflict", detail: {} } } };
      const t = this.takes.find((x) => x.id === body.take_id)!;
      if (!this.validity(t).valid_for_approval) return { status: 409, body: { error: { code: "chain_mismatch", message: "Сцена снята от другого дубля предыдущей сцены", detail: {} } } };
      s.approved_sketch_take_id = t.id;
      s.state_version += 1;
      for (const other of this.scenes) {                      // the cascade, to a fixed point
        if (other.approved_sketch_take_id && !this.validity(this.takes.find((x) => x.id === other.approved_sketch_take_id)!).valid_for_approval) {
          other.approved_sketch_take_id = null;
          other.review_state = "needs_review";
          other.state_version += 1;
        }
      }
      return { status: 200, body: { scene: s, cascade: [] } };
    }
    throw new Error(`unexpected ${key}`);
  }
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/components/PlanItems.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Plan, Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { minutes } from "@/components/PlanSummary";

const MODE = { "h3-fl2va": "От кадра", "h3-ref2va": "Референсы" } as Record<string, string>;

/** The scenes of a render plan (spec §4.4 step 5): template, time, and what blocks each one. */
export function PlanItems({ plan, snap }: { plan: Plan; snap: Snapshot }) {
  const number = (id: string | null) => (snap.scenes.findIndex((s) => s.id === id) + 1);
  return (
    <ol className="grid gap-1.5 text-[13px]">
      {plan.items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.scene_id} aria-label={`План сцены ${number(item.scene_id)}`}>
          <span>{`Сцена ${number(item.scene_id)} · ${MODE[item.template] ?? item.template} · ${minutes(item.estimate_s)} · `}</span>
          {item.status === "ready" && <span>готова</span>}
          {item.status === "blocked" && <span>{`после сцены ${number(item.depends_on_scene as string)}`}</span>}
          {item.status === "invalid" && (
            <ul role="alert" className="mt-1 grid gap-0.5 text-danger">
              {item.reasons.map((r, i) => <li key={i}>{r}</li>)}
            </ul>
          )}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/RenderStep.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Clapperboard } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Plan, Snapshot } from "@/api/types";
import { PlanItems } from "@/components/PlanItems";
import { PlanSummary } from "@/components/PlanSummary";
import { TaskLine } from "@/components/TaskLine";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useIsPhone, useMobileAction } from "@/mobileAction";

/** Scenes offered by default: those without a finished sketch (revision 2, Codex blocker 2). */
export function unshot(snap: Snapshot): string[] {
  return snap.scenes.filter((s) => !snap.takes.some((t) => t.scene_id === s.id && t.tier === "sketch" && t.status === "done"))
    .map((s) => s.id);
}

/** Step 5 «Эскизы» (spec §4.4): the owner picks the scenes — one, several, or by default all
 *  without a finished sketch — then a plan (O10 render, sketch) with time, model switches and
 *  blocks → «Подтвердить» (O11, fresh Idempotency-Key) → progress per scene; a continuation
 *  waits for the previous scene's frame («Ждёт кадр предыдущей сцены»). */
export function RenderStep({ snap, onChanged, newKey = () => crypto.randomUUID() }: {
  snap: Snapshot;
  onChanged: () => void;
  newKey?: () => string;
}) {
  const pid = snap.project.id;
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState<Plan | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<Set<string> | null>(null);
  const phone = useIsPhone();
  const chosen = picked ?? new Set(unshot(snap));
  const toggle = (id: string) => {
    const next = new Set(chosen);
    if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id); else next.add(id);
    setPicked(next);
  };

  async function run(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил");
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === "plan_stale") setPlan(null);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  const makePlan = () => run(async () => {
    setPlan(await api<Plan>("POST", `/projects/${pid}/plan`, {
      kind: "render", tier: "sketch", scene_ids: snap.scenes.filter((s) => chosen.has(s.id)).map((s) => s.id) }));
  });
  const confirm = () => run(async () => {
    await api("POST", `/projects/${pid}/render`, { plan_id: plan!.plan_id }, { "Idempotency-Key": newKey() });
    setPlan(null);
    setPicked(null);
    onChanged();
  });
  const runnable = plan !== null && plan.invalid.length === 0;
  useMobileAction("render", plan && runnable ? { label: "Подтвердить", onClick: confirm, disabled: busy, priority: 1 } : null);

  if (snap.scenes.length === 0) return null;
  const tasks = new Map(snap.tasks.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="render-h" className="mt-10">
      <div className="eyebrow">Шаг 5</div>
      <h2 id="render-h" className="h-display mt-1 text-[24px]">Эскизы</h2>
      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 rounded-lg border border-line p-5">
        {!plan && (
          <fieldset className="grid gap-2">
            <legend className="text-[13px] text-ink2">Какие сцены снять</legend>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              {snap.scenes.map((scene, i) => (
                <label key={scene.id} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px]">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={chosen.has(scene.id)} onChange={() => toggle(scene.id)} />
                  {`Сцена ${i + 1}`}
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
            <Button size="lg" onClick={makePlan} disabled={busy || chosen.size === 0} className="justify-self-start">
              <Clapperboard />
              Снять эскизы
            </Button>
          </fieldset>
        )}
        {plan && (
          <div role="region" aria-label="План эскизов" className="grid gap-2 rounded-md border border-cobalt-line bg-cobalt-soft p-4">
            <PlanItems plan={plan} snap={snap} />
            <PlanSummary plan={plan} />
            {!phone && (
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <Button size="lg" onClick={confirm} disabled={busy || !runnable}>Подтвердить</Button>
                <Button size="lg" variant="outline" onClick={() => setPlan(null)} disabled={busy}>Отмена</Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        <ol className="grid gap-2">
          {snap.scenes.map((scene, i) => {
            const take = [...snap.takes].reverse().find((t) => t.scene_id === scene.id && t.tier === "sketch");
            const task = take ? tasks.get(take.task_id) : undefined;
            return (
              <li key={scene.id} aria-label={`Эскиз сцены ${i + 1}`} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[13px]">
                <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink3">{`Сцена ${i + 1}`}</span>
                {task ? <TaskLine task={task} onChanged={onChanged} /> : <span className="text-ink3">не снималась</span>}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ol>
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-[13px] text-danger">{error}</p>}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

Create `h3_48gb/studio/web/src/screens/TakesStep.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { api, ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import type { Plan, Scene, Snapshot, Take } from "@/api/types";
import { PlanSummary } from "@/components/PlanSummary";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

const SHOWN = new Set(["done", "stale"]);

/** Why a take cannot be chosen, in words (И4, И11). */
export function takeProblem(take: Take, snap: Snapshot): string | null {
  if (take.reason === "take_stale") return "Снят до правки сцены";
  if (take.reason === "chain_mismatch") {
    const scene = snap.scenes.find((s) => s.id === take.scene_id)!;
    const source = snap.scenes.findIndex((s) => s.id === scene.source_frame.scene_id) + 1;
    return `Снят от другого дубля сцены ${source}`;
  }
  return take.reason === null ? null : "Нельзя выбрать";
}

/** The chain conflict of a continuation (§4.4 step 6): its source has a chosen sketch and no
 *  take of this scene is (being) shot from it. */
export function chainConflict(scene: Scene, snap: Snapshot): number | null {
  if (scene.source_frame.kind !== "prev_last_frame") return null;
  const source = snap.scenes.find((s) => s.id === scene.source_frame.scene_id);
  if (!source?.approved_sketch_take_id) return null;
  const mine = snap.takes.filter((t) => t.scene_id === scene.id && t.tier === "sketch" && !["failed", "cancelled"].includes(t.status));
  if (mine.length === 0 || mine.some((t) => t.upstream_take_id === source.approved_sketch_take_id)) return null;
  return snap.scenes.indexOf(source) + 1;
}

/** Step 6 «Выбор» (spec §4.4): sketches of each scene with the video and frames at 0/2/4/8 s,
 *  «Выбрать» (O16 with the scene's state_version); a continuation shot from another take of the
 *  previous scene says so and offers «Переснять сцену N от выбранного» (O10 + O11). */
export function TakesStep({ snap, onChanged, newKey = () => crypto.randomUUID() }: {
  snap: Snapshot;
  onChanged: () => void;
  newKey?: () => string;
}) {
  const pid = snap.project.id;
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState<{ scene: string; plan: Plan } | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  async function run(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Сервер не ответил");
      onChanged();
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  const choose = (scene: Scene, take: Take) => run(async () => {
    await api("POST", `/scenes/${scene.id}/approve`, { tier: "sketch", take_id: take.id, expected_state_version: scene.state_version });
    onChanged();
  });
  const reshootPlan = (scene: Scene) => run(async () => {
    setPlan({ scene: scene.id, plan: await api<Plan>("POST", `/projects/${pid}/plan`, { kind: "render", tier: "sketch", scene_ids: [scene.id] }) });
  });
  const reshoot = () => run(async () => {
    await api("POST", `/projects/${pid}/render`, { plan_id: plan!.plan.plan_id }, { "Idempotency-Key": newKey() });
    setPlan(null);
    onChanged();
  });

  const scenes = snap.scenes.filter((s) => snap.takes.some((t) => t.scene_id === s.id && t.tier === "sketch" && SHOWN.has(t.status)));
  if (scenes.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="takes-h" className="mt-10">
      <div className="eyebrow">Шаг 6</div>
      <h2 id="takes-h" className="h-display mt-1 text-[24px]">Выбор</h2>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-[13px] text-danger">{error}</p>}
      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-6">
        {scenes.map((scene) => {
          const n = snap.scenes.indexOf(scene) + 1;
          const takes = snap.takes.filter((t) => t.scene_id === scene.id && t.tier === "sketch" && SHOWN.has(t.status)).reverse();
          const conflict = chainConflict(scene, snap);
          return (
            <div key={scene.id} role="group" aria-label={`Дубли сцены ${n}`} className="grid gap-3">
              <div className="eyebrow">{`Сцена ${n}`}</div>
              {conflict !== null && (
                <div role="status" className="grid gap-2 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface2 p-3 text-[13px]">
                  <span>{`Сцена ${n} снята от другого дубля сцены ${conflict}`}</span>
                  {plan?.scene === scene.id ? (
                    <div className="grid gap-2">
                      <PlanSummary plan={plan.plan} />
                      <Button size="lg" onClick={reshoot} disabled={busy || plan.plan.invalid.length > 0} className="justify-self-start">Подтвердить</Button>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => reshootPlan(scene)} disabled={busy} className="justify-self-start">{`Переснять сцену ${n} от выбранного`}</Button>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
              <ul className="flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
                {takes.map((take) => {
                  const chosen = scene.approved_sketch_take_id === take.id;
                  const problem = takeProblem(take, snap);
                  return (
                    <li key={take.id} aria-label={`Дубль ${take.id}`} className="grid w-[300px] shrink-0 gap-2 rounded-md border border-line p-2">
                      {take.asset_url && <video controls preload="metadata" src={take.asset_url} className="aspect-video w-full rounded-sm bg-black" />}
                      <div className="flex gap-1">
                        {take.frames_urls.map((url, i) => <img key={url} src={url} alt={`Кадр ${[0, 2, 4, 8][i]} с`} className="w-1/4 rounded-sm" />)}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        {chosen && <Badge>Выбран</Badge>}
                        {problem && <Badge variant="outline">{problem}</Badge>}
                        {take.same_inputs_as.length > 0 && (
                          <Badge variant="outline">{`Те же входы и сид, что у ${take.same_inputs_as.join(", ")}`}</Badge>
                        )}
                        {!chosen && (
                          <Button size="lg" onClick={() => choose(scene, take)} disabled={busy || !take.valid_for_approval}>Выбрать</Button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

## Что осталось непроверенным при написании плана

- Живой ComfyUI ни разу не вызывался кодом адаптера: формат `outputs` узла `SaveVideo` в `/history` v0.42 взят из `gpu_day21_lib.out_files` (ключи `images`/`videos`/`gifs` — адаптер берёт любой список словарей с `filename`), WebSocket-прогресс по `prompt_id` — из GPU-DAY п.7. Это проверяют живые контракты задач 10 и 15.
- `restarted_since` против настоящего диспетчера: поле `own["comfy-studio"].started_at` проверено тестом диспетчера на `FakeHost`, на хосте — нет (GPU-DAY-20261021 п.5 оговорил это же).
- Выкладка: образ с ffmpeg и `websockets` не собирался; путь диспетчера требует решения (задача 10, шаг 2 — override юнита на клон студии); compose-файл проверен тестом текста, а `docker compose config` и запуск образа — шаг координатора в задаче 10 до `up -d`; скрипт отката проверен на стабах, на хосте — нет.
- `assemble._extract_valid_last_frame` на настоящем H3-ролике в студии не гонялся (на имитации — да; на роликах старой панели он работает с 08.2026).
- Оценки времени до истории — по формуле карточки (R-S3-6); первые проходы владельца заменят их историей.
- Мутация «без `proxy=None`» видна только в окружении с прокси; мутация «`find` вернул тот же ref» ловится зависанием, а не assertion (записано в задачах 4 и 8).
- Плоское хранение `asset.path` абсолютным путём контейнера: студия и воркер монтируют один и тот же путь (`/home/alex/Outputs/studio`), иначе пути не сойдутся — проверено только тем, что compose монтирует одинаково.

## Числа

Мастер: Vitest 66 (было 49), `npm run build`, Playwright 2. Число до задач — в отчёт задачи 1 по команде Global Constraints.
